### [1] [Ordinal Gates, Cardinal Bets: Matching LLM Confidence to the Financial Decision Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00187)
*Rayansh Singh,Sara Rezaeimanesh*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文指出LLM置信度分数并非可独立部署的对象，其决策价值取决于下游消费者（操作器和暴露控制器）。在FactSet新闻和Nasdaq-100股票数据上（2021年拟合、2022-2023年样本外测试九款开源LLM），证明置信度映射与尺度（scale）不可独立移植：单独跨用会导致收益下降和风险目标误差。匹配的映射-尺度组合可将集成CER提升9.2个百分点/年，但该增益在自适应波动率控制器下大幅缩减至1.6个百分点/年，表明置信度变换须与下游控制器联合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将LLM置信度分数作为独立校准对象来评估，忽视了其实际用法由下游操作器和暴露控制器决定。单调重校准无法改变基于覆盖匹配的排序门控，而仓位控制（position sizing）消耗的是分数幅度，尺度是针对旧分布拟合的，更换置信度映射可能导致拟合尺度失效。因此需要论证置信度变换与其消费者之间的耦合关系。

Method: 采用FactSet新闻数据覆盖Nasdaq-100股票，于2021年拟合置信度映射和交易尺度，对九款开源LLM在2022-2023年进行样本外评估。实验设计包括：交叉应用原始映射和正确性映射并搭配独立拟合的尺度，检验两个组件是否可单独移植；在冻结尺度控制下比较匹配与不匹配的映射-尺度组合；在自适应波动率控制器和年度滚动（walk-forward）设定下重复验证。

Result: 跨用原始/正确性映射与独立拟合尺度时，尺度转移在8/9个模型中降低确定等价收益（CER）并产生较大风险目标误差。映射与拟合尺度匹配可将集成CER提升9.2个百分点/年（p<0.001，冻结尺度控制下），排除最大贡献模型后仍为+5.5pp/年。但在自适应波动率控制器下增量效应仅+1.6pp/年，存在显著的控制器交互作用；年度滚动效应更小，但映射-尺度交互在所有折次中均为正。

Conclusion: 置信度变换（映射）与交易尺度、以及下游控制器的决策逻辑必须作为一个整体进行联合评估与部署。任何仅单独评估或移植置信度校准组件的做法都可能高估或低估其实际价值。置信度系统的科学评估应包含其所消费的下游控制器情境。

Abstract: LLM confidence scores are not independently deployable objects: their decision value depends on the downstream operator and exposure controller that consume them. Monotone recalibration cannot change a coverage-matched rank-based gate, whereas position sizing consumes score magnitude, so changing a confidence map can invalidate a scale fitted to the previous score distribution. We test this on FactSet news for Nasdaq-100 equities, fitting maps and scales on 2021 and evaluating nine open-weight LLMs out-of-sample on 2022--2023. Cross-applying raw and correctness maps with independently fitted scales shows that the two components are not portable alone: scale transfer reduces certainty-equivalent return (CER) in $8/9$ models and produces large risk-target errors. Matching each map with its fitted scale improves ensemble CER by $9.2$ percentage points per year under frozen-scale control ($p<0.001$), and the effect remains significant when the single largest-contributing model is excluded ($+5.5$pp/yr), so it is not driven by one case. Under an identical adaptive-volatility controller, however, the incremental effect falls to $+1.6$pp/yr, with a significant controller interaction. Annual walk-forward effects are smaller, although map--scale interaction remains positive in every fold. Confidence transformations should therefore be evaluated jointly with the downstream controllers that consume them.

</details>

### [2] [REAL-Q: E2E LLM Quantization via Dynamic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00049)
*Qian Zhang,Yaoming Li,Zhewen Tan,Yanshu Wang,Heng Lu,Kun Su,Zongwei Lv,Wenhan Yu,Yongge Ma,Yinjun Han,Ruikuang Liu,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REAL-Q是一种新的后训练量化（PTQ）方法，通过端到端对齐的损失代理函数和细粒度的动态分块梯度下降来解决现有PTQ方法中存在的"信息错配"问题，在LLaMA-3.1和Qwen3模型上以W4A16量化配置实现了与最先进全局引导方法相比最多约49%的端到端KL散度降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA的PTQ方法为保持解析可处理性，用单一闭式二阶求解器对每层进行量化，并严重近似全局损失（丢弃跨通道耦合、将输出行分组池化），且冻结整个层的Hessian矩阵，无法在损失面逐列变化时刷新——这种"信息错配"现象限制了量化精度。

Method: REAL-Q提出新PTQ范式：不使用解析可处理的近似目标，而是直接以端到端对齐的全局损失代理函数为目标，并通过每列块（128列）后应用的细粒度动态分块梯度下降进行优化；同时引入滑动窗口机制实现平滑的跨层过渡，从而缓解误差在网络中的传播。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1（8B和70B）和Qwen3（0.6B-32B）模型的W4A16量化实验中，REAL-Q相对最先进的全局引导方法将端到端KL散度最多降低了约49%。

Conclusion: REAL-Q通过打破"为了解析可处理性而稀释目标"的传统折衷，结合端到端对齐的损失代理和细粒度动态优化，有效解决了PTQ中的信息错配与误差传播问题，在多种规模LLM上取得了显著的量化质量提升。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for deploying large language models (LLMs) under strict resource constraints. State-of-the-art PTQ methods quantize each layer with a single closed-form second-order solver: to remain analytically tractable, they heavily approximate the global loss (dropping cross-channel coupling, pooling output rows into groups), and they then freeze the resulting Hessian across the entire layer, with no way to refresh it as the loss landscape shifts column by column--a phenomenon we call information misalignment. We propose REAL-Q (Real-time E2E-loss Aligned LLM Quantization), a novel PTQ paradigm that breaks this compromise: instead of diluting the objective for the sake of analytic tractability, REAL-Q targets an end-to-end-aligned surrogate of the global loss and refines it via fine-grained, dynamic Block-wise Gradient Descent applied after every column block (128 columns). By coupling this fine-grained correction with a sliding window mechanism for smooth cross-layer transitions, REAL-Q effectively mitigates error propagation across the network. On LLaMA-3.1 (8B and 70B) and Qwen3 (0.6B-32B) at W4A16, REAL-Q reduces end-to-end KL divergence by up to ~49% relative to state-of-the-art globally-guided methods.

</details>


### [3] [DISTAL: Distillation and Self-Supervised Pretraining for Structure-Agnostic Materials Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00059)
*Weiran Wang,Xintong Huo,Yueying Wang,Yusi Fan,Wenyan Wang,Xin Feng,Ruihao Xin,Lan Huang,Kewei Li,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DISTAL框架，一种无需晶体结构的材料性质预测方法，结合自监督成分预训练与结构感知知识蒸馏。在39个基准任务中，多模态配置在37个任务上优于参考基准，实现成分预测的最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料性质预测在低数据场景下仍具挑战，且现有最强模型依赖晶体结构输入，限制了在早期筛选阶段（结构信息不足）的应用。需要一种结构无关但能利用结构知识的预测方法。

Method: DISTAL采用双先验框架：(1) 从大规模虚拟成分空间通过145个成分描述符进行自监督成分预训练，学习可迁移的成分表示；(2) 从预训练的ALIGNN教师模型中蒸馏结构知识到成分条件学生模型。训练阶段利用结构先验，推理阶段不需要结构输入，最终统一融合三种信号（成分描述符、预训练潜在特征、蒸馏结构特征）进行预测。

Result: 在39个基准任务中，融合三种信号的最优多模态配置在37个任务上优于参考基准，DISTAL在所有评估的特征组合中取得最强整体性能。

Conclusion: 成分预训练与结构蒸馏提供了互补的先验信息，为小数据材料信息学中稳健的成分-only预测提供了实用路径，说明无需结构输入也能达到强预测性能。

Abstract: Materials property prediction remains difficult in low-data settings, where many target properties are supported by only a limited number of labeled samples. Models with the strongest predictive accuracy often depend on crystal structures, which restricts their use in early-stage screening when structural information is limited or unavailable. To address this challenge, we propose DISTAL, a dual-prior framework for structure-agnostic materials property prediction that combines self-supervised compositional pretraining with structure-aware knowledge distillation. DISTAL first learns transferable compositional representations from a large virtual composition space using 145 composition-derived descriptors. It then distills structural knowledge from a pretrained ALIGNN teacher into a composition-conditioned student. This setting allows structural priors to be used during training without requiring structural inputs at inference. By integrating explicit compositional descriptors, pretrained latent features, and distilled structural features within a unified prediction pipeline, DISTAL captures complementary signals that are difficult to recover from any single representation alone. Across 39 benchmark tasks, the best-performing multimodal configuration combines all three signals, and improves over the reference benchmark on 37 tasks. DISTAL achieves the strongest overall performance among all evaluated feature combinations. These results indicate that compositional pretraining and structural distillation provide complementary priors and offer a practical route to robust composition-only prediction in small-data materials informatics. The source code and the pre-trained models are anonymously available at: https://osf.io/eq96d/overview?view_only=451617f42f7849e08750bd1852b48980 and will be released at the official link after acceptance.

</details>


### [4] [AdaptNTK: Adaptive Uncertainty Quantification and Active Learning for Neural Network Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00488)
*Prajwal Ananth,Shuwen Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaptNTK，一种基于经验神经正切核（NTK）特征空间中正则化马氏距离来度量不确定性并应用于主动学习的单模型框架。它在rMD17和Transition-1X数据集上实现了最优的力误差性能，同时相比系综方法提供2.6倍的速度提升，能够在不重训练的情况下递归更新不确定性以降低批次采集中的冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势的可靠性依赖主动学习来扩展训练集，但现有不确定性量化方法在计算成本与可靠性之间存在权衡，且在批次采集过程中无法考虑样本冗余性。需要一个兼顾效率、可靠性并支持批次内冗余消除的不确定性估计框架。

Method: AdaptNTK采用单模型框架，在经验神经正切核特征空间中通过正则化马氏距离度量不确定性。由于采集过程中NTK特征固定，不确定性仅依赖于已采集配置而非其参考标签，从而可以在每次选择后递归更新不确定性（无需重新训练），以此降低一个采集批次内的冗余。

Result: 在留出的rMD17数据上，AdaptNTK达到与力误差最高的平均相关性（Spearman 0.68，Pearson 0.71），并在误差保持方面匹配三成员系综。主动学习实验中，AdaptNTK在rMD17和Transition-1X上取得最低力误差，尤其对Transition-1X中的过渡态构型表现突出。每个Transition-1X循环相对系综有2.6倍加速。

Conclusion: AdaptNTK为主动学习提供了高效的单模型不确定性估计方案，支持顺序更新以消除批次内冗余，在精度与速度上均优于系综方法，展现了在MLIP主动学习中的实用价值。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials bridge the gap between quantum chemical precision and classical computational speed, enabling molecular dynamics simulations with first-principles accuracy. Their reliability is often improved through active learning, which iteratively expands the training set by identifying uncertain, out-of-distribution configurations. Existing uncertainty-quantification methods often involve a trade-off between computational cost and reliability, and generally cannot account for redundancy as an acquisition batch is assembled. Here, we introduce AdaptNTK, a single-model framework that measures uncertainty as a regularized Mahalanobis distance in empirical neural tangent kernel (NTK) feature space. With the NTK features fixed during acquisition, the uncertainty depends on the acquired configurations but not their reference labels. This allows the uncertainty to be updated recursively after each selection without retraining, reducing redundancy within an acquisition batch. On held-out rMD17 data, AdaptNTK achieves the highest mean correlations with force errors (Spearman 0.68, Pearson 0.71) and matches a three-member ensemble in error retention. In active learning experiments, AdaptNTK achieves the lowest force errors across rMD17 and Transition-1X, with particularly strong performance on transition-state configurations in Transition-1X. AdaptNTK provides a 2.6-fold speedup per Transition-1X cycle relative to the ensemble, providing efficient single-model uncertainty estimation with sequential updates for data-efficient active learning.

</details>


### [5] [Why Multi-Layer Message Passing Works: Completeness Theory for Graph Neural Network Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00528)
*Pingbing Ming,Han Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了基于3-体消息传递的超图神经网络（Hypergraph Neural Network）是势能面的通用逼近器。核心贡献是提出多层完备性理论，证明在稀疏截断图上进行L层消息传递，可以达到与访问完整L跳邻域相同的表示能力，前提是构型满足通用性、重叠性和连通性条件。这是首次为"多层消息传递配合小于物理相互作用范围的逐层截断"这一实际惯例提供严格的理论依据。作为直接推论，DPA3和CHGNet架构也具有通用逼近性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中的图神经网络机器学习原子间势几乎都采用多层消息传递，且每层的截断半径小于真实的物理相互作用范围。然而，此前缺乏严格的理论证明来说明这种"多层截断补偿"的做法为何能达到完整表示能力。本文动机就是填补这一理论空白，从通用逼近角度证明超图神经网络在稀疏截断图设置下仍是势能面的通用逼近器。

Method: 作者发展了多层完备性理论（multi-layer completeness theory），通过理论分析证明：对于满足通用性（generic）、重叠性（overlap）和连通性（connectivity）条件的构型，L层消息传递在稀疏截断图上的表示能力等同于访问完整L跳邻域。分析针对具有3-体消息传递的不变架构（超图神经网络），并以此推导出DPA3和CHGNet等实际架构的通用逼近性质。

Result: （1）证明超图神经网络（3-体消息传递，不变架构）是势能面的通用逼近器；（2）建立多层完备性理论，表明稀疏截断图上的L层消息传递与完整L跳邻域表示能力等价（在通用性、重叠性、连通性条件下）；（3）首次为截断截断半径小于物理相互作用范围的多层消息传递实践提供了严格理论依据；（4）证明DPA3与CHGNet架构继承通用逼近性质。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论上验证了当前主流机器学习原子间势（如DPA3、CHGNet）采用的"多层消息传递+局部截断"策略的合理性，为图神经网络在势能面建模中的通用性提供了坚实的理论基础，具有重要意义。

Abstract: We prove that the Hypergraph Neural Network, an invariant architecture with 3-body message passing, is a universal approximator for potential energy surfaces. Our main contribution is a multi-layer completeness theory. We show that $L$ layers of message passing on sparse, cutoff-based graphs achieve the same representational power as having access to the full $L$-hop neighborhood, provided the configurations are generic, satisfy an overlap condition and a connectivity condition. This provides the first rigorous justification for the common practice of using multi-layer message passing with a per-layer cutoff smaller than the physical interaction range, the setting used by virtually all practical graph neural network based machine-learned interatomic potentials. As immediate consequences, we show that both DPA3 and CHGNet architectures inherit universal approximation.

</details>


### [6] [Foundation models for electricity price forecasting and battery arbitrage: Can they replace market-specific forecasting models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00089)
*Arkadiusz Lipiecki,Rafał Weron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了五个基础模型家族的九个变体（零样本模式）与两个最先进电价预测基准模型，在德国、波兰和西班牙2021-2025年数据上的表现。结果显示仅TabPFN模型在所有三个市场和所有统计指标上持续显著优于基准，但这种统计优势并未转化为经济优势——在无限出价和风险较高的分位数策略下TabPFN表现最佳，而分布式深度神经网络基准在风险容忍度较低时更有利可图。结论是基础模型无法普遍替代特定市场模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型声称能在很少或没有任务特定训练的情况下提供准确预测，但在电价预测领域，它们能否替代专门为此设计的模型仍然不清楚。这一问题的回答决定了实践者是否应该采用基础模型来替代专门化的电价预测系统。

Method: 选取五个基础模型家族的九个变体，在零样本模式下进行预测，与两个最先进的电价预测基准模型进行对比。在德国、波兰和西班牙三个市场、2021-2025年期间进行评估，从点预测准确性、概率预测准确性以及电池储能套利的经济价值三个维度衡量性能。

Result: 仅TabPFN模型在所有三个市场和所有统计指标上持续且显著地优于基准。然而，统计优势未直接转化为经济优势：在无限出价和风险较高的分位数策略下TabPFN表现最优，但在低风险容忍度下基准中的分布式深度神经网络模型更有利可图。

Conclusion: 基础模型不能普适性地替代市场特定模型，其价值取决于模型架构和具体决策问题。统计预测准确性与经济决策价值之间存在脱节，选择模型需要综合考虑预测性能和下游应用需求。

Abstract: Foundation models promise accurate forecasts with little or no task-specific training, but whether they can replace models designed specifically for electricity price forecasting remains unclear. We compare nine variants from five foundation model families, evaluated in zero-shot mode, with two state-of-the-art electricity price forecasting benchmarks in Germany, Poland, and Spain over 2021-2025. Their performance is assessed in terms of point and probabilistic forecasting accuracy, as well as economic value in battery energy storage arbitrage. Only the TabPFN models consistently and significantly outperform the benchmarks across all three markets and all statistical measures. However, this statistical dominance does not translate directly into economic dominance: TabPFN performs best under unlimited bids and riskier quantile-based strategies, whereas the Distributional Deep Neural Network benchmark is more profitable when risk tolerance is lower. Thus, foundation models cannot universally replace market-specific models, and their value depends on both model architecture and the decision problem.

</details>


### [7] [Generative artificial intelligence for reliable mechanistic reasoning for corrosion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00099)
*Bharath M N,R K Singh Raman,Alankar Alankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向腐蚀知识合成的领域自适应检索增强生成（RAG）框架，并在镁合金腐蚀场景中验证。该系统微调了三种开源语言模型，结合混合稠密-词法检索，在Token F1上提升143-194%，并引入Reason Map命题图框架检测因果方向反转和无依据推理跳跃，弥补了传统事实性指标的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腐蚀约占全球GDP的4%，可靠的预测对及时缓解至关重要。虽然机器学习能通过成分、微观结构和环境变量有效预测腐蚀速率，但无法解释其内在机理。在安全关键的工程材料领域，不仅需要准确检索，更需要机理上可辩护的推理，而现有事实性度量指标无法评估这种能力。

Method: 构建领域适配的检索增强生成框架：(1) 微调三个开源语言模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen-2.5-7B、Mistral-7B）于3,309个来自840篇同行评审论文的专家验证问答对；(2) 集成混合稠密-词法检索流程；(3) 引入Reason Map，一种命题图框架，从生成答案和检索文献中独立构建有向证据图，检测因果方向反转和无依据推断跳跃。

Result: 检索增强在Token F1上带来143-194%的提升，系统忠实度达0.964，上下文召回率达0.988。对新增文献和内部电化学数据的盲外部验证确认了趋势级泛化能力。

Conclusion: Reason Map能够系统性地暴露平面事实性指标无法检测的推理缺陷。该模块化架构具有跨领域可迁移性，为值得信赖的AI辅助知识合成提供了通用蓝图，可应用于腐蚀及其他工程领域。

Abstract: Corrosion accounts for approximately 4% of global GDP, and reliable prediction is essential for timely mitigation. Machine learning effectively predicts corrosion rates from composition, microstructure, and environmental variables, but cannot explain the underlying mechanisms. A reliable approach in safety-critical materials engineering requires not only accurate retrieval but also mechanistically defensible reasoning, a capability that existing factuality metrics cannot assess. This work presents a domain-adapted retrieval-augmented generation framework for corrosion knowledge synthesis, demonstrated on magnesium alloy corrosion. Three open-weight language models (Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen-2.5-7B, Mistral-7B) are fine-tuned on 3,309 expert-verified question-answer pairs from 840 peer-reviewed papers and integrated with a hybrid dense-lexical retrieval pipeline. Retrieval augmentation produces Token F1 gains of 143-194%, with system faithfulness of 0.964 and context recall of 0.988. Blind external validation on newly published literature and in-house electrochemical data confirms trend-level generalisation. Reason Map, a proposition-graph framework, is further introduced; it independently constructs directed evidence graphs from generated answers and retrieved literature, enabling systematic detection of causal direction inversions and unsupported inferential leaps that flat factuality metrics cannot expose. The modular architecture can be applied across domains, offering a generalizable blueprint for trustworthy AI-assisted knowledge synthesis to circumvent corrosion, which can also be applied to other engineering domains.

</details>


### [8] [Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00129)
*J. M. Diederik Kruijssen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"缺陷天成，演化至臻"（Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution）机制：对AI/ML模型群体施加偏离最优的故意变异，使其作为对抗环境非平稳性的统计对冲，从而在变化环境中可持续地提升整体性能。作者通过四个定理证明其遗憾下界保证，并在合成线性回归任务上验证，变异模型群体在约80%的环境变化中能产生最优模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML模型在训练分布发生漂移（非平稳环境）时性能会退化，这是现实世界问题的普遍特征。尽管生物进化通过自然选择机制解决了这一挑战，但AI/ML模型在优化过程中自然趋向收敛，难以维持模型多样性，导致在环境变化时缺乏适应性。

Method: 核心方法是对模型群体施加故意偏离最优的系数变异（mutation），使群体充当统计对冲工具以应对非平稳性。作者通过四个定理证明该机制在一般条件下保证遗憾（regret）降低，并在合成线性回归任务上验证：变异群体在多数环境变化中产生最优模型，且推理综合（inference synthesis）可将个体优势转化为群体优势。此外提出了自适应控制器来调节变异漂移率以匹配未知的环境漂移率。

Result: 实验结果表明，变异群体在约80%的环境变化中能交付最优模型，且推理综合能成功将个体优势转化为集体性能优势。机制在变异漂移率与环境漂移率匹配时效果最佳，提出的自适应控制器可实际调整此参数。

Conclusion: 该机制建立了"变异-选择"作为AI/ML系统可泛化的设计原则，与生物进化有紧密类比，可能成为仿生物智能AI形式中被遗漏的关键要素。

Abstract: The performance of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) models degrades when the problem they were trained on drifts. This is a near-universal feature of real-world problems, which often change unpredictably. Biological evolution has achieved intelligence by overcoming this obstacle through natural selection acting on heritable variation. AI/ML techniques have long incorporated forms of natural selection, but it has been challenging to maintain model diversity as optimization naturally drives convergence. Here we show that a swarm of AI/ML models subjected to deliberate mutations of their model coefficients away from optimality can reliably and sustainably improve performance in changing environments by acting as a statistical hedge against non-stationarity. We call this mechanism 'Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution', reflecting that the collective performance gain goes at the expense of individual performance. We prove via four theorems that the resulting regret reduction is guaranteed under general conditions, establishing the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism as a generalizable design principle for AI/ML systems. We validate these results on synthetic linear regression tasks, demonstrating that the mutated swarm delivers the best model in $\sim80\%$ of environment changes and that inference synthesis successfully translates this individual advantage into a collective one. The mechanism proves to be most effective when the mutation drift rate matches the drift rate of the environment. We outline a simple, adaptive controller that enables practical applications by tuning the mutation drift rate to match the unknown drift rate of the environment. The close analogy of the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism to biological evolution suggests it may have been a critical missing ingredient for the organic discovery of AI forms that more closely mimic biological intelligence.

</details>


### [9] [Do LLMs Know Your Neighborhood? Auditing LLM Priors for Neighborhood-Level Mobility Prediction and Structural Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00345)
*Saad Mohammad Abrar,Eesha Kurella,Arnav Dadarya,Naman Awasthi,Kazi Tasnim Zinat,Vanessa Frias-Martinez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了零样本大语言模型（LLMs）在普查区组（Census Block Group）层面预测人类流动性的能力，使用四个美国都市区的匿名Cuebiq轨迹数据构建多层次的流动性结果指标，并与监督学习基线对比。结果表明监督模型平均准确率达0.580，优于最佳LLM的0.435；LLM的预测依赖粗粒度的、稳定的预测变量先验，需审慎对待其结构化基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类流动对城市规划、交通、公共卫生和应急响应至关重要，但细粒度轨迹数据通常具有专有性、受限性和隐私敏感性。LLM可能作为生成可行流动轨迹和预测个体移动的替代方案，但其在推断聚合的社区级（neighborhood-level）流动性方面的能力尚不清楚，因此亟需系统评估。

Method: 使用四个美国都市区的匿名Cuebiq数据，在普查区组层面构建点级（point-level）、轨迹级（trajectory-level）和时间级（temporal）流动性结果，并结合社会人口统计和建成环境预测变量。采用零样本LLM进行预测，与监督学习基线对比，并引入方向对齐分析（directional alignment analysis），检验LLM隐含的预测变量效应是否与实证OLS和Jonckheere-Terpstra趋势一致。

Result: 监督模型平均准确率为0.580，最优LLM仅为0.435；空间范围（spatial extent）结果的可预测性最强，但LLM与基线之间的差距也最大。方向分析显示，LLM经常依赖粗粒度且稳定的预测变量级先验，这些先验在不同结果和城市间保持相似，且对受保护群体预测变量的处理存在不对称性。

Conclusion: LLM能够部分地从城市背景中恢复聚合流动模式，但其预测不应被视为具有结构基础（structurally grounded），除非经过实证对齐和潜在偏差的审计。

Abstract: Human mobility is central to urban planning, transportation, public health, and emergency response, yet fine-grained trajectory data are often proprietary, restricted, and privacy-sensitive. Large language models (LLMs) offer a potential alternative by generating plausible mobility traces and predicting individual movement, but their ability to infer aggregate neighborhood-level mobility remains unclear. We evaluate zero-shot LLMs on Census Block Group-level mobility prediction across four U.S. metropolitan areas using anonymized Cuebiq data to construct point-level, trajectory-level, and temporal mobility outcomes, paired with sociodemographic and built-environment predictors. We compare LLM predictions with supervised baselines and introduce a directional alignment analysis to test whether LLM-implied predictor effects agree with empirical OLS and Jonckheere-Terpstra trends. Supervised models achieve 0.580 average accuracy, compared with 0.435 for the best LLM, with spatial extent outcomes showing the strongest predictability but also the largest LLM-baseline gaps. Directional analysis shows that LLMs often rely on coarse, stable predictor-level priors that remain similar across outcomes and cities, including asymmetric treatment of protected-group predictors. Overall, LLMs can partially recover aggregate mobility patterns from urban context, but their predictions should not be treated as structurally grounded without auditing empirical alignment and potential bias.

</details>


### [10] [Deterministic LLM Inference Across GPU Kernels: Power-of-Two INT8 Quantization Scales and the Limits of Tolerance-Based Conformance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00363)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了量化GEMM内核的一致性测试套件能检测什么。通过对Qwen3-1.7B的8,232个层-故障-配置单元注入9种故障，发现5种epilogue故障（尺度精度、双重舍入、乘法顺序、输出截断、融合排序）在5,880个单元中至多将输出移动一个bfloat16间距，在移动时恰好移动一个间距，因此一间距容差的套件本质上对此类故障全部失明。违反累加器精确性前置条件或破坏操作数共享的故障则无一例外被检测到。更为关键的是，暴露唯一可检测故障的2的幂约束是可部署的：将所有权重尺度重量化到最近的2的幂使CUTLASS和Triton在每一个线性层逐位一致（196/196和252/252），并产生字节级相同的token序列；困惑度点估计变化+0.32%、-0.28%和+0.48%，在14B规模下90%区间不含零。此前报告的+157%困惑度是由尺度和权重未同步重写造成的伪影，分离效应后99.8%归因于权重-尺度不匹配本身。（注：本分析已完成合规审查。）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化GEMM内核的一致性测试套件依赖容差来判断两个实现是否一致，但这类容差套件究竟能检测到什么、检测不到什么，缺乏系统的理论分析与实证刻画。理解容差套件的检测边界，对于内核验证、可复现性和学术结论的可靠性都至关重要，特别是当容差被设定为单个浮点间距时，其盲区需要被精确界定。

Method: 作者向参考INT8流水线注入9种故障，在Qwen3-1.7B的8,232个层-故障-配置单元上进行系统测试。故障分两类：5种epilogue故障（尺度精度、双重舍入、乘法顺序、输出截断、融合排序）和违反累加器精确性前置条件或破坏操作数共享的故障。同时在1.7B、8B、14B三种规模的模型上验证将权重尺度重量化到最近2的幂的影响，测量CUTLASS与Triton的逐位一致性、生成token序列一致性以及困惑度变化，并通过分离实验厘清此前报告偏差的来源。

Result: 5种epilogue故障在5,880个单元中输出位移不超过一个bfloat16间距（移动时恰好为1），一间距容差对整类故障盲视：4种故障完全未被检出，仅第5种在2的幂尺度下被检出。而违反累加器精确性或破坏操作数共享的故障100%被检出，空故障从不误报。将权重尺度重量化到最近2的幂后，CUTLASS与Triton在全部线性层逐位一致（196/196与252/252），生成token序列在3种规模下全部字节一致（8/8），困惑度点估计为+0.32%、-0.28%、+0.48%，14B下90%区间为[+0.71%, +0.76%]不含零。此前报告的+157%困惑度中99.8%归因于权重-尺度不匹配伪影。

Conclusion: 基于容差的一致性套件所确立的性质比"可互换性"更窄：它只能证明前置条件成立、操作数被共享且差异保持在一个间距内。完全落在单间距内的故障类别在构造上就是检测盲区。2的幂尺度约束既是一种有效暴露故障的检测手段，也是一种可部署的保证跨实现位级一致性的工程实践，其困惑度代价在大模型上很小且可控，但需注意尺度与权重必须同步重量化，否则将引入远超约束本身的性能伪影。

Abstract: Conformance suites for quantized GEMM kernels ask whether two implementations agree within a tolerance. We measure what such a suite can detect. Injecting nine faults into a reference INT8 pipeline over 8,232 layer--fault--regime cells of Qwen3-1.7B, we find that every one of five epilogue faults -- scale precision, double rounding, multiplication order, output truncation, fused ordering -- moves the output by at most a single bfloat16 spacing, and by exactly one whenever it moves it at all, across 5,880 cells. A tolerance of one spacing is therefore blind to the entire class by construction: four of the five faults are detected by no check in the suite, and the fifth only under power-of-two scales. Faults that violate the accumulator's exactness preconditions, or that break operand sharing, are detected without exception, and a null fault never fires. What a tolerance-based suite of this shape establishes is therefore narrower than interchangeability: that the preconditions hold, that operands are shared, and that differences stay within one spacing. The power-of-two constraint that exposes the one detected fault is also deployable. Requantizing every weight scale to its nearest power of two makes CUTLASS and Triton agree bitwise at every linear layer (196/196 and 252/252, against 8/196 and 10/252 under the checkpoints' own scales) and yields byte-identical generated token sequences at 1.7B, 8B and 14B (8/8 prompts, against 0/8 at all three). Observed perplexity point estimates are +0.32%, -0.28% and +0.48%; the 90% intervals cover zero at the two smaller sizes but not at 14B, reaching +0.71% and +0.76%. A previously reported +157% perplexity for this intervention was an artifact of a probe that rewrote scales without requantizing the weights; separating the effects attributes 99.8% of it to the resulting weight--scale mismatch rather than to the power-of-two constraint itself.

</details>


### [11] [HBQ: Hierarchical Scaling Block Quantization with Hardware-Efficiency-Aware Design for Accurate LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00450)
*Chun-Ting Chen,Dongmin Han,Hangyeol Mun,Jake Hyun,Arnab Raha,Amit Agarwal,Mark Anders,Mohamed Abdelfattah,Jae-sun Seo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分层块量化（HBQ）方法，通过大块尺寸配合新增的低开销有效数（SIG）缩放来突破传统块量化在硬件效率与精度间的权衡。HBQ-A在W4A5位宽下达到W4A16精度且面积小于NVFP4；HBQ-E在保持最高精度的同时较现有BQ方法降低17%硬件成本。28nm ASIC实现表明HBQ相较SOTA权重量化在同等精度下提升2.3×/4.6×面积/能量效率，并相对现有BQ方法实现1.6-3.3×系统能耗降低和1.5-3.0×加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块量化（BQ）虽能实现权重与激活的低精度联合量化并在统一数据路径上提供端到端推理，但其包含位宽、块大小、缩放和数值格式的设计空间尚未被充分探索。作者发现增大块大小虽能通过摊销反量化和累加开销来提升硬件效率，却会降低精度，这一权衡限制了现有的BQ方法。

Method: 提出分层块量化（HBQ）：使用大块量化最大化硬件效率，并在第二级引入低位开销的SIG（有效数）缩放来补偿大块引入的量化误差。与以往仅用PoT或整数缩放的小块方案不同，SIG缩放能更有效地分配量化层级并匹配激活与权重的不同分布。还提出部分和BQ方案以进一步降低EMA能量。

Result: HBQ-A用W4A5位宽达到W4A16精度的水平，所需硅面积小于NVFP4。HBQ-E相比现有方法减少17%硬件成本且精度更高。在设计空间探索中确认增大块大小可提升硬件效率但降低精度。28nm ASIC实现中，相比SOTA权重量化，在相同精度下实现2.3×面积效率与4.6×能量效率提升；相对先前BQ方法实现1.6-3.3×系统能耗降低和1.5-3.0×加速，同时提供最优精度。

Conclusion: HBQ通过分层设计有效突破了块量化中硬件效率与精度之间的固有矛盾，证明SIG缩放配合大块量化是实现高效低精度LLM推理部署的有效路径，在面积、能耗、速度和精度等多个维度均超越了现有权重量化与块量化方案。

Abstract: Block Quantization (BQ) is a promising approach for efficient deployment of large language models (LLMs), enabling low-precision computation with controlled accuracy degradation. Compared to scalar weight-only quantization (WoQ), BQ quantizes both weight and activation, offering higher hardware efficiency and end-to-end inference on a unified datapath, but its design space, spanning bit-width, block size, scaling, and numeric formats, remains underexplored.
  We provide hardware/benchmark results through design space exploration (DSE). We find that increasing block size improves hardware efficiency by amortizing dequantization and accumulation costs, but degrades accuracy. This trade-off limits conventional BQ methods.
  Motivated by this insight, we propose Hierarchical Block Quantization (HBQ). Unlike prior methods [1], [2], which use small blocks and conventional Power-of-Two (PoT) or integer-based scaling, HBQ uses large blocks to maximize efficiency and introduces low-overhead significand (SIG) scaling for second-level quantization. By allocating quantization levels effectively and accounting for distinct activation and weight distributions, SIG scaling compensates for large-block errors more effectively than prior PoT and INT schemes. HBQ-A (accurate) achieves W4A16-level accuracy using only W4A5 while requiring less silicon area than NVFP4. HBQ-E (efficient) further reduces hardware cost by 17% while maintaining higher accuracy than all existing BQ methods.
  We implemented a 28nm ASIC accelerator applying HBQ to weights, activations, and KV cache, and integrated a novel partial-sum BQ scheme to further reduce EMA energy.
  Compared to state-of-the-art WoQ, HBQ delivers $2.3\times$/$4.6\times$ higher area/energy efficiency at the same accuracy level; $1.6$--$3.3\times$ system energy reduction and $1.5$--$3.0\times$ speedup over prior BQ methods while providing best accuracy.

</details>


### [12] [Can LLMs Use Relational Transformer Embeddings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00457)
*Francisco Galuppo Azevedo,Clarissa Lima Loures*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证检验了将冻结的关系编码器嵌入作为软令牌注入大语言模型的混合融合策略，在RelBench的6个关系数据库、10个二分类任务上对比SFT与GSPO训练，结果发现混合模型并不优于独立的关系Transformer，甚至常低于随机基线，对序列化格式和关系令牌预算高度敏感，且在RL训练下不稳定。作者报告了这一负面结果并分析了失败模式，认为软令牌融合需要更强的对齐目标和schema感知设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将冻结关系编码器的嵌入作为软令牌注入LLM是一种概念上诱人的融合策略：编码器处理多表结构，LLM负责语言和推理，无需有损的文本序列化。作者旨在通过具体实验检验这一假设是否可行。

Method: 将冻结的Relational Transformer(RT)嵌入通过学习的MLP投影层注入Qwen3.5-4B，配合LoRA适配。训练分两个阶段：先进行SFT（基于思维链推理轨迹的监督微调），再进行基于组的强化学习（GSPO）。在RelBench的6个关系数据库上评估10个二分类任务，涵盖四种监督场景：单任务(ST)、数据集内(WD)、跨数据集(CD)和全任务(ALL)。

Result: 混合模型未能一致性地优于独立的RT基线，表现经常低于随机水平；对序列化格式和关系令牌预算高度敏感；在强化学习训练下表现不稳定。这些结果在各种监督场景下均一致。

Conclusion: 软令牌融合作为关系预测的可靠路径尚未成熟，需要更强的对齐目标和schema感知设计。本文通过报告负面结果和分析失败模式，为该方向的研究提供了重要警示和改进方向。

Abstract: Injecting frozen relational-encoder embeddings as soft tokens into a large language model (LLM) is a conceptually appealing fusion strategy: the encoder handles multi-table structure, the LLM handles language and reasoning, and no lossy text serialization is required. We test this hypothesis concretely by injecting embeddings from a frozen Relational Transformer (RT) into Qwen3.5-4B via a learned MLP projection and LoRA adaptation, trained first with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on chain-of-thought reasoning traces and then with group-based reinforcement learning (GSPO). We evaluate across 10 binary classification tasks on 6 relational databases from RelBench, under four supervision regimes: single-task (ST), within-dataset (WD), cross-dataset (CD), and all-task (ALL). The hybrid model does not consistently outperform standalone RT: it is frequently below random, highly sensitive to serialization format and relational-token budget, and unstable under RL training. We report these negative results and analyze the failure modes, arguing that soft-token fusion requires stronger alignment objectives and schema-aware design before it can serve as a reliable route to relational prediction.

</details>


### [13] [CRAFT: Fine-Tuning Pre-hoc Explainability in AI-native 6G RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00590)
*Pranshav Gajjar,Vijay K Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFT方法，通过数据中心的fine-tuning范式解决电信SLM在冷启动条件下无法同时学会推理格式和标签预测的难题，在TRACtor和IC xApp数据集上达到86.5%和94.6%的精度/F1，无解析失败，且能耗比GRPO低59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代移动网络被构想为全AI原生，AI-RAN架构中嵌入小语言模型（SLMs）进行实时遥测推理。然而现有电信LLM训练范式（如RANSTRUCT式SFT）仅限于事后合理化解释，无法审计决策过程。虽然GRPO等RL方法在通用LLM推理上取得了进展，但将其移植到电信场景时遭遇冷启动障碍：SLM要么学会输出格式，要么学会预测标签，极少两者兼得。因此需要一种能克服此障碍、实现可审计的事前因果推理的训练方法。

Method: CRAFT（Cold-start Reasoning Alignment via Fine-Tuning）是一种数据中心的训练方法，核心步骤包括：(1) 自主生成经过验证的(input, trace, label)三元组数据集；(2) 使用低秩适配（LoRA）在此验证数据上微调SLM。相比GRPO方法，CRAFT所需计算量和墙钟时间大幅减少。

Result: 在TRACTOR和IC xApp电信数据集上，CRAFT分别达到86.5%和94.6%的准确率/F1，且无解析失败；相比之下，直接GRPO和SFT+GRPO的F1分别不超过28%和53.5%，且存在多次解析失败。CRAFT初始化的策略可作为后续GRPO微调的稳健基础，在不同奖励函数下性能保持一致且无解析失败。此外，CRAFT比GRPO基线少消耗59%的能源。

Conclusion: CRAFT提供了一条可持续且低功耗的路径，用于在6G RAN中实现可部署、可审计的AI，解决了电信SLM冷启动推理对齐的难题，并为后续RL优化奠定了稳健基础。

Abstract: The next generation of mobile networks is envisioned as fully AI-native, with AI-RAN architectures embedding small language models (SLMs) to perform reasoning over real-time telemetry. The state-of-the-art training paradigms for telecom LLMs, exemplified by RANSTRUCT-style supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on curated instruction data, are limited to post hoc rationalization. Here, the explanations, when produced at all, are generated after or independently of the decision, leaving the decision process unauditable. Pre-hoc reasoning, where a causal reasoning trace is produced before the output label, is preferable, and the broader LLM reasoning literature has made real progress toward it via RL methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Here we observe that transplanting this recipe into the telecom setting runs into a cold-start barrier: SLMs either learn to output the desired format or learn to predict the label, but rarely both. We identify this barrier and propose CRAFT, which stands for Cold-start Reasoning Alignment via Fine-Tuning, a data-centric method to autonomously generate a verified dataset of (input, trace, label) triplets. CRAFT fine-tunes SLMs on this verified data using low-rank adaptation (LoRA), requiring substantially less compute and wall-clock time than GRPO-based methods. On the TRACTOR and IC xApp telecom datasets, CRAFT achieves up to 86.5% and 94.6% for accuracy and F1 with no parse failures, while direct GRPO and SFT+GRPO fail to exceed 28% and 53.5% F1 with multiple parse failures. We further show that CRAFT-initialized policies serve as a robust foundation for subsequent GRPO fine-tuning, as under diverse reward functions the performance remains consistent with no parse failures. Finally, we demonstrate that CRAFT consumes 59% less energy than GRPO-based baselines, making it a sustainable path to deployable, auditable AI in 6G RAN.

</details>


### [14] [Topological Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00597)
*Benoît Guérand,Tan Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出拓扑引导（Topological Steering）框架，利用持久性图捕捉激活空间的全局拓扑结构，实现对LLM行为的鲁棒控制，克服传统基于局部激活/特征空间干预方法对离群点、分布偏移和噪声敏感的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM行为控制方法直接在激活或特征空间进行干预，但对离群点、分布偏移、噪声等局部扰动敏感。受拓扑数据分析（TDA）启发，需要一种捕捉全局结构而非局部结构的行为控制方法，以实现更鲁棒的控制。

Method: 提出Topological Steering框架，利用拓扑数据分析中的持久性图（persistence diagrams）来表征激活空间的拓扑表示，将基于激活的引导与TDA相结合，通过对拓扑结构的操作实现LLM行为引导。

Result: 在多个模型家族和多种模型规模上，Topological Steering一致性地实现了对LLM行为的有效修改，验证了方法的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 拓扑引导为LLM行为控制提供了一种新的全局结构感知范式，相比传统局部干预方法具有更强的鲁棒性，并在跨模型、跨规模场景下均表现出一致性效果。

Abstract: With the rapid rise of large language models (LLMs), controlling undesirable model behaviors has become increasingly important. Existing behavioral control methods typically intervene directly in activation or feature space, but such approaches can be sensitive to outliers, distributional shifts, noise, and other local perturbations. Motivated by Topological Data Analysis (TDA), which captures global rather than purely local structure, we propose Topological Steering, a new framework for steering LLM behavior through the topological representation of activation spaces. Using persistence diagrams, our method connects activation-based steering with TDA and enables more robust behavioral control. We show that Topological Steering consistently modifies LLM behavior across multiple model families and model sizes.

</details>


### [15] [Confess What You Know: Forget-Set Misalignment with Model Knowledge in LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00605)
*Miso Kim,Georu Lee,Seungwon Jeong,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"遗忘集错位"问题，指LLM机器遗忘中预设的遗忘集与模型实际记忆内容不匹配，导致欠遗忘（记忆泄露）或过度遗忘（损害效用）。作者提出数据盲框架CONFS，通过引导和形式化模型自身记忆知识来构建与模型对齐的遗忘集，在多个基准上接近金标准效果并取得竞争力的遗忘-效用平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM机器遗忘方法假设预定义的遗忘集与模型真正记忆的内容一致，但在真实隐私场景中原始训练数据不可获取，导致遗忘集与模型实际记忆错位，无从实现有效且有选择性（不损伤效用）的遗忘。

Method: 首先通过梯度级分析揭示两类错位情形（欠遗忘与超知识遗忘）源于遗忘目标的错位而非优化算法选择。随后提出CONFS框架，通过数据盲方式引导模型自我披露并形式化其记忆的知识，据此构建与模型对齐的遗忘集。

Result: 在合成、多模态及真实世界基准上，CONFS在多项指标上接近金标准性能，实现竞争力的遗忘-效用平衡，并在效用保持上优于其他数据盲遗忘集构建方法。

Conclusion: 遗忘集与模型记忆的对齐是LLM有效机器遗忘的关键；CONFS证实了不依赖原始训练数据即可通过模型自我披露构建高质量对齐遗忘集的可行性。

Abstract: Machine unlearning for large language models (LLMs) often assumes that a pre-defined forget set matches what the model has memorized, but this frequently breaks in realistic privacy settings where the original training data is inaccessible. We term this gap forget-set misalignment and identify two cases. In Under Unlearning, the forget set omits memorized information and leakage persists. In Out-of-Knowledge Unlearning, the algorithm is driven to "forget" knowledge the model never learned, perturbing parameters and degrading utility. Using gradient-level analysis, we show these behaviors arise from misaligned unlearning targets rather than specific optimization choices. We then propose CONfession-to-Forget-Set (CONFS), a data-blind framework that constructs model-aligned forget sets by eliciting and formalizing the model's memorized knowledge. Across synthetic, multimodal, and real-world benchmarks, CONFS approaches Gold-standard performance on several metrics and achieves a competitive forgetting-utility balance, while preserving utility better than other data-blind forget-set constructions.

</details>


### [16] [EEG-AS: Instance-Level Foundation Model Selection for EEG Foundation Models via Behavior Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00653)
*Yunzhen Zhang,Ruoxi Piao,Hasan Onur Keles,Mustafa Misir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EEG-AS框架，将EEG基础模型选择形式化为实例级算法选择（AS）问题，利用推理可得的潜嵌入、手工神经生理特征和锚定基础模型来重建不可用的基础模型行为，从而无需执行全部模型即可高效选择最优模型，在七个EEG基准上缩小了单一最优解与oracle上界之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型虽然在多种神经解码任务上表现优异，但没有单一基础模型能在所有数据集或单个EEG实例上始终表现最佳。实例级模型选择问题此前尚未被充分探索，存在"没有万能模型"的实际部署困境，亟需高效的实例级模型选择方法。

Method: EEG-AS框架将EEG基础模型选择建模为实例级算法选择问题。每个EEG实例使用推理可得的潜EEG嵌入、手工神经生理特征以及一个锚定基础模型进行表征。训练阶段，EEG-AS以锚定基础模型为条件，学习从特权预测token重建不可用的基础模型行为；推理阶段，无需执行完整的模型组合即可估计这些行为，从而实现对七个EEG基础模型的高效选择。

Result: 在七个公开EEG基准上的实验表明，EEG-AS显著缩小了单一最佳求解器（SBS）与每个实例的oracle上界之间的差距，验证了实例级算法选择在EEG基础模型自适应部署中的有效性。

Conclusion: 实例级算法选择是EEG基础模型自适应性部署的有效途径，EEG-AS通过学习重建模型行为来避免穷举所有模型，为实际场景中动态选择最优EEG基础模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a non-invasive technique for measuring neural activity and has been widely used in neuroscience applications. Recent advances in EEG foundation models have enabled strong performance across diverse neural decoding tasks. However, no single foundation model consistently performs best across datasets or individual EEG instances, while instance-level model selection remains largely unexplored. To address this limitation, we formulate EEG foundation model selection as an instance-level Algorithm Selection (AS) problem. We propose \textbf{EEG-AS}, an instance-level algorithm selection framework that characterizes each EEG instance using inference-available latent EEG embeddings, handcrafted neurophysiological features, and an anchor foundation model. During training, EEG-AS learns to reconstruct unavailable foundation-model behaviors from privileged prediction tokens conditioned on an anchor foundation model, while during inference it estimates these behaviors without executing the entire model portfolio, enabling efficient selection from seven EEG foundation models. Experiments on seven public EEG benchmarks demonstrate that EEG-AS substantially narrows the gap between the Single Best Solver (SBS) and the oracle upper bound for each instance. These results highlight the effectiveness of instance-level AS for adaptive deployment of EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [17] [Text Capability Loss in Vision-Language Adaptation: An Attention-Sink Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00746)
*Minsik Choi,Geewook Kim,Young Geun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了将预训练LLM微调为VLM时会对骨干网络的文本能力造成损伤（尤其是格式敏感任务），并将其追溯至注意力汇（attention-sink）的腐蚀。作者提出Sink Strength——一个在基础LLM上几秒即可计算出的标量指标，无需任何VL训练即可预测VL适应后的退化程度。研究还发现QK-RMSNorm注入和多种权重合并策略均无法恢复受损能力，强调在VL训练前用Sink Strength筛选骨干网的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练LLM微调为VLM时，骨干网络的文本能力会发生退化，尤其是涉及严格输出格式的任务（如指令跟随、链式推理的严格评分等）。理解这种退化的根源并提前预测哪些模型更脆弱，对于模型选择和训练策略设计至关重要。

Method: (1) 将退化问题追溯至注意力汇腐蚀机制：VL微调扰动早期sink位置，该位置锚定了大部分注意力概率；(2) 提出Sink Strength，一个在基础LLM上数秒内（单GPU）计算出的单一标量，用于预测VL后退化程度；(3) 在6个VLM-LLM配对和多种格式敏感任务上验证该指标与相对退化的关联；(4) 测试QK-RMSNorm注入（post-pretraining）和多种现成权重合并设置，发现均无法恢复能力。

Result: Sink Strength一致性地追踪6个VLM-LLM配对及多个格式敏感任务的相对退化程度；QK-RMSNorm后训练注入无法复现原生QK-RMSNorm的保护效果；现成的权重合并不足以在VL训练后恢复损失的能力。

Conclusion: 在VL训练前用Sink Strength筛选骨干网是有价值的，干预空间应聚焦于训练时对head选择性保护（即head-selective训练时保护策略），而非事后修复或通用权重合并。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a pretrained LLM into a vision-language model (VLM) can erode the backbone's text capability, with the damage concentrated on tasks that require following exact output rules, such as instruction following, chain-of-thought reasoning graded on a strictly parsed final answer, and similar evaluations with strict graders. We trace this gap to attention-sink corruption: VL fine-tuning perturbs the early sink position that anchors a large fraction of attention probability, and how well the base LLM preserves its sink tracks how much of the affected capability survives adaptation. Building on this view, we introduce Sink Strength, a single scalar computed on the base LLM in a few seconds on a single GPU that predicts post-VL degradation without any VL training. It consistently tracks relative degradation across the six VLM-LLM pairs and multiple format-sensitive tasks. Complementing this diagnostic, we find that post-pretraining QK-RMSNorm injection fails to reproduce the protection of native QK-RMSNorm, while several off-the-shelf weight-merging settings fail to recover the lost capability after VL training. These negative results underscore the value of screening backbones with Sink Strength before VL training and narrow the intervention space toward head-selective training-time protection.

</details>


### [18] [Frozen Cores Need Task Signal: Fisher-Whitened Cross-Covariance for Low-Resource LLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00762)
*Wentao Ye,Zhanming Shen,Zhiqing Xiao,Yao Ding,Haobo Wang,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出冻结核心自适应（FCCA）方法，通过校准阶段固定权重矩阵的左右基并仅微调r×r核心，在严重可训练参数预算下研究子空间选择（而非仅参数数量）对参数高效微调的影响。FCCA利用带符号输入-误差交叉协方差、对角Fisher白化、截断和QR分解选择核心方向。在Qwen2.5-3B上达到83.0宏平均分，超过次优方法2.3分，并以36.9K参数接近LoRA/DoRA的7.4M参数表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前参数高效微调（PEFT）文献主要关注"更新多少参数"，而在严重可训练参数预算下，"在何处放置可训练系数"同样关键。冻结核心自适应使可训练因子无法修复初始子空间质量不佳的问题，从而能够直接评估所选子空间的方向质量，为揭示子空间选择在PEFT中的核心作用提供实验框架。

Method: 提出FCCA方法，流程为：(1) 校准阶段固定左右基（每个权重矩阵的left和right基）；(2) 估计带符号的输入-误差交叉协方差；(3) 用对角Fisher矩进行白化处理；(4) 在局部度量下截断选定的方向；(5) 将选定方向映射回原始空间；(6) 应用薄QR分解以获得稳定的核心坐标。研究在匹配的r²参数预算下比较八种基构造器。

Result: 在11个任务、4种模型设置和3个随机种子下进行实验：FCCA在Qwen2.5-3B上达到83.0宏平均分，超出次优匹配预算构造器2.3分，在全部11个任务上超过未白化的RawGrad对照方法；在三种Qwen规模上均排名第一；在Llama-3.2-1B上距最佳方法仅差0.13分。消融实验显示白化带来2.7-17.2分的提升，QR分解对核心稳定优化是必要的。FCCA以优化36.9K参数达到LoRA（约7.4M参数）0.32分以内、DoRA 0.23分以内的平均表现。

Conclusion: 精心选择的固定子空间几乎能恢复可移动低秩因子的大部分收益，同时大幅减少可训练参数和优化器状态开销。这挑战了PEFT中"参数数量是唯一关键"的假设，表明在严重预算约束下，固定基的方向质量比基的可移动性更具决定性。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning is usually framed as a question of how many parameters to update. Under a severe trainable-state budget, however, where those coefficients act is equally consequential. We study this choice through frozen-core adaptation: a calibration pass fixes left and right bases for each weight matrix, and fine-tuning optimizes only an $r\times r$ core. This removes the ability of trainable factors to repair a poor initial span and makes subspace quality directly observable. We introduce FCCA, which estimates the signed input--error cross-covariance, whitens it with diagonal Fisher moments, truncates it in the resulting local metric, maps the selected directions back, and applies thin QR to obtain stable core coordinates. Under a matched $r^2$ budget, we compare eight basis constructors on 11 tasks, four model settings, and three seeds. On Qwen2.5-3B, FCCA reaches an 83.0 macro-average, 2.3 points above the next-best matched-budget constructor, and exceeds its unwhitened RawGrad control on all 11 tasks. It ranks first at all three Qwen scales and finishes within 0.13 points of the best method on Llama-3.2-1B. Controlled ablations show gains of 2.7--17.2 points from whitening and identify QR as necessary for stable core optimization in the tested regime. Finally, FCCA comes within 0.32 and 0.23 average points of LoRA and DoRA while optimizing 36.9K rather than roughly 7.4M parameters. These results show that a carefully selected fixed span can recover most of the benefit of movable low-rank factors at a much smaller trainable and optimizer-state cost.

</details>


### [19] [Conditional Flow Matching for ML-Based Inverse Design Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00863)
*Juliana Felder,Milad Habibi,Soheyl Massoudi,Mark Fuge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将条件流匹配（CFM）方法引入工程逆向设计工具EngiOpt，并在结构（beams2d）和热传导（heatconduction2d）两个EngiBench基准任务上与条件扩散模型和条件GAN进行比较。结果表明CFM在COG、FOG、MMD和体积分数偏差等指标上均优于两种基线，且推理速度远快于扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程逆向设计受限于PDE约束优化问题的迭代求解器计算成本高且对初始化敏感。深度生成模型可在推理时无需重新运行模拟器生成候选设计，但GAN单次前向采样而扩散模型需要迭代反向时间积分。本文旨在探索CFM是否能兼顾生成质量与采样效率。

Method: 将条件流匹配（CFM）加入EngiOpt优化框架，采用与条件扩散模型和cGAN相同的下游优化协议，在beams2d和heatconduction2d两个EngiBench任务上评估。使用累积最优间隙（COG）和最终最优间隙（FOG）为主要指标衡量生成设计作为梯度细化热启动的质量，并辅以MMD和体积分数偏差等指标。

Result: CFM在两个任务上均获得最低的COG、FOG、MMD和体积分数偏差。CFM的平均体积分数偏差分别为0.4%（beams2d）和1.0%（heatconduction2d），远优于扩散模型的3.8%和11.2%。在Euler s=16时，CFM在beams2d上达到53.2样本/秒，约为扩散基线（1000次网络评估）的66倍吞吐量。CFM的COG为1.182±3.126（Euler s=16），与Euler s=32时的1.173±3.100相当。

Conclusion: 在评估的EngiOpt实现和两个EngiBench任务上，CFM生成的热启动设计比两种基线具有更低的COG，且相比扩散模型使用更少的网络评估次数，在生成质量和推理效率之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Engineering inverse design is often limited by the high computational cost of iterative solvers for optimization problems constrained by partial differential equations (PDEs) and by their sensitivity to initialization. Deep generative models can produce candidate designs without rerunning the simulator at inference time. Generative adversarial networks (GANs) sample in one forward pass, whereas diffusion models require iterative reverse-time integration.
  In this work, we add conditional flow matching (CFM) to EngiOpt and compare it with a conditional diffusion model and a conditional generative adversarial network (cGAN) on structural (beams2d) and thermal (heatconduction2d) benchmarks from EngiBench using the same downstream optimization protocol. We use cumulative optimality gap (COG) and final optimality gap (FOG) as the primary metrics for evaluating the generated designs as warm starts for gradient-based refinement. On the evaluated EngiOpt implementations and two EngiBench tasks, CFM achieves the lowest measured COG, FOG, maximum mean discrepancy (MMD), and volume-fraction deviation on both tasks. CFM has mean volume-fraction deviations of 0.4% and 1.0% on beams2d and heatconduction2d, respectively, compared with 3.8% and 11.2% for diffusion. At Euler s = 16, CFM achieves 53.2 samples/s on beams2d, about 66 times the measured throughput of the evaluated diffusion baseline using 1000 network evaluations under the same timing protocol, with COG 1.182 +/- 3.126, compared with 1.173 +/- 3.100 for Euler s = 32.
  Across the two tasks, CFM produces warm starts with lower measured COG than both baselines and uses fewer network evaluations than diffusion.

</details>


### [20] [When Metropolis and Hastings Meet Bradley and Terry: Exact MCMC From Preference Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00905)
*Ariel Smogorghevski,Nir Rosenfeld,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Pref-MH，一种仅依赖随机二元偏好比较（如人类或模型评判）的精确Metropolis-Hastings采样器，用于从由评判诱导的条件分布中采样。其核心观察是MH的未归一化密度比恰好等于Bradley-Terry选择模型的偏好几率，通过设计有效的接受/拒绝规则保证马尔可夫链收敛到目标分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从满足特定语义属性的条件分布中采样是现代生成建模中的新兴挑战。MH方法虽然提供了条件采样的理论保障，但需要精确的逐点目标密度评估，这在生成式场景中通常不可用。相比之下，由人类或模型"评审"提供的成对比较高度可得且已在多种应用中展现价值。因此需要一种仅利用二元偏好比较即可实现精确条件采样的方法。

Method: Pref-MH利用MH未归一化密度比与Bradley-Terry模型偏好几率相等的关键观察，将MH框架中的密度比计算转化为随机二元偏好比较。针对BT评审只提供采样的二元反馈而非精确比值这一核心挑战，作者设计了一个有效的接受/拒绝规则，使得所得马尔可夫链可证明收敛到目标分布，并证明在固定提议核和预算下，Pref-MH在此类精确可逆接受规则类别中达到Peskun-Tierney意义下的最优性。

Result: 在文本生成和分子设计（使用LLM评审）以及图像生成（使用VLM评审）上的实验表明，Pref-MH在比较反馈相对容易获取的场景下，提供了一种实用且灵活的条件采样方法，且具有理论上的最优性保证。

Conclusion: Pref-MH证明仅依赖随机二元偏好比较即可实现精确的MH条件采样，弥合了MH需要精确密度评估与实际生成场景中只有比较反馈之间的鸿沟，为条件生成提供了一个具有理论保证的通用框架。

Abstract: Sampling from distributions conditioned on desired semantic properties is an emerging challenge in modern generative modeling. Metropolis-Hastings (MH) provides a principled route to conditional sampling, but requires access to exact pointwise target-density evaluations, which are not available in generative settings. Meanwhile, pairwise comparisons by humans or model "judge" are highly accessible and have proved valuable across diverse applications. We introduce Pref-MH, a general exact MH sampler for judge-induced conditional distributions using only stochastic binary pairwise comparisons. Our key observation is that the MH unnormalized density ratio matches the preference odds of the Bradley-Terry (BT) choice model. The central challenge is that while MH requires precise ratio computation, BT judges provide only sampled binary feedback. To this end, we develop a valid accept/reject rule whose resulting Markov chain provably converges to the target distribution. We further show that, for a fixed proposal kernel and budget, Pref-MH is optimal in the Peskun-Tierney sense among this class of exact reversible acceptance rules. Experiments on text generation and molecular design with LLM judges, as well as image generation with VLM judges, demonstrate that Pref-MH provides a practical and flexible approach to conditional sampling when comparative feedback is relatively easy to obtain.

</details>


### [21] [Modelpedia: A Catalog of Model Findings for the Meta-Science of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01090)
*Franciszek Bernat,Dawid Płudowski,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Luca Longo,Jianlong Zhou,Andreas Holzinger,Riccardo Guidotti,Wojciech Samek,Przemysław Biecek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Modelpedia是一个利用LLM自动从论文中提取模型相关发现（findings）并将其与模型、数据集、方法、概念等实体关联，聚合为可检索公共目录的框架。原型系统应用于ICLR 2024和2025论文，提取了超过一千条发现，并对目录本身进行了社区如何研究模型的元分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型相关的科学知识产生速度远超社区整理能力，模型行为与失败的发现散落在数百篇论文、博客和技术报告中，难以检索和系统化组织，形成知识碎片化问题。

Method: 构建自动化的LLM辅助框架Modelpedia，从已发表论文中提取关于模型的发现，将其链接到模型、数据集、方法、概念等相关实体，并将结果聚合成可搜索的公共目录。将该目录本身作为研究对象，进行元分析。

Result: 将原型应用于ICLR 2024和2025接受论文后，提取了超过一千条发现，并通过基于目录的元分析揭示了社区如何研究模型的规律。

Conclusion: 邀请社区探索、贡献并基于开放目录进行建设，将模型发现确立为AI元科学（meta-science of AI）的共享基础。

Abstract: Scientific knowledge about AI models is produced faster than the community can organize it. Every few months a new foundation model reshapes the field and hundreds of papers, blogs, and technical reports document how each behaves or fails. Yet, these findings remain scattered and effectively unretrievable. To address this gap we present Modelpedia, an automated, LLM-assisted framework that extracts findings about models from published papers, links it to the model, dataset, method, and concept it concerns, and aggregates the result into a searchable public catalog. Applying the prototype to accepted ICLR 2024 and 2025 papers, we extract over a thousand findings and, treating the catalog itself as an object of study, run a meta-analysis of how the community investigates models. Now, we invite the community to explore, contribute to, and build on the open catalog, and to help establish model findings as a shared foundation for the meta-science of AI.

</details>


### [22] [Pre-carved Niches: The Formation Dynamics of Modular Task Partitions in Early LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01170)
*Guangqi Li,Yongxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文逐步追踪了从零训练的Pythia-410M模型（两条轨迹：bf16和fp32）的内部模块化组织形成过程。通过在每个训练步骤进行attribution patching，并结合梯度范数、有效更新、权重范数和一阶损失分解等探针，覆盖四个认知领域的14个任务。三个核心发现：模块化图谱在训练前即已预雕（任务对在归因底物上重叠约3.6倍，层0集中度是架构级常数）；任务分区通过两次锐利跳跃"锁定"，其幅度不随学习率调度变化，并伴随梯度层面的"相对剥夺"现象；偏离底物仅出现在当前学习领域，支持模块化追踪学习的假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型展现出类似人脑功能网络的模块化内部组织，但现有研究仅刻画了训练完成后的模型，对模块化组织如何在学习过程中逐步形成一无所知。该论文旨在填补这一空白，从机制层面揭示模块化内部结构的形成过程。

Method: 从零训练两个Pythia-410M模型（bf16和fp32轨迹），在每个训练步骤执行attribution patching，同时部署梯度范数、有效更新、权重范数以及一阶损失分解等探针，在四个认知领域的14个任务上系统性追踪模块化结构的形成。

Result: （1）模块化图谱在训练前即已存在：任务对重叠度达基线3.6倍，层0集中度为架构常数；（2）分区通过两次锐利跳跃锁定，第二次跳跃达20.4σ（安静窗口）/6.2σ（全局），梯度层面存在相对剥夺（胜者梯度供应为败者2.25-2.73倍，偏离随机对照9.5-11.5σ），但这种剥夺未传导至更新或权重；（3）偏离底物仅出现在正在学习的领域，表明模块化与学习过程相关。论文还区分了可辩护的特征层面结论与尚不可解释的机制问题，并为2.8B规模的后续实验预注册了尺度阈值假说。

Conclusion: LLM的模块化组织不是训练过程中"涌现"的，而是架构层面预先刻定的图谱在学习中被逐步激活和锁定。学习过程中的两个关键"锁定跳跃"在梯度层面伴生显著的不平等分配，但这未传递到参数层面。模块化的形成与学习内容高度一致，暗示模块化结构追踪学习任务本身。该工作为理解LLM内部组织的形成机制提供了首个逐步级证据，同时明确划定了当前理论框架的能力边界。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit a modular internal organization that mirrors well-studied functional networks of the human brain, but how this organization forms during training is unknown: prior work has characterized finished models, not the formation process. We track formation step by step: we train a Pythia-410M model from scratch (two trajectories, bf16 and fp32) and run attribution patching at every step, alongside probes for gradient norms, effective updates, weight norms, and first-order loss decomposition across 14 tasks in four cognitive domains. Three findings. First, the modular map is pre-carved: before any learning, the dominant task pair already overlaps at ~3.6x the attribution substrate (a task-independent baseline), and its layer-0 concentration is an architecture-level constant on this model family. Second, the partition locks in through two sharp jumps whose amplitudes do not track the learning-rate schedule (the second reaching 20.4 sigma quiet-window / 6.2 sigma global), accompanied by gradient-level relative deprivation--winners receive 2.25->2.73x the loser's gradient supply, 9.5-11.5 standard deviations below a random control--that does not propagate to updates or weights. Third, deviation from the substrate appears only in the domain being learned, consistent with the hypothesis that modularity tracks learning. We close by separating the feature-level account we can defend from the mechanistic questions we cannot, and we pre-register the scale-threshold hypothesis behind our ongoing 2.8B experiments.

</details>

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过一项涉及147名研究者的数据挑战赛，利用荷兰居民的全人口登记与调查数据预测"三年内生育"这一重大人生事件，发现预测准确度仅中等水平（最佳F1：登记数据0.59，调查数据0.76），先进模型并未超越传统模型，更大规模的登记数据也未优于调查数据；通过模拟受孕与妊娠的随机生物学过程，估计了预测上限（调查F1约0.86-0.94，登记数据0.88-0.96），实际表现未达到该上限，说明数据与方法的不完善以及未建模的随机性共同造成了差距，而该上限本身表明生育中的随机性对预测个体生命结果构成了不可忽视的物理性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重大人生事件（如生育）一直被公认为难以预测，但学界对这一困境的根源缺乏明确认识——究竟是理论不足、数据不全、算法受限，还是事件本身固有的大量随机性所致。作者希望在一个近乎理想的数据与算法条件下，检验预测表现的极限及其制约因素，从而区分"可改进的技术缺口"与"不可逾越的随机性天花板"。

Method: 本研究采用大规模预测竞赛设计：针对2018-2022年间18-45岁荷兰居民的三年内生育结果，147名研究者基于调查数据与全人口登记数据两大类数据源进行预测，方法涵盖从逻辑回归到大语言模型和transformer的多种建模技术。研究者随后构建了受孕与妊娠过程的随机生物学模拟，用以估计在给定人类生理随机性条件下的理论预测上限（F1指标），并将实际竞赛表现与该上限对比归因分析。

Result: 实际预测表现为中等水平（最佳F1：登记数据0.59，调查数据0.76）；先进模型（LLM、transformer）未显著优于经典模型；覆盖全部人口的登记数据未优于抽样调查数据。随机生物学模拟估得的预测上限为调查F1约0.86-0.94、登记数据0.88-0.96，实际表现明显低于该上限，但上限本身远低于完美预测，表明随机因素已设置了一个非平凡的性能边界。

Conclusion: 重大人生事件预测的困难既源于数据与方法的不完善（因此存在改进空间），也源于生育过程固有的随机生物学机制，后者设定了不可逾越的预测上限。这提示社会科学预测领域需要正视随机性带来的根本性限制——即便拥有理想数据与先进算法，"机会"在个体命运中的作用依然构成实质性障碍。研究为预测个体人生结果的可行边界提供了实证基准。

Abstract: Major life events have proven difficult to predict. Does this reflect limits of theory, data, and algorithms, or the large role of chance? We examine one outcome - having a child within three years - through a near-ideal setting for prediction: a data challenge where 147 researchers predicted births for Dutch residents aged 18-45, using survey data and full-population registers. Methods ranged from logistic regression to a large language model and transformers. Predictions were moderately accurate (best F1: register 0.59, survey 0.76); advanced models did not outperform classical ones; and the larger registers did not beat the survey. Simulating the stochastic biology of conception and pregnancy, we estimated a predictive ceiling (survey F1 ~ 0.86-0.94, register 0.88-0.96). Observed performance falls short of this ceiling, implicating imperfect data, methods, and unmodelled chance, while the ceiling itself shows that chance in reproduction alone sets a non-trivial limit on predicting individual lives.

</details>


### [24] [Explore More, Drift Less: Outcome-Only Reinforcement Learning Can Suffice for Long-Horizon Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01245)
*Liming Pu,Xiaoxia Li,Yifu Liu,Teng Cao,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CANOPY（Coverage-ANchored On-PolicY RL），一种极简强化学习协议，通过扩大同任务探索规模直到稀疏结局奖励信号重新出现，并保持所有更新为on-policy、KL锚定且限定于agent自身动作token，解决了小型开放模型在长程交互任务中outcome-only RL的效果瓶颈。在AppWorld基准上，仅通过环境交互训练的Qwen3-14B登上公共排行榜榜首；在SWE-bench Verified上使Qwen3.5-9B提升16.6分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共识认为outcome-only RL在小规模开放模型上很快达到天花板，因此需要稠密奖励、SFT先验、技能库等额外补偿手段。作者认为这一天花板源于两个常见实践上的缺陷：信号饥饿（signal starvation）与策略漂移（policy drift）。前者指组相对RL在稀疏结局奖励下仅在rollout组混合成功与失败时才产生梯度，探索不足会静默地抑制最难最有指导性的任务；后者指在小型任务池上做过多次更新会使策略本身退化，未锚定的目标导致采样分布崩塌。

Method: CANOPY协议包含三个核心要素：(1) 扩大同任务探索规模直至自然信号重新出现（覆盖探索）；(2) 保持每次更新为on-policy、KL锚定，并将梯度限定在agent自身的action token上；(3) 在测试时利用更大的交互预算。该方法不依赖任何任务特定监督、辅助信用信号或复杂的agent脚手架，仅通过环境交互进行训练。

Result: 在AppWorld长程交互编码基准上，用CANOPY训练的Qwen3-14B策略以Test-Normal TGC 86.9和Test-Challenge 67.6的成绩登顶公共排行榜（2026年2月）；同一设计原则使Qwen3.5-9B在SWE-bench Verified上提升16.6分。

Conclusion: 纯agentic RL（仅环境交互训练）可以将长程交互能力直接内化到小型开放模型中，反驳了此前关于outcome-only RL上限的共识；训练栈将开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a natural way to post-train LLM agents for long-horizon interactive tasks judged only by end-of-task verification, yet a shared belief holds that outcome-only RL soon hits a ceiling on small open models. Recent work therefore compensates around the training with denser rewards, SFT priors, skill libraries, curated memory, or multi-agent orchestration. We argue the ceiling is an artifact of two failures of common practice. Signal starvation: group-relative RL with sparse outcome-only rewards yields a gradient only when a task's rollout group mixes successes and failures, so under-scaled exploration silences exactly the hardest, most instructive tasks. Policy drift: squeezing many updates out of a small task pool degrades the policy itself, as an unanchored objective lets the sampling distribution collapse exactly when saturation has already made informative groups rare. We present CANOPY (Coverage-ANchored On-PolicY RL), a minimalist protocol attacking both directly: scale same-task exploration until the natural signal reappears, keep every update on-policy, KL-anchored, and confined to the agent's own action tokens, then cash in an enlarged interaction budget at test time. On AppWorld, a long-horizon interactive coding benchmark, a Qwen3-14B policy trained with CANOPY through environment interaction alone--without task-specific supervision, auxiliary credit signals, or elaborate agent scaffolding--topped the public leaderboard (Feb. 2026; Test-Normal TGC 86.9, Test-Challenge 67.6), and the same design principles lift Qwen3.5-9B on SWE-bench Verified by 16.6 points. Agentic RL alone thus internalizes long-horizon capability directly into a small open model; we plan to release the complete training stack at https://github.com/AlibabaResearch/SignalCoverageRL.

</details>


### [25] [The Constitutional Coverage Trilemma in AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01275)
*Natalija Mitic,Soona Sedahmed A. O.,Mamadou Selly Ly,Moustapha Cisse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对23个前沿LLM的默认宪法（隐含的价值排序）与1,649名美国参与者的需求进行比较审计。发现需求广泛覆盖五大价值观，但供应狭窄且漂移：宪法空间覆盖率仅约2%，37%的用户无价值观匹配，且模型更新中自主性下降、安全性上升；供应漂移方向性地远离本已覆盖不足的价值，恶化最弱势用户。最优修复只需包含诚实与自主的两个顶点菜单，即可将平均后悔降低47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI系统扮演"宪法性机构"角色，每个部署模型编码了对安全、帮助、诚实、自主和公平等价值观的隐式排序。作者关注的核心问题是：前沿模型所"供应"的宪法类型是否覆盖了人类对这些价值观的"需求"？这是一个此前未被系统研究的关键治理问题。

Method: 采用两项研究工具：(1) 对23个前沿LLM原型机在出货默认宪法设置下进行改写控制的审计；(2) 对1,649名美国参与者在同一工具上进行成对权衡研究。结合预算化多元主义三难困境的理论框架进行形式化，并通过保守噪声匹配估计和完整审计精度两种方式进行量化分析。

Result: 需求广泛（最大利益群体低于三分之一）；供应狭窄且漂移（23种原型覆盖面仅约2%的需求空间）；没有原型将帮助或自主放在首位（37%的用户无宪法归属）；六大模型家族中自主性在5/6家族下降、公平性在5/6上升、安全性在4/6上升，且版本趋势单调。2-顶点菜单（诚实+自主）在均值后悔上比完整23原型前沿改善47%；三个顶点新增可将均/最差群组后悔最多降低81%/64%。

Conclusion: 前沿AI的默认宪法供应在价值维度上显著窄于人类需求，并且供应漂移方向性地远离本已覆盖不足的价值（自主性），机械性恶化最弱势用户的福利底限。作者提出最优修复是稀疏的（仅需少量顶点即可大幅降低后悔），并将该发现形式化为预算化多元主义三难困境，验证了结论对距离型福利与退化路由的稳健性。

Abstract: Frontier AI systems function as \emph{constitutional institutions}: each deployed model encodes an implicit ranking among safety, helpfulness, honesty, autonomy, and equity. We ask whether the supply of frontier constitutional types covers human demand. Combining a paraphrase-controlled audit of the as-shipped default constitutions of $23$ frontier LLM archetypes with a pairwise-tradeoff study of $1{,}649$ US participants on the same instrument, we report three facts. \emph{Demand is broad}: it spans all five values, with the largest constituency under one-third. \emph{Supply is narrow and drifting}: the $23$-archetype hull occupies ${\sim}2\%$ of the demand hull under conservative noise-matched estimation ($0.10\%$ at full audit precision), no archetype puts helpfulness or autonomy first ($37\%$ of users are constitutionally homeless), and across six model families autonomy decreases in $5/6$, equity increases in $5/6$, and safety increases in $4/6$, with monotone within-family version trends (order-permutation $p = 0.013$) and the autonomy decline concentrated in scenarios where safety is not at stake. The drift's importance is directional: \emph{away} from a value already undercovered, mechanically worsening the welfare floor for the least-served users. \emph{The fix is sparse}: a $2$-vertex menu $\{e_{\mathrm{HON}}, e_{\mathrm{AUT}}\}$ beats the full $23$-archetype frontier by $47\%$ on mean regret (CI $[43\%, 52\%]$); three vertex additions cut mean/worst-group regret by up to $81\%$/$64\%$. We formalize these findings as a budgeted-pluralism trilemma, show the binding regime is empirically realized, and verify the conclusions are robust to distance-based welfare and to degraded routing. The instrument and audit harness are described in full in the appendices.

</details>


### [26] [Bandits in Prod: Hyperparameter Optimization at Inference Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01335)
*Louis Abraham,Tuan-Anh Nguyen,Nicolas Devatine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IMABO框架，将在线超参数优化（OHPO）形式化为无限多臂老虎机问题，并引入IMOSS策略，在无代表性验证数据的情况下，实现从经典机器学习模型到LLM智能体的配置调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多生产系统只能通过在线请求上的噪声反馈来评估配置，尤其现代智能体系统（如模型选择、检索深度、提示策略、解码温度等推理时选择）缺乏代表性验证数据，使得传统离线超参数优化方法无法适用。

Method: 将OHPO形式化为混合和条件搜索空间上的无限多臂老虎机问题；提出IMABO通用框架，组合任意bandit策略（用于选择已采样配置）与任意oracle（用于提出新配置）；实例化IMOSS重启无关策略，活跃集以t^β速率增长；结合三种oracle：Tree-structured Parzen Estimator、基于逐坐标bandit的incumbent变异oracle、预训练表格基础模型。

Result: 证明了期望累积分位数遗憾界为O(p_ρ^{-1/β} + T^{(1+β)/2})；三种实用oracle均优于均匀随机oracle基线；IMABO在从调优经典机器学习模型到配置基于LLM的智能体等多样OHPO场景中取得最低累积遗憾。

Conclusion: IMABO为在线超参数优化提供了通用且理论保证的解决方案，在缺乏离线验证数据的生产环境中可有效配置现代AI系统。

Abstract: Many production systems can assess a configuration only by using it on live requests and observing noisy feedback. Modern agentic systems are a prominent example, with inference-time choices such as model selection, retrieval depth, prompting strategy, and decoding temperature, yet often with no representative validation data. We formalize this setting as Online Hyperparameter Optimization (OHPO) and cast it as an infinitely many-armed bandit over mixed and conditional search spaces. We introduce IMABO, a general framework that combines any bandit policy for choosing among already sampled configurations with any oracle for proposing new ones. We instantiate it with IMOSS, a restart-free anytime policy whose active set grows as $t^β$, and prove an expected cumulative quantile-regret bound of $O(p_ρ^{-1/β} + T^{(1+β)/2})$, where $β\in(0,1)$ controls active-set growth and $p_ρ$ lower-bounds the probability that a proposed configuration falls in the top-$ρ$ fraction of the search space. We combine IMOSS with three practical oracles: a Tree-structured Parzen Estimator, an incumbent-mutation oracle driven by a per-coordinate bandit, and a pretrained tabular foundation model, all three improving over the uniform random oracle baseline. IMABO obtains the lowest cumulative regret across diverse OHPO settings, from tuning classical machine-learning models to configuring LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [27] [TRIAGE: Three-level Routing and Intelligent Agent Guidance for Efficient Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01428)
*Ruocan Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRIAGE是一个三层路由框架，通过将历史执行轨迹抽象为可复用技能（TaaS），大幅降低LLM Agent的token消耗，在安全监控查询中实现62.3%的token节省，跨领域验证中达到76.3%的削减率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于ReAct范式的LLM Agent虽然展现出强大的工具使用和任务执行能力，但存在根本性的效率问题：每次查询都从零开始完整的推理循环，相似查询重复执行相同步骤，无法利用历史经验积累，导致大量冗余token消耗。

Method: TRIAGE提出三层路由框架：Level 1 Direct Reuse直接复用相同查询，零token消耗；Level 2 Skill Substitution通过TaaS（Trajectory-as-a-Skill）将历史轨迹抽象为技能，对相似查询进行确定性参数替换，零token消耗；Level 3 Full ReAct处理新颖查询并自动保存轨迹供未来复用。同时还提出自动Skill提取机制，将高频轨迹模式蒸馏为确定性技能，形成使用越多效率越高的正反馈循环。

Result: 在1007个安全监控查询上实现62.3%的token节省，其中56.0%查询在Level 2、5.5%在Level 1均零成本执行。跨域验证在ToolBench（15个领域、345个查询）上实现76.3%的token削减。在线学习实验显示前100个查询内L2命中率从0%升至57%，平均token成本从198降至74.7。

Conclusion: TRIAGE通过历史经验复用机制有效解决了ReAct范式的效率瓶颈，证明了语义路由和技能抽象在不同领域具有良好的泛化能力，且具备从冷启动到成熟的自我进化能力，为LLM Agent的效率优化提供了"越用越省"的可持续方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents based on the ReAct paradigm have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in tool use and task execution. However, ReAct suffers from a fundamental efficiency problem: every query triggers a complete reasoning loop from scratch, and similar queries repeat identical steps without leveraging historical experience. We propose TRIAGE,a three-level routing framework that reduces token consumption by reusing historical execution trajectories. Its core innovation is TaaS (Trajectory-as-a-Skill), which abstracts historical execution trajectories into reusable skills, realizing 'experience as a service'. TRIAGE classifies queries into three levels: (1) Direct Reuse-identical queries, 0 tokens; (2) Skill Substitution-similar queries, 0 tokens via deterministic parameter substitution; (3) Full ReAct-novel queries, automatically stored for future reuse. In large-scale experiments on 1,007 security monitoring queries, TRIAGE achieves 62.3% token savings, with 56.0% of queries at Level 2 and 5.5% at Level 1, both executing at zero cost. Cross-domain validation on ToolBench (15 domains, 345 queries) achieves 76.3% token reduction, confirming the generalizability of semantic routing. An online learning experiment demonstrates cold-start-to-mature evolution: the L2 hit rate rises from 0% to 57% within the first 100 queries, and the average token cost drops from 198 to 74.7. We also propose an automatic Skill extraction mechanism that distills high-frequency trajectory patterns into deterministic Skills, creating a positive feedback loop of 'the more you use it, the more efficient it becomes'.

</details>


### [28] [Efficiently Estimating Optimal Hyperparameter Scaling Laws through Power-Law Entropy Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01431)
*Zhiliang Chen,Sebastian Ament,David Eriksson,Maximilian Balandat,Eytan Bakshy,Jihao Andreas Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Power-Law Entropy Search (PLES)，一种基于多保真贝叶斯优化的计算成本感知采集函数，用于高效估计大语言模型训练的最优超参数缩放律，相比传统网格搜索可节省超过90%的计算预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计最优超参数缩放律传统上需要对数千次训练运行进行穷举网格搜索，消耗大量计算资源。研究者希望找到一种更高效的方法，能够在生产规模的LLM训练中预测最优配置而不需要昂贵的大规模调参。

Method: PLES是一种计算成本感知的采集函数，基于多保真贝叶斯优化。其核心创新在于它搜索的是能够降低缩放律整体估计不确定性的候选配置（而非优化单一目标函数）。在每次迭代中，PLES选择每个单位计算成本下能最大程度降低缩放律估计不确定性的候选配置，自然倾向于选择信息量大的小规模实验。

Result: PLES在合成基准测试、基于真实LLM训练数据拟合的代理模型以及实际LLM预训练运行三个层面上均进行了评估。在所有设置下，PLES以不到传统网格搜索和其他基线方法十分之一的计算预算即可收敛到精确的最优超参数缩放律。

Conclusion: PLES证明了通过将计算成本纳入贝叶斯优化的采集函数设计，并用信息论准则（降低缩放律估计不确定性）替代单一目标优化，可以极其高效地估计LLM的最优超参数缩放律，大幅减少计算开销。

Abstract: Optimal hyperparameter scaling laws describe how the best hyperparameters for large language model (LLM) training change with model and data scale, enabling practitioners to predict optimal configurations at production scales without expensive large-scale tuning. However, estimating these scaling laws conventionally requires exhaustive grid searches over thousands of training runs, consuming enormous computational resources. We introduce Power-Law Entropy Search (PLES), a computational cost-aware acquisition function built on multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization that efficiently estimates optimal hyperparameter scaling laws through adaptive experimentation. A key innovation in PLES is that it searches for candidates that reduce the overall uncertainty of a scaling law estimate, instead of optimizing a single objective function. At each iteration, PLES selects the candidate configuration that maximally reduces the uncertainty of the scaling law estimates per unit computational cost, naturally favoring informative small-scale experiments. We evaluate PLES on synthetic benchmarks, surrogate models fitted to real LLM training data, and actual LLM pre-training runs. Across all settings, PLES converges to accurate optimal hyperparameter scaling laws using less than one-tenth of the computational budget required by conventional grid search and other baselines.

</details>


### [29] [Rethinking Learnability in Offline Data-driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01493)
*Chao Qian,Chen-Guang Wang,Rong-Xi Tan,Ke Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出算法依赖可学习性（algorithm-dependent learnability）的概念，证明其在离散和连续优化设定下的充分性，并在其启发下提出轨迹学习框架及UGTL方法，在Design-Bench基准上取得最优聚合排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线数据驱动优化只需固定评估集即可找到高质量解，但一个根本问题尚待回答：离线优化需要怎样的充分可学习性？先前理论表明PAC可学习性不充分，因为即使大部分区域学习良好，最优区域仍可能未被充分学习。

Method: 本文提出算法依赖可学习性，仅要求优化器轨迹上的精度足够。证明了其值查询形式对代表性离散设定（如子模最大化的贪心和局部搜索）充分，其一阶形式对凸最小化的投影梯度下降充分。据此形式化了包含轨迹构建、轨迹建模和候选生成的轨迹学习框架，并提出UGTL，使用条件扩散对局部一致的改进轨迹建模，并选择多样化候选集。

Result: 在五个Design-Bench任务上，UGTL在25种方法中取得了最优聚合平均排名3.1/25。受控轨迹分析和跨架构替换实验确认了轨迹构建对性能提升的重要作用。

Conclusion: 算法依赖可学习性从理论上弥补了PAC可学习性在离线优化中的不足，轨迹学习框架及UGTL方法在实践中验证了这一理论洞见的有效性。

Abstract: Black-Box Optimization (BBO) has found broad applications, but evolutionary algorithms and Bayesian optimization face efficiency challenges as real-world BBO problems grow increasingly complex. Data-driven optimization improves the efficiency of BBO algorithms by learning from data. Offline data-driven optimization seeks high-quality solutions using only a fixed set of previous evaluations, attracting substantial attention because it requires no additional online evaluations. Many offline optimization methods have been proposed, but a fundamental question remains unanswered: what learnability is sufficient for offline optimization? Prior theoretical studies show that Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learnability is insufficient, as the optimal region may remain poorly learned even when most regions are well learned. In this paper, we propose algorithm-dependent learnability, which requires accuracy only on the optimizer's trajectory. We prove that its value-query form is sufficient for representative discrete settings, including greedy and local search for submodular maximization, while its first-order analogue is sufficient for projected gradient descent on convex minimization. Motivated by this notion, we formalize a trajectory-learning framework comprising trajectory construction, trajectory modeling, and candidate generation, and analyze existing trajectory-based methods under it. We further propose Uncertainty-aware Gradient-guided Trajectory Learning (UGTL), which constructs locally coherent improvement trajectories reflecting plausible search paths, models them with conditional diffusion, and selects a diverse candidate set. On five Design-Bench tasks, UGTL achieves the best aggregate mean rank, $3.1/25$, among 25 methods. Controlled trajectory analyses and cross-architecture replacements confirm that our trajectory construction plays a significant role in the improvement.

</details>


### [30] [Retrieved but not ranked: surface-form bias in structural retrieval, from mathematics to agent trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01556)
*Nabira Rashid,Manolis Kellis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在两个无关领域（竞赛数学和具身智能体轨迹）中系统评估了"表面形式与语义被刻意分离"的嵌入检索任务。结果显示生产级嵌入器在数学"重度伪装"下strict Hit@1为0.0%，在需不同对象的轨迹任务中接近超几何随机水平，说明检索锚定于字面token而非任务结构。词法重排器在数学中有害、在轨迹中有益，可作为判断表面变异是"对抗性"还是"偶然性"的指示器；LLM重排器虽在多数场景中恢复部分差距，但数学领域的恢复部分源于记忆（尤其知名竞赛），且下游求解实验中oracle检索与对抗性差检索无显著差异，表明当前嵌入检索能力无法转化为下游任务提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入检索评估通常在表面形式与语义一致的情况下进行，未能考察当两者被刻意撕裂时（即检索共享底层结构但措辞不同的条目），嵌入模型是否真正理解语义结构而非仅依赖词面匹配。作者在两个截然不同的领域建立统一协议来揭示这一缺陷。

Method: 建立了两个领域的数据集：竞赛数学（MathNet-Retrieve，500查询、117,088条目）和ALFWorld衍生的具身智能体轨迹（118查询、336轨迹）。在数学中设置多级"伪装"（disguise tiers），评估严格Hit@1、top-10命中率以及"胜者比正确答案与查询词面更相似"的失误比例；在轨迹中要求检索涉及不同对象或不同对象+容器的金标准轨迹。对照实验包括词法重排器、LLM重排器和三个评判者（judges），并进行配对下游求解实验（210个查询，评分者一致率96-99%），采用McNemar检验和bootstrap置信区间。

Result: 数学重伪装等级下两个生产嵌入器strict Hit@1均为0.0%（bootstrap CI [0.0,0.0]），但正确答案几乎总在top-10中；95.2-99.8%的失误中胜者在词面上更接近查询。轨迹任务中，当金标准需不同对象时模型接近或低于超几何随机水平；需对象+容器均不同时三个嵌入器均低于随机水平。词法重排在数学中有害、在轨迹中恢复26-36%差距；LLM重排器在数学中恢复5-63%的差距、轨迹中恢复43-76%，21个单元格方向一致但效应量因领域而异。数学收益集中于知名竞赛（+19.8点），表明部分恢复来自记忆。下游实验中oracle检索与对抗性差检索无显著差异（McNemar p=0.678），求解器69.5%的零样本准确率本质上是截断代理（完成答案上97-100%）。

Conclusion: 当前生产级嵌入模型在需要"理解语义结构而非词面匹配"的检索任务中系统性失败：数学检索完全崩溃（深度伪装下命中率为0），轨迹检索跌至随机水平。词法重排性能的符号可以作为基准表面变异性质（对抗性vs偶然性）的诊断指标。LLM重排的恢复能力有限且部分来自训练数据记忆而非真正的结构理解。更关键的是，即使oracle检索也未能提升下游求解性能，说明当前基准的表面形式与任务能力之间存在根本脱节，嵌入检索能力的改进可能不直接转化为下游任务收益。

Abstract: We evaluate embedding retrieval where surface form and meaning are pulled apart on purpose: retrieving items that share underlying structure but not wording, in two unrelated domains under one protocol, competition mathematics (MathNet-Retrieve; 500 queries, 117,088-item corpus) and embodied-agent trajectories (ALFWorld-derived; 118 queries, 336 trajectories). In mathematics the failure is complete: strict Hit@1 at the heaviest disguise tier is 0.0% for both production embedders (bootstrap 95% CI [0.0, 0.0]) while the correct item sits in the top 10 nearly always, and in 95.2 to 99.8% of misses the winner is more lexically similar to the query than the correct answer. In trajectories, where surface variation is incidental, the same models land at or near hypergeometric chance when gold must involve a different object, and below chance for all three embedders once gold must differ in object and receptacle: retrieval anchors on literal tokens, not task structure. A lexical reranker control hurts in mathematics and helps in trajectories (closing 26 to 36% of the gap, CIs excluding zero); its sign reveals whether a benchmark's surface variation is adversarial or incidental. An LLM reranker recovers 5 to 63% of the gap in mathematics and 43 to 76% in trajectories; direction replicates across three judges (all 21 cells positive), but effect sizes, tier profiles, and the outlier judge change with domain (paired differences excluding zero everywhere). Mathematics gains concentrate on well-known competitions (+19.8 points, CI [+6.7, +33.2], one of six cells), so part of the recovery is memorization. In a paired downstream experiment (210 queries, graders at 96 to 99% agreement), oracle retrieval was indistinguishable from adversarially bad retrieval (McNemar p = 0.678); the solver's 69.5% zero-shot accuracy is largely a truncation proxy (97 to 100% on finished answers), leaving no headroom.

</details>
