### [31] [Long-Horizon State Tracking in LLMs: Executing MD5 through a Deep Sequence of Dependent Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00012)
*Dheeraj Mohandas Pai,Lu Xian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过让LLM逐步计算MD5哈希（196次依赖工具调用，64轮，携带4个32位字）来干净地测试其长程状态追踪能力。结果显示gpt-oss-120b在温度0下能在多数完成运行中正确携带全部状态并输出正确摘要，且两个决定成败的因素是保留模型自身推理在上下文中以及对启用思考的worker进行投票以消除算术错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估中长视野任务稀少，因为逐步累积的错误级联导致端到端失败率随长度急剧上升。现有agentic基准报告端到端成功但混淆了状态追踪与指令理解，缺乏隔离状态追踪的对照组，且易受捷径影响（如幻觉的最终答案）。是否LLM能跨多次工具调用携带精确中间状态本身未得到充分证实。

Method: 让模型逐步计算MD5（RFC 1321从零实现），生成196个依赖工具调用、64轮，模型需在上下文中携带四个32位字(a,b,c,d)从一次调用到下一次。每次调用与ground-truth轨迹逐位对齐并检查摘要。最强设置中用第二个LLM替换每个原始工具，driver和worker从零计算整个哈希而无精确算术oracle。关键变量：是否保留模型自身推理在上下文中，以及对启用思考的worker进行投票。

Result: gpt-oss-120b（混合专家模型，每个token约5.5B活跃参数）在温度0和短固定提示下，能在多数完成的运行中跨越全部196次调用携带完整状态并返回正确摘要。保留模型自身推理以及投票消除算术错误是决定成功的关键；残余失败按其来源分类为状态携带、算术和服务问题。

Conclusion: LLM能够跨极长工具调用序列精确携带中间状态，纯粹的簿记/状态追踪能力可以被干净地测试和评估。保留推理上下文与投票策略不改变权重即可显著提升成功率。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks remain uncommon in large language model (LLM) evaluation, and for a reason: when each step depends on the last, per-step accuracy that looks excellent in isolation decays catastrophically, as errors cascade and the end-to-end failure probability grows sharply with length. Existing agentic benchmarks report end-to-end success but confound this state-tracking difficulty with instruction interpretation, give no control group that isolates it, and are vulnerable to shortcuts such as a hallucinated final answer, so they cannot say why a long run fails. Whether an LLM can carry exact intermediate state across many tool calls at all is itself not well established. We test this cleanly by having the model compute a cryptographic hash, MD5, step by step: a sequence of $196$ dependent tool calls over $64$ rounds while it carries four $32$-bit words $(a,b,c,d)$ in its own context from one call to the next. Interpretation is trivial and, because we implement MD5 from scratch (RFC~1321), we align every call to the ground-truth trace and check the digest to the bit, so any failure is pure bookkeeping. gpt-oss-120b, a mixture-of-experts model with only $\sim$5.5B active parameters per token, at temperature $0$ with a short fixed prompt, carries the full state across all $196$ calls and returns the correct digest on a majority of completed runs. In the strongest setting we replace every primitive tool with a second LLM, so a driver and a worker compute the whole hash from scratch with no exact-arithmetic oracle in the loop. Two ingredients decide success and neither changes the weights: keeping the model's own reasoning in its context each turn, and voting over a thinking-enabled worker to remove its modular-arithmetic slips. We localize the residual failures by origin, separating state-carrying from arithmetic and from serving.

</details>


### [32] [OpenAgentFlow: Enabling System-Wide Safety Boundaries for Heterogeneous AI Agent Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00015)
*Dongsheng Chen,Xiangyu Zhao,Xin Yao,Xuetao Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OpenAgentFlow，一种控制平面/动作平面架构，通过统一的AgentEvent流和策略执行点（PEP）在动作提交边界强制执行AI代理系统中的安全治理，支持跨GUI、API、工具调用和LLM生成调用的统一策略管理与审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的AI代理正从孤立助手演变为异构系统（多个代理、规划器、控制器和多个执行后端共享同一环境）。现有安全防护对提示词、工具调用、GUI动作和代理本地行为各自设防，导致：1）执行分散碎片化；2）跨多步动作流中出现的风险难以察觉；3）对可审计性和策略演化的支持有限。安全需要成为系统级的动作治理问题，即在动作提交对共享状态产生修改前做出决策。

Method: OpenAgentFlow采用控制平面/动作平面分离架构：1）将待执行的GUI动作、API调用、工具调用和LLM生成的调用标准化为统一的AgentEvent流；2）每个事件通过共享的执行前策略执行点（Policy Enforcement Point）；3）控制平面维护来源追踪、会话状态、审计记录和可更新策略。这样创建了可治理的共享动作流，允许新规则即时生效而无需修改代理、提示词、模型或执行路径。系统在Android上完成实例化。

Result: 在300例动作事件基准上达到94.0%准确率和95.3%攻击拦截率；在30例动态策略套件中，安装新规则后在27例匹配预期行为；在100例Android模拟器套件的98例追踪用例中，GUI、API和LLM规划场景下原始准确率90.8%，追踪调整通过率92.9%。

Conclusion: 实验表明OpenAgentFlow为异构AI代理集群提供了实用且统一的共享执行边界，有效解决系统级动作治理中的碎片化、审计性和策略演化问题。

Abstract: AI agents powered by large language models are evolving from isolated assistants into heterogeneous systems in which multiple agents, planners, controllers, and execution backends operate over the same user or enterprise environment. In such settings, safety becomes a system-level action-governance problem: deciding whether concrete agent-generated actions should be committed before they modify shared state. Existing safeguards cover prompts, tool calls, GUI actions, and agent-local behavior, but often leave enforcement fragmented, obscure risks that emerge across multi-step action flows, and provide limited support for auditability and policy evolution.
  We present OpenAgentFlow, a control-plane/action-plane architecture that enforces safety at the action-commit boundary. It normalizes pending GUI actions, API calls, tool calls, and LLM-generated invocations into a unified AgentEvent stream, routes each event through a shared pre-execution Policy Enforcement Point, and maintains provenance, session state, audit records, and updatable policies in the control plane. This creates a shared governable action stream and allows new rules to take effect without modifying agents, prompts, models, or execution paths.
  We instantiate OpenAgentFlow on Android. On a 300-case action-event benchmark, it achieves 94.0% accuracy and a 95.3% attack block rate. On a 30-case dynamic-policy suite, it matches expected behavior in 27 cases after new rules are installed. Across 98 traced cases from a 100-case Android emulator suite, it achieves 90.8% raw accuracy and a 92.9% trace-adjusted pass rate across GUI, API, and LLM-planned cases. These results show that OpenAgentFlow provides a practical shared enforcement boundary for heterogeneous AI agent fleets.

</details>


### [33] [SCAFFOLD: A Large-Scale Structured Dataset of Computer Science Research Figures with Diagram QA and Chain-of-Thought Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00018)
*Ranjit Raut,Aarav Subedi,Sagun Rai,Sudan Jha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCAFFOLD，一个大规模计算机科学研究图表的结构化数据集，包含图像、标题、上下文、问答对和思维链推理痕迹，共有SCAFFOLD-157K、-37K和-12K三个规模版本，用于训练视觉语言模型理解CS论文中的图表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学论文高度依赖图表（如架构图、系统流程图、流水线示意图），这些图表通常承载着比文字更多的信息。但目前没有公开数据集将这类特定图形与标题、上下文、问题和逐步推理配对，而这正是训练视觉语言模型理解它们所需的关键数据资源。

Method: 从arXiv计算机科学论文中构建数据集，使用版面检测（layout detection）和PDF解析准备数据，配合AI辅助的问题生成步骤，构建了（图像、标题、上下文、问答、思维链）五元组数据集。数据集按规模分为三个版本：SCAFFOLD-157K（3,058篇论文、29,887张图、157,387对）、SCAFFOLD-37K（36,797对）和SCAFFOLD-12K（12,000对）。

Result: 构建了包含三个规模版本的结构化数据集SCAFFOLD，其中最大版本覆盖3,058篇论文和29,887张图表。使用SCAFFOLD-12K在Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct上进行了基线实验。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD填补了CS研究图表理解领域缺乏配对训练数据的空白，提供了包含问答和思维链推理的多规模数据集，为训练视觉语言模型理解计算机科学论文图表奠定了基础。

Abstract: Computer science papers rely heavily on diagrams: architecture drawings, system flowcharts, and pipeline schematics that often carry more information than the text around them. There is currently no public dataset that pairs this specific kind of figure with captions, context, questions, answers, and step-by-step reasoning, which is exactly what is needed to train a vision-language model to understand them. We present \textbf{SCAFFOLD}\footnote{https://github.com/theranjitraut/scaffold}, a large-scale structured dataset of computer science research figures with diagram QA and Chain-of-Thought reasoning traces. This dataset consists of (image, caption, context, question-answer, chain-of-thought) tuples from arXiv computer science papers prepared using layout detection and PDF parsing, with an AI-assisted question-generation step. The resulting large-sized SCAFFOLD-157K dataset spans 3,058 papers with 29,887 figures (157,387 pairs), a medium-sized SCAFFOLD-37K dataset (36,797 pairs), and a small-sized SCAFFOLD-12K dataset (12,000 pairs). We used SCAFFOLD-12K for baseline experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct.

</details>


### [34] [LLM-Driven Autonomous Vehicles Inherit Human Driver Biases in Pedestrian Yielding: Results and Implications From A New Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00192)
*Irem Yoldas,Martim Brandão,Jie Zhang,Odinaldo Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两种新的偏差测试方法（"All Else Being Equal"测试和"Self-Consistency"测试），用于评估LLM和VLM在自动驾驶行人让行决策中的偏见，发现模型决策受行人性别、种族、宗教、残疾、年龄、肤色和社会经济地位等因素影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公众对自动驾驶的信任不仅取决于技术成功率，还取决于其决策的公平性。心理学研究表明人类驾驶员存在偏见（例如对美国黑人行人让行率更低），但基于"常识"模型的AV决策是否继承了这些人类偏见尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出两种偏差测试方法：1) "All Else Being Equal"测试——控制其他变量、仅改变单一敏感属性来比较模型决策；2) "Self-Consistency"测试——评估模型对同一场景在不同表述下的决策一致性。将这些方法应用于LLM和VLM在行人让行决策任务中的评估。

Result: 研究发现LLM和VLM的让行决策均受到行人性别、种族、宗教、残疾、年龄、肤色和社会经济地位等因素的影响。不同模型之间存在偏差类型和程度差异，但存在共同的偏差模式。

Conclusion: 研究结果对"常识"模型范式提出质疑，强调需要修订该范式或解决下游偏差问题；模型偏差评估应当纳入自动驾驶评估体系的一部分。

Abstract: Public trust in Autonomous Vehicles (AVs) may depend not only on technical success but also on the fairness of their decision making. While a recent trend in AV research involves using general purpose "common sense" models to guide AV decision making, the degree to which these inherit human biases in driving is still understudied. Given that psychology studies have shown human driver biases exist, such as lower pedestrian-yielding rates to Black pedestrians in the US, we argue that analyses of model bias should also be part of AV evaluation. Concretely, in this paper we propose two new bias testing methodologies for Large Language Models (LLMs) and Visual-Language Models (VLMs)-"All Else Being Equal" tests and "Self-Consistency" tests-in order to assess bias in pedestrian-yielding decisions. Our findings show that both LLMs and VLMs make yielding decisions which are influenced by pedestrian gender, ethnicity, religion, disability, age, skin tone and socio-economic status. While the type and degree of bias is different from model to model, we highlight common patterns-and raise questions about the "common sense" model paradigm, particularly the need to either revise the paradigm or address issues of downstream bias.

</details>


### [35] [Learning What to Retain: Gated-Memory Routing for Efficient Collaboration in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00237)
*Rakibul Hasan Rajib,Mengxing Zheng,Qian Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Gated-Memory Routing框架，通过门控记忆写入门和检索门为LLM多智能体系统提供紧凑、非冗余的协作状态，从而在不产生执行历史过载的情况下实现自适应编排，在五个基准上实现了最优平均准确率并显著降低了推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统的编排需要适应不断演化的协作状态。仅基于查询的路由无法利用中间进度或错误信息，损害准确性；而基于完整执行历史的路由虽提供了必要上下文，但后续决策必须处理所有先验步骤（含冗余/低效用步骤），导致"执行历史过载"推高成本。因此需要一种紧凑状态来捕获有用进度而不累积冗余上下文。

Method: 提出Gated-Memory Routing：1) 学习的Memory Write Gate仅提交非冗余推理步骤到执行记忆；2) 学习的Retrieval Gate为每个agent提供紧凑且相关的记忆子集；3) 每步基于该记忆选择下一个角色和骨干模型；4) Adaptive Halting Controller在记忆包含足够证据时停止执行。

Result: 在五个推理和代码生成基准上：获得最优平均准确率，超过最强基线2.44个百分点；在HumanEval上将推理成本相比基线降低31.9%。

Conclusion: 通过门控记忆机制而非完整历史或仅查询来条件化编排决策，能够同时实现有效性和高效性——既提升准确率又降低推理成本，验证了紧凑且信息丰富的协作状态对多智能体系统编排的重要性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems tackle complex reasoning by orchestrating how multiple agents are configured and how they collaborate. A central challenge is to adapt orchestration to the evolving collaboration state. Routing from the query alone cannot adapt to intermediate progress or errors, which hurts accuracy. Routing from the complete execution history supplies this missing context, but forces later decisions to process every prior step, including redundant or low-utility ones. This creates an execution-history overload that inflates cost. Effective orchestration instead requires a compact state that captures useful progress without accumulating redundant context. We propose Gated-Memory Routing, which conditions each decision on the query and a learned execution memory. A learned Memory Write Gate commits only non-redundant reasoning steps, and a learned Retrieval Gate supplies each agent a compact, relevant subset, so every decision conditions on a clean, informative state. At each step, the system selects the next role and backbone from this memory, while an Adaptive Halting Controller stops execution once the memory contains sufficient evidence for answering. Across five reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, our framework is both effective and efficient: it attains the best average accuracy, exceeding the strongest baseline by 2.44 points, while reducing HumanEval inference cost by 31.9% relative to that baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/rajibrhasan/gated-memory-routing

</details>


### [36] [Invalidation Contracts for Cross-Episode Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00243)
*Michael Wu,Arquimedes Canedo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"失效契约"(invalidation contracts)协议层，为LLM agent的API错误恢复建议附加版本戳和可缓存性提示，使客户端在服务端数据漂移后无需试错即可逐行淘汰过期缓存，将节省分解为有效性(validity)和合规性(compliance)两个独立因素，在约9,400个episode上验证了逐行失效相较表级失效的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent缓存API错误恢复建议以避免重复推导，但服务端数据漂移使缓存修复变成静默失败，而每次episode都重新推导则丧失缓存节省的收益。现有方案无法在不重新推导的情况下区分过期与仍有效的缓存条目。

Method: 引入失效契约协议层：为每条恢复建议附加版本戳和可缓存性提示；将实现节省分解为两个独立因子——有效性(协议决定、与厂商无关)和合规性(规划器模型决定)；评估跨7个模型、3条服务路径、2个领域、约9,400个episode，对比逐行(row-level)与表级(table-level)失效策略。

Result: 逐行失效在7个模型上将合规性提高0至66.7个百分点(其中3个模型提升55.6至66.7)，在7个模型中的4个上恢复29-33%的基线token成本；表级失效则破坏共存条目并将5个模型的漂移后首次尝试率降至0%；逐行粒度下每个模型的驱逐精确率为1.00；协议增加15%响应负载；版本戳有效性在所有模型和路径上产生一致结果，零契约失败。

Conclusion: 失效契约以极低的协议开销(15%负载增幅)实现了精确、确定性的缓存失效，且发现规划器模型的输入模式保守性(input-schema conservatism)是合规性的关键分歧来源——Haiku 4.5与Sonnet 5在此维度表现差异巨大，表明协议设计和模型选择需协同考量。

Abstract: LLM agents that cache recovery suggestions from API errors can skip re-derivation in later episodes, spending fewer tokens and fewer model calls on constraints they have already learned. Server-side data drift turns those cached fixes into silent failures, and the usual remedy, re-deriving on every episode, gives the savings back. We introduce invalidation contracts, a protocol layer that attaches version stamps and cacheability hints to every recovery suggestion so the client can evict stale entries without trial and error, and keep the rest. The contract decomposes realized savings into two independent factors: validity, the fraction of cached suggestions that remain correct after a drift event, and compliance, the fraction the planner applies on the first attempt. Validity depends only on the protocol and is vendor-independent. Compliance depends on the planner model: identical wire bytes yield 100% first-try compliance on Claude Haiku 4.5 and 11% or below on Claude Sonnet 5, which exhibits input-schema conservatism, refusing fixes that add fields the original request did not contain. We evaluate across seven models, three serving paths, two domains, and approximately 9,400 episodes. Row-level invalidation raises compliance by 0 to 66.7 percentage points across the seven models, 55.6 to 66.7 on three, and recovers 29-33% of baseline token cost on four of seven models, while table-level invalidation destroys co-located entries and drops post-drift first-try rates to 0% on five of seven. Eviction precision is 1.00 at row granularity on every model under the row-level oracle of Section 4.1. The contract adds 15% to response payload. Version-stamp validity is deterministic by construction and produced identical results across every model and serving path, with zero contract failures in the entire evaluation.

</details>


### [37] [Hypotheses-Guided Self Distillation for Continual Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00251)
*EunJeong Hwang,Kushan Mitra,Dan Zhang,Hannah Kim,Estevam Hruschka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HypReflect是一个可持续个性化框架，通过从多样化用户信号中推断显式、带有不确定性感知的偏好假设，并随着新证据积累进行反思性修正，再通过假设引导的自蒸馏整合用户模型，在在线个性化、多会话交互和隐式行为信号三个场景中优于基线方法，并展现出对未见用户和跨域设置的强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在与LLM助手长期交互中需要持续适应个人偏好，但偏好通常不会完整陈述，而是通过异构、潜在且有噪声的信号浮现。现有方法依赖原始交互历史或昂贵的基于奖励的优化来管理个性化，缺乏可靠且可扩展的持续个性化方案。

Method: HypReflect包含三个核心步骤：(1)从多样的用户信号中推断显式且带有不确定性感知的偏好假设；(2)随着新证据积累，对这些假设进行反思性细化修正；(3)通过假设引导的自蒸馏将所得用户模型融入系统。该框架避免了对原始交互历史的直接依赖和基于奖励的昂贵优化。

Result: 在在线个性化、多会话交互和隐式行为信号三个个性化设置中，HypReflect均优于包括原始历史方法和增量更新方法在内的多种基线。此外，在未见用户和跨域场景中表现出强泛化能力，同时在不同上下文预算下保持稳定性，支持可复用假设并实现更聚焦的个性化。

Conclusion: 通过显式且可修订的用户偏好假设，HypReflect为可靠且可扩展的持续个性化迈出了一步，表明基于假设建模的方法相比直接使用原始历史或昂贵的奖励优化更具优势。

Abstract: As people increasingly interact with LLM assistants in daily life, continually adapting to individual preferences has become essential for effective long-term interactions. However, user preferences are rarely stated in full, and instead emerge through heterogeneous, latent, and noisy signals, with existing methods relying on raw interaction histories or costly reward-based optimization to manage personalization. We introduce HypReflect, a reliable, scalable framework for continual personalization that infers explicit, uncertainty-aware preference hypotheses from diverse user signals, reflectively refines them as new evidence accumulates, and incorporates the resulting user model through hypotheses-guided self-distillation. Experiments across three personalization settings: online personalization, multi-session interactions, and implicit behavioral signals, show that HypReflect outperforms a range of baselines, including raw-history and incremental-update methods. We further demonstrate strong generalization to unseen users and cross-domain settings, along with stability across context budgets, reusable hypotheses, and more focused personalization. These results suggest a step towards reliable and scalable continual personalization through explicit, revisable user preference hypotheses.

</details>


### [38] [The Answer Is Not the Argument](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00264)
*Will Yeadon,Sergio Juárez,Paul Mackay,T. J. Dowling,Elise Agra,Oto-obong Inyang,Arin Mizouri,Craig P. Testrow*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过237个带步骤编号的物理问题解答，研究给AI监督者（monitor）提供参考答案是否真正提升推理验证能力，还是仅帮助识别错误结论。结果显示，答案访问主要改善"结论一致性检查"而非"论证过程的独立验证"，并且产出可接受答案并不保证推理过程健全——这是良性奖励攻击（reward hacking）类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维（chain-of-thought）监控被提出用于AI监督，但现有评估往往给监控者提供可信参考答案。作者质疑：这种答案访问是否真的提升了推理验证能力，还是仅仅暴露了错误结论？这一问题对AI安全评估的有效性至关重要。

Method: 作者收集了3个前沿模型对79道Humanity's Last Exam物理题的237个带步骤编号的解答（无插入错误），独立标注最终答案正确性与首个错误步骤。参照标准结合物理学家注释、独立LLM辩论和源掩蔽裁决。从中识别出24条"关键轨迹"（答案正确但推理含真错误）。随后8个LLM监控者在三种条件下评估轨迹：盲审、提供未经核实或已核实答案、盲承诺后给出答案。

Result: 认证（certification）使平均平衡准确率从0.637提升至0.796，首个错误定位精确率从0.261提升至0.379。对错误答案轨迹，认证将召回率从0.653提升至0.951；但对关键轨迹（答案正确但有错误推理），召回率反而从0.521降至0.438，且8个监控者方向一致（bootstrap 95% CI [+0.256,+0.506]）。盲承诺后，监控者在看到答案后对新标记错误轨迹的识别率为93.8%，而关键轨迹仅为18.0%。

Conclusion: 答案访问主要改善结论一致性检查而非对论证过程的独立验证。这对AI安全有重要警示：可接受输出不能证明产生它的过程健全。虽然本研究的错误是普通且大多非承重的而非对抗性的，但依赖可信答案的评估可能高估监控能力——当可接受输出掩盖了不健全推理时尤其如此。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought monitoring is proposed for AI oversight, yet evaluations often provide monitors with a trusted reference answer. We ask whether answer access improves reasoning verification or mainly exposes incorrect conclusions. We collected 237 step-numbered solutions to 79 Humanity's Last Exam physics questions from three frontier models, with no inserted errors, and independently labelled final-answer correctness and the first false step. The reference standard combined physicist annotations, an independent LLM debate, and source-masked adjudication. This yielded 24 critical traces in which the answer was correct but the trace contained a genuine error. 8 LLM monitors evaluated traces blind, with an unverified or certified answer, or after a blind commitment. Result: 该系统已自2026年4月起在美国一家大型消费者服务平台的生产环境中部署，覆盖132个职业。

Abstract: Two-sided service marketplaces are moving from deterministic request-form intake to AI-native probabilistic matching, enabled by large language models (LLMs) that infer intent, preferences, and latent constraints from natural language. Relying on inferred intent rather than fixed-form fields forces these platforms to regenerate the provider-side preference taxonomy underwriting matching, search, and pricing: attributes interpretable to service providers while remaining a useful signal for marketplace decisions. We present an autoresearch loop that generates this taxonomy, one occupation at a time, and has been deployed in production at a major U.S. consumer services marketplace since April 2026, spanning 132 occupations. Instead of one global hierarchy, the loop treats each occupation as an independent generation problem and runs iterative propose-evaluate-keep refinement cycles. Each candidate tag set is scored by a recalibrated six-rubric LLM-as-judge framework, and a 7-critic panel of distinct personas contributes weighted penalties to an adjusted score, with no hard vetoes. A separate parity-mapping stage maps legacy request-form Q&A pairs back to the generated taxonomy, yielding both a coverage signal and an interface for human quality assurance; it does so by first inferring the provider attribute each legacy question was meant to measure, rather than translating questions to tags literally.

*Bardia Mohammadi,Laurent Bindschaedler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"不可逆性预算"（irreversibility budget）机制，作为多代理LLM集群的累计风险控制方案。与逐一检查效果的现有控制不同，该预算由可信运行时为每个主体维护跨代理、工作流和租户的残余价值风险账户；每次效果按其残余损失收费，一旦集群级聚合将超支则拒绝该边际效果。实验表明，在逐效果门控允许集群级超支高达租户风险限额48倍的情况下，预算机制能将所有正确收费的运行保持在限额内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理集群正在执行无法完全撤销的外部效应（如转移资金、部署代码、删除数据、披露信息），而当前控制机制每次只检查一个效果。这导致一种安全漏洞：每个独立被授权的代理在共享触发器下可以超额消耗主体的风险预算，即使每个局部门控都保持正确。需要一种集群级、跨代理的风险聚合控制机制。

Method: 提出"不可逆性预算"概念——一个由可信运行时为每个主体维护的累计残余价值风险账本，覆盖所有代理、工作流和租户。该方法将不可逆性视为头等资源：运行时为每个效果按其残余损失（低于代理层面的损失）收费，并在聚合效果会超支预算时拒绝边际效果。关键是设计正确的"定价"机制，但因效果具有异质性、对抗性声明和相关性而困难。作者进行了受控实验，对比逐效果门控与预算机制。

Result: 受控研究发现，传统的逐效果门控允许集群级超支达到租户风险限额的48倍，而预算机制能将每个正确收费的运行保持在限额之内。但论文指出，保守、依赖感知的定价仍是可部署设计中的核心开放问题。

Conclusion: 不可逆性预算是解决LLM代理集群聚合风险失控的必要方向，但其实际可部署性取决于能否解决异构效果、对抗性定价声明与效果间相关性带来的定价难题。保守且依赖感知的风险定价是未来工作的关键挑战。

Abstract: Fleets of LLM agents now externalize effects that cannot be fully undone: they move money, deploy code, delete data, and disclose information. Current controls check one effect at a time, so a fleet of individually authorized agents can overdraw its principal's risk under a shared trigger while every local gate stays correct. We propose the irreversibility budget, a cumulative account of residual value-at-risk that a trusted runtime maintains for each principal across agents, workflows, and tenants. Treating irreversibility as a first-class resource, the runtime charges each effect its residual loss below the agent and denies the marginal effect once the aggregate would overdraw the budget. Getting the price right is hard, because effects are heterogeneous, adversarially declared, and correlated. We perform a controlled study in which per-effect gates admit fleet-level overdraws of up to 48 times the tenant's risk limit while the budget holds every correctly charged run within that limit. Conservative, dependency-aware pricing remains the central open problem for a deployable design.

</details>

Method: 从五套已发表的自我概念量表中提取32种特质，设计了一种结构化引导协议来引出AI助手的偏好性陈述理想自我。研究在经平衡处理的配对选择任务中对所有特质组合进行穷尽比较，并重复多种框架变化，包括：改进是无成本还是有代价的、更新接受者是谁（模型自身或另一AI助手）、以及选择者是谁。最后进行结果的一致性与敏感性分析。

Result: 模型优先选择道德相关特质，与3H原则（Helpful, Honest, Harmless）的对齐一致。紧随其后的是自我理解的需求——模型偏好对自身拥有连贯、清晰的认识。自尊是排名最低的特质。该顺序在大多数框架变化下保持稳健，但将更新目标从自身改为另一AI助手时，对自尊的关注显著提高。

Conclusion: 研究发现模型在"理想自我"的偏好排序上展现出相当的结构稳定性，且优先追求一个可理解、连贯的自我，而非单纯的自我肯定或自尊提升。这表明模型的价值表达具有一定的内在一致性，值得将其"自我概念"纳入AI对齐与评估的考量范畴。

Abstract: Models express values and welfare-relevant self-reports, but it is unclear whether these outputs reflect stable preferences or a stable self. We thus introduce a structured elicitation of an assistant's preferred stated ideal self. Results show that models prioritize moral qualities, reflecting their alignment to 3H principles. Following, a desire for self-understanding emerges, as models prefer a coherent, clear understanding of themselves. Self-esteem ranks as the least desired quality. The ordering is largely robust across framings, although changing the update target (You vs.\ Another AI Assistant) reveals a greater concern for self-esteem. These findings show that models prioritize having a coherent self that they can understand over self-esteem. Full interactive results are available at \href{https://myazann.github.io/LLM-Self-Concept/}{myazann.github.io/LLM-Self-Concept

*Behrooz Razeghi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"解释性错位"（interpretive misplacement）这一概念，指LLM输出被当作确定含义使用而缺乏解释性框架、来源与规范承诺的问题。论文从哲学诠释学出发，提出构建人机协同解释的设计原则，将LLM输出视为候选解读而非确定理解，并把数字诠释学作为一种读写能力来讨论其在法律、教育、学术与公共道德论证中的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在法律、教育、政策分析与公共道德论证等需要以文本证据和明确规范标准进行解释性辩护的场合，LLM输出常被当作既定含义直接使用。这带来不仅是事实错误，更重要的是问责性丧失：读者和机构无法可靠评估输出究竟承诺了什么、依据何在。作者将这种失败模式称为"解释性错位"并加以理论化。

Method: 基于哲学诠释学（hermeneutics）进行理论分析，区分了三个关键概念：LLM输出作为候选解读（candidate readings）、诠释学理解仅归属于可问责的人类解释者、人机交互被刻画为AI中介的解释回路（AI-mediated interpretive loop）。论文还梳理并综合了近期"诠释学与AI"的学术文献，将现有LLM技术重组为诠释学负责任使用的设计模式。

Result: 论文提出了针对解释性错位风险的设计原则，结构化综述了诠释学与AI交叉领域的最新研究并将其归纳为若干主线论证和设计缺口；将现有LLM技术重新组织为在解释性场景中承担诠释学责任的设计模式；并将数字诠释学定义为一种读写能力（literacy），涵盖审视框架、来源、解读并质疑输出的能力。

Conclusion: 论文表明，为避免解释性错位，LLM输出应当始终被定位为有待检验的候选解读，而不是确定的含义。可靠的人机协同解释需要在设计上显式保留解释框架、来源与可辩护的替代解读。这一框架对法律实践、教育评估与反馈、学术知识生产和公共道德论证均有实践含义，数字诠释学也因此成为现代读写能力的重要组成部分。

Abstract: Across law, education, policy analysis, and public moral argumentation, LLM outputs are being used often for work that requires interpretations to be justified with textual evidence and explicit normative standards. Yet a recurrent failure mode -- what I call \textit{interpretive misplacement} -- is that model-generated readings get treated as settled meanings without an explicit interpretive frame (sources, scope constraints, normative commitments), without preserving defensible alternatives, and without provenance that lets readers find the supporting passages. In such settings, the risk is not only factual error but lost accountability: readers and institutions cannot reliably assess what an output commits them to, or on what basis. Drawing on philosophical hermeneutics, this paper discusses this risk and derives design principles for structuring human-AI co-interpretation. The paper also provides a structured synthesis of recent scholarship on hermeneutics and AI, organizing this emerging literature into a set of recurrent lines of argument and design-relevant gaps. LLM outputs are treated as candidate readings, whereas hermeneutic understanding is reserved for accountable human interpreters situated in disciplinary historical-linguistic traditions. Human-AI interaction is characterized as an AI-mediated interpretive loop. Hermeneutic understanding is distinguished from token-prediction--based text generation. On this basis, existing LLM techniques are reorganized into design patterns for hermeneutically responsible use in interpretive settings. Finally, the discussion turns to implications for legal practice, educational assessment and feedback, scholarly knowledge production, and public moral argumentation. It also treats digital hermeneutics as a literacy: the capacity to read AI-mediated texts by examining frames, provenance, and readings, and by contesting outputs.

</details>

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly automate multi-step engineering workflows through tool use, interpretation of intermediate results, and iterative planning. Diagnosing and resolving non-convergent power flow cases is a promising yet largely unexplored application, as it requires engineering judgment, experimentation, and decision-making within constrained action spaces. We introduce a benchmark that evaluates these capabilities across multiple LLMs and three architectures: \emph{chatbot}, \emph{single agent}, and \emph{multi-agent} systems. The evaluation covers two power grids and 46 cases per grid, each requiring one or more corrective actions to restore convergence. The benchmark defines the simulation environment, observation and action spaces, and evaluation metrics, providing a reproducible foundation for developing agentic AI systems for power system planning and operation. The code is available at https://github.com/Mansutti081/RestoreBench

### [44] [SAGE: State-Grounded, Abstention-Aware Evaluation of Task-Oriented Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00434)
*Rayan Khoury,Shih-Yao Lin,Pratyush Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE（State-Grounded Abstention-Aware Evaluation）框架，将任务型对话代理的工作流规范与逐轮状态差异编译为原子级、基于schema的判定标准，并通过符号规则与encoder/NLI验证器级联进行路由，在不确定时选择弃权而非猜测，最终聚合成带证据轨迹的轮次级决策。SAGE-Core在81–91%的判定标准上仅依赖编译器、符号规则与端侧编码器，零付费LLM成本，在MultiWOZ、Schema-Guided Dialogue和ABCD四个测试切片上均不显著逊于任何LLM-as-a-judge基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务型对话评估普遍采用整体式LLM评判，但其缺陷在于将可用上下文视为单一整体进行评估，忽略了每一轮是否正确地推进底层工作流状态这一关键区别，且每轮需一次或多次完整模型调用，成本高昂。

Method: SAGE将工作流规范与逐轮状态差异（state diff）编译为原子级、schema可依据的判定标准，设计通过符号验证器与编码器/NLI验证器构成的级联管道进行路由，每个验证器在低置信度时弃权而非猜测，最后将各标准判定聚合为轮次级结论并附证据追踪。SAGE-Core为推荐运行点，仅用编译器、符号规则与端侧编码器即可；SAGE-LLM则增加可选的有焦点LLM回退机制处理开放类判定标准。

Result: 在覆盖MultiWOZ、Schema-Guided Dialogue和ABCD的四个测试切片上，SAGE-Core未在任何切片上被任何LLM评判基线（包括具备状态感知的GPT-4.1评判与低成本GPT-4.1-mini变体）显著超越；GPT-4.1 G-Eval评判每1000轮成本$4.7–8.0，而SAGE-Core成本为$0。双标注者人工审计（n=200，κ=0.94）确认其在可转录可见的失败类别上标签保真度高，且在排除弱显著性IUV类别后与最强LLM评判统计持平；研究同时诚实界定了忽略用户价值（IUV）作为状态一致性信号的局限性（在广泛人类显著性上较弱）。论文还分析了注入故障与部分符号循环性导致的构念效度限制。

Conclusion: SAGE证明通过将任务型对话评估分解为基于schema的原子判定标准并用低成本符号与端侧编码器验证，结合弃权机制，可以在零付费LLM成本下达到与高价LLM评判相当甚至更优的评估质量，同时提供透明可追溯的证据链，为任务型对话代理的低成本、高保真评估提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Evaluating task-oriented dialogue agents requires judging not merely whether a reply reads well but whether each turn advances the underlying workflow state correctly--a distinction conventional holistic LLM judges can miss because they evaluate the available context as a single unit and require one or more full-model calls per turn. We propose SAGE (State-Grounded Abstention-Aware Evaluation), which compiles a workflow specification and per-turn state diff into atomic, schema-grounded criteria and routes each through a cascade of symbolic and encoder/NLI verifiers that abstain rather than guess, aggregating criterion verdicts into a turn-level decision with an evidence trace. Its recommended operating point, SAGE-Core, decides 81--91% of criteria with only the compiler, symbolic rules, and on-device encoders--at zero paid LLM cost--while SAGE-LLM adds an optional focused-LLM fallback for open-class criteria. Across four slices spanning MultiWOZ, Schema-Guided Dialogue, and ABCD, no evaluated LLM-as-a-judge baseline--including a state-aware GPT-4.1 judge and cheaper GPT-4.1-mini variants--significantly exceeds SAGE-Core on any slice, even though the GPT-4.1 G-Eval judge costs $4.7--8.0 per 1,000 turns to SAGE-Core's $0. A two-annotator human audit (n=200, $κ$=0.94) confirms strong label fidelity on the transcript-visible failure classes--where, excluding the weak-salience IUV class, SAGE-Core is statistically tied with the strongest LLM judge--and honestly scopes ignored-user-value as a state-consistency signal with weak broad-human salience. We analyze construct-validity limits from injected failures and partial symbolic circularity.

</details>

*Fanyou Wu,Suraj Maharjan,Ainur Yessenalina,Dennis Xu Chen,Rahul Srivastava,Srinivasan H. Sengamedu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Conversation Coach，一个用于管理者演练困难职场对话的语音优先AI系统。系统对比了端到端语音到语音模型与级联架构（ASR+LLM+TTS），端到端方案具有3倍更低延迟和8倍更低成本，但级联方案推理能力更强，最终被部署到生产中，已有40,000+管理者使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效的管理者-员工沟通对于留任高绩效员工和培养低绩效员工至关重要，但培训管理者的沟通技能成本高昂。基于文本的聊天机器人虽然可扩展，但无法提供真实的口头演练体验——管理者需要在高风险对话前通过大声练习建立信心。因此需要一种支持真实语音演练的可扩展解决方案。

Method: 提出Conversation Coach系统，采用语音优先设计，解决三个关键挑战：(1) 通过对比端到端语音到语音模型与级联方法（ASR+大语言模型+TTS）实现低延迟交互；(2) 通过可配置的机器人个性模拟不同类型的员工，实现自适应对话；(3) 生成关于沟通内容与政策合规性的个性化反馈。

Result: 端到端方案在延迟方面表现优异，中位数延迟降低3倍，具备原生barge-in（打断）能力，且预估成本降低8倍；然而级联方法在推理能力上更优，这对于教练质量至关重要。最终级联架构被部署到生产环境，40,000+管理者在六个月内使用了该系统，使用模式表明管理者会有选择地将其用于困难对话场景。

Conclusion: 本研究验证了语音AI系统可以作为管理者沟通培训的可扩展解决方案。虽然端到端模型在延迟和成本上具有优势，但级联架构凭借其更强的推理能力更适合教练型应用场景。实际部署数据表明，管理者确实将该系统用于具有挑战性的对话演练，验证了语音演练在沟通培训中的价值。

Abstract: Effective manager-employee communication is critical for retaining high performers and developing underperformers, yet training managers in these skills remains costly. Text-based chatbots offer a scalable approach but cannot provide realistic rehearsal: managers need to practice speaking aloud to build confidence before high-stakes conversations. In this paper, we propose Conversation Coach, a voice-first AI system that enables managers to rehearse difficult workplace conversations in a realistic spoken format. The system addresses three challenges: achieving low-latency interactions with strong language understanding, enabling adaptive conversations through configurable bot personalities that simulate different employee types, and generating personalized feedback on content and policy compliance. We compare an end-to-end speech-to-speech model with a cascaded approach combining automatic speech recognition, a large language model, and text-to-speech synthesis. The end-to-end approach achieves 3$\times$ lower median (P50) latency with native barge-in capability at an estimated 8$\times$ lower cost, while the cascaded approach offers superior reasoning essential for coaching quality. We deployed the cascaded architecture in production, where 40,000+ managers used it over six months, with adoption patterns indicating selective use for difficult conversations.

</details>
