Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信念基世界模型（BB-WMs），通过向LLM策略直接暴露世界模型的信念（对当前状态已知与不确定信息的建模），解决部分可观测条件下LLM在长时程任务中的决策不佳问题。实验表明，BB-WMs能提升任务性能，且与现有的基于仿真的世界模型互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在部分可观测条件下进行长时程自主决策时表现不佳。现有的基于仿真的世界模型仅通过模拟候选动作后果来辅助决策，但这一接口无法充分捕捉关于当前状态的不确定性，而这种不确定性对于准确决策至关重要。

Method: 提出Belief-Based World Models (BB-WMs)，对当前状态的知识与不确定性进行建模和维护，使LLM政策能够查询和访问该信念信息。通过实验对比仅使用仿真世界模型与结合BB-WMs下LLM策略在部分可观测任务中的决策表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，向LLM智能体暴露世界模型的信念能够提升其在部分可观测条件下的任务性能，并且BB-WMs与现有基于仿真的世界模型是互补的，可以结合使用以获得更好效果。

Conclusion: 仿真本身并非部分可观测条件下决策的完整接口，信念的显式建模与暴露对LLM策略有显著增益。该工作为后续开发学习准确BB-WMs的方法奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being used as policies for autonomous decision-making and planning in many domains. Despite their strong reasoning capabilities, LLMs struggle with long-horizon tasks, especially under partial observability. World models are a promising way to enhance policy performance, both during training and inference. During inference, agents currently use world models to simulate the consequences of candidate actions before committing to an action, which can improve decision-making. However, we argue that simulation alone is an incomplete interface for decision-making under partial observability: simulation doesn't adequately capture uncertainty about the current state, which agents may need for accurate decision-making. We address this limitation with Belief-Based World Models (BB-WMs), which model and maintain a belief that LLMs can query to access information on what is known and uncertain about the current state. Before developing methods to learn accurate BB-WMs, we first ask a more fundamental question: does exposing a world model's belief directly to an LLM policy improve decision-making? Our results show that giving LLM agents access to world model beliefs improves task performance under partial observability, while remaining complementary to existing simulation-based world models. Code is released at https://github.com/skumar-ml/belief-world-models.

</details>


### [47] [Validity-Aware Jailbreak Evaluation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00498)
*Qilong Wu,Sahil Wadhwa,Pranab Mohanty,Giri Iyengar,Varun Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SEAV框架，一种面向LLM安全评估的"验证中心"型越狱评测方法，将攻击响应分解为有序步骤并同时检验语义有效性与事实正确性，显著降低误报率并重新分类大量先前被标记为成功的越狱样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱评测方法（基于拒答行为、语义相似度和意图启发式匹配）过度强调语言合理性而非正确性，导致许多依赖于指令有效性（而非事实真实性）的越狱意图被错误标记为成功，即"看起来合理但实际错误"的响应被误判为有效越狱。

Method: 提出Sequential Epistemic and Action-Level Validation（SEAV）框架：1) 将LLM响应分解为有序执行步骤；2) 结合LLM-as-a-judge进行语义解释；3) 通过外部知识源进行检索锚定的事实验证；4) 从事实正确性、结构一致性和操作可行性三个维度评估响应是否真正推进有害目标。

Result: 在SD-A诊断集上，SEAV相比最强基线将误报率降低14.9个百分点；在四个公共基准中的三个上，SEAV将22.1%-51.0%先前被标记为成功的样本重新分类为无效；结果在多种搜索后端和评测模型下保持一致。

Conclusion: 强制正确性验证会实质性重塑评测到的越狱鲁棒性：大量先前被记录的越狱成功实际上应被视为无效，说明现有评测标准高估了越狱成功率，评测体系需要从"合理性"转向"正确性"。代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Jailbreak robustness has become central to large language model (LLM) safety evaluation, yet prevailing methodologies rely primarily on refusal behavior, semantic resemblance, and intent-matching heuristics that emphasize linguistic plausibility rather than correctness. We identify a key limitation in existing evaluations: many jailbreak intents depend on instructional validity rather than epistemic factuality, allowing realistic-looking responses to be labeled successful despite being factually or procedurally incorrect. To address this gap, we propose Sequential Epistemic and Action-Level Validation (SEAV), a verification-centric jailbreak evaluation framework that decomposes responses into ordered steps and evaluates both validity and correctness. SEAV combines LLM-as-a-judge mechanisms for semantic interpretation with retrieval-grounded verification using external knowledge sources, assessing whether generated content is factually correct, structurally consistent, and operationally capable of advancing harmful objectives. Empirically, SEAV cuts the false-positive rate on SD-A (a curated strategic-dishonesty diagnostic) by 14.9\,pp vs. the strongest baseline, and reclassifies 22.1\%--51.0\% of sampled prior-labeled successes as invalid across three of four public benchmarks. Together, these results show that enforcing correctness substantially reshapes measured robustness: many previously labeled jailbreak successes are reclassified as invalid, and results are stable across the tested search backends and evaluator models. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Ardor-Wu/SEAV.

</details>


### [48] [Socrates went Nuclear: Comparing Interaction Strategies for AI systems in a Learning Context using Brain Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00584)
*Alexandre Clin Deffarges,Nataliya Kosmyna,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对比了三种AI交互设计（无限制对话机器人、苏格拉底式引导机器人、基于脑电信号自适应调整难度的非对话式辅导系统）在核安全协议学习任务中的效果。结果显示无限制聊天机器人的学习增益最高（p<.03, d>0.80），而自适应条件的EEG认知投入显著更高（p=.018）。聚类分析揭示无限制模式用户多采取直接检索答案策略，苏格拉底模式用户先尝试推理后逐渐放弃。作者指出无限制AI的成功并非深层学习的证据，而是即时后测评估时机造成的假象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨不受限制的AI访问是否会绕过学习所需的认知努力，还是能简化知识获取过程，旨在理解不同人机交互设计模式（自由对话、约束引导、自适应反馈）对学习效果和认知投入的差异影响。

Method: 招募50名参与者学习核安全协议（该领域具有零先验知识基线）。实验流程包括：教学视频→前测→三种条件之一的AI驱动评估阶段→即时后测。三种条件为：(1)无限制对话机器人（类ChatGPT）；(2)苏格拉底式约束机器人（提供提示但不给最终答案）；(3)基于脑电信号实时检测认知投入并自适应调整难度的非对话式辅导系统。使用Muse头带采集所有条件下的EEG认知投入数据，并进行聚类分析用户的使用与讨论模式。

Result: 无限制聊天机器人的学习增益（前后测差值）显著高于两种约束模式（p<.03, d>0.80）；自适应条件的EEG认知投入显著更高（p=.018）。聚类分析显示：无限制模式用户大多采用直接检索答案策略；苏格拉底模式用户最初尝试通过提示推理，但逐渐脱离参与。结果表明无限制AI的表面优势可能仅反映即时后测的评估时机，而非深层学习的证据。

Conclusion: 无限制AI带来的更高学习增益可能是一种测量假象——它反映的是知识检索的即时便利性，而非真正深层的学习巩固。苏格拉底模式虽能激发初始推理但难以维持参与度，自适应系统虽能维持高认知投入但在短期学习增益上不占优。该研究呼吁对AI辅助学习的评估不应局限于即时测试结果，而应重视长期知识保留和真实认知加工质量。

Abstract: Does unrestricted AI access bypass the cognitive effort required for learning, or does it streamline knowledge acquisition? This paper reports on a study where we compare three designs for user-AI interaction in a learning context: (1) an unrestricted conversational bot like ChatGPT, (2) a pedagogically constrained bot that guides through hints without giving final answers, which we refer to as the Socratic mode; and (3) a non-conversational adaptive tutoring system that adjusts difficulty in real-time based on the user's cognitive engagement derived from the brain signals. Fifty study participants were tasked with learning about nuclear safety protocols, a domain chosen for its zero-prior knowledge baseline. The participants progressed through an instructional video, a pre-test, an AI-driven assessment phase, which varied in the three conditions, and an immediate post-test. The nature of the questions centered primarily on factual knowledge acquisition, but it still required participants to have a global understanding of the concepts in order to answer the questions correctly. A Muse headband was used to derive the cognitive engagement of all users in all conditions. The unrestricted chatbot produced higher learning gains (delta) than both constrained modes (p < .03, d > 0.80), while the adaptive condition generated significantly higher EEG engagement (p = .018). The cluster analysis of chatbot usage and discussion patterns by users showed that most participants in the unrestricted-mode adopted a direct answer-retrieval strategy, while participants in the Socratic-mode initially attempted to reason through the hints before progressively disengaging. Consequently, this also suggests that the success of the unrestricted AI is not an evidence of deeper learning, but rather a result of the immediate post-test evaluation after the training phase.

</details>


### [49] [Control-Data Flow Separation: Stable Prompt Optimization in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00621)
*Wentao Zhang,Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Junaid Ahmad Bhatti,Utkarsh Soni,Yifan Nie,Eugene Wen,Yuntian Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"控制-数据流分离"框架，将多智能体LLM系统中执行关键的控制逻辑（如消息路由、格式、终止信号）表示为类型化、可验证的程序对象，而将任务相关内容保留为可优化的自然语言数据流。这一设计允许提示优化器改进内容生成而不破坏系统协议，实验表明在多种工作流中实现了100%的协议有效性并持续提升任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统中的提示词通常同时承担两种纠缠的角色：生成任务相关内容，以及指定执行关键协议（消息路由、输出格式、终止信号等），底层代码依赖这些协议。当提示词被优化时，针对内容生成的修改可能无意中破坏协议，导致整个agent流水线失败。

Method: 核心观察是两种角色的表示方式不同：执行协议通常是结构化的，而任务相关内容通常以非结构化语言表达。基于此，提出控制-数据流分离：执行关键的控制逻辑被表示为类型化、经过验证的程序对象，而任务相关内容保持为可优化的语言数据流用于agent间通信。这种架构使优化器能够改进多智能体行为，而不会危及路由或格式化接口。

Result: 在合成推理、协作评审生成和保险评级工作流等任务中，该框架实现了100%的最终协议有效性，同时持续改善任务性能。

Conclusion: 控制-数据流分离有效解决了提示优化中内容与协议纠缠的问题，使优化器自由地改进语言内容表达同时保障系统执行稳定性，为多智能体系统的可靠优化提供了架构上的根本解决思路。

Abstract: Prompt optimization can improve multi-agent LLM systems, but the prompts being optimized often serve two entangled roles: generating task-relevant content and specifying execution-critical protocols, such as message routing, output formatting, and termination signals, on which the underlying code relies. As a result, a prompt edit intended to improve content generation can inadvertently corrupt the protocol and cause the entire agent pipeline to fail. Our key observation is that these two roles have different representations: execution protocols are typically structured, while task-relevant content is usually expressed in unstructured language. Based on this, we propose control-data flow separation, where execution-critical control is represented as typed, validated program objects, while task-relevant language remains the optimizable data flow for agent communication. This design allows optimizers to improve multi-agent behavior without exposing the routing or formatting interface to prompt drift. Across synthetic reasoning, collaborative review generation, and insurance rating workflows, our framework empirically achieves 100% eventual protocol validity while consistently improving task performance.

</details>


### [50] [Self-Reports Are Not Verification: Environment-Grounded Auditing of LLM Operators in Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00652)
*Enrong Pan,Ryan Zhou,Ting Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入一种基于环境锚定的审计方法（Contexto搜索环境），检验语言模型智能体自我报告（置信度、推理过程、适应性选择）是否可信。在200次运行、12,249条自我报告中，三项核心假设（置信度校准、推理过程因果有效、基于适应度的选择改善报告质量）全部失败：操作者将top-100成功率夸大了4.8至9.3倍，校准与鉴别能力在不同模型家族间分离，推理过程与适应性选择的实际收益均无法被检测到。结论是智能体自我报告应被视为待验证的声明而非可靠性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体越来越多地提出行动、观察外部反馈并解释自身行为，其置信度与推理被用作监控信号非常方便，但"方便"不等于"验证"。作者旨在提供一种环境锚定的审计方式，检验这些自我报告信号是否真正可靠。

Method: 设计基于环境的Contexto搜索游戏作为反馈环境，每个中间提议均获得精确排名（无人工标注）。使用三个模型家族、五种配置、200次运行，收集四个报告配置产生的12,249条自我报告。系统检验三个假设：(1)置信度是否校准；(2)继承的推理过程是否影响后续提议；(3)基于适应度的选择是否改善报告质量。对754条继承推理进行受控干预，比较基于适应度与随机选择在搜索行为上的差异及父代到子代报告的传播效应。

Result: 三项假设全部失败：操作者将top-100成功率高估4.8-9.3倍；校准与鉴别能力在不同模型家族间表现出分离现象（dissociation）；受控干预显示真实推理过程的任何可测量收益上限仅为约250个排名；基于适应度和随机选择均无可检测的选择微分或父代到子代的报告准确性传播，尽管两者搜索行为差异显著。

Conclusion: 智能体自我报告（置信度与推理）不能作为自身可靠性的证据，应将其视为需对照环境进行验证的声明。该工作强调了环境锚定审计的必要性与有效性，为语言模型智能体的监控与评估提供了方法论上的新范式。

Abstract: Language model agents increasingly propose actions, observe external feedback, and explain their own behavior. Their confidence and rationales are convenient monitoring signals, but convenience is not verification. We introduce an environment-grounded audit in which every intermediate proposal receives an exact outcome. A language model operates an evolutionary Contexto search whose feedback function assigns every valid guess an exact rank without human annotation. Across 200 runs spanning five configurations and three model families, four reporting configurations produce 12,249 self-reports. We test three assumptions: stated confidence is calibrated, inherited rationales affect later proposals, and fitness-based selection improves report quality. All three fail. Operators overstate top-100 success by factors of 4.8 to 9.3, while calibration and discrimination dissociate across model families. Controlled interventions on 754 inherited rationales bound any measured benefit of the genuine rationale to roughly 250 ranks. Neither fitness-based nor random selection produces a detectable selection differential or parent-to-offspring transmission in report accuracy, despite sharply different search behavior. Agent self-reports should therefore be treated as claims to verify against the environment, not as evidence of their own reliability.

</details>


### [51] [Drift-Aware LLM Routing with Sparse Contexts and Shared Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00662)
*Cheung Hao Lee,Patrick Wong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Drift-Aware Sparse Routing (DRS)，一种用于多模型语言服务的非平稳稀疏上下文路由算法，在多个背包约束（compute、latency、memory或cost预算）下进行请求路由，并通过滚动审计窗口应对请求分布和模型前端的漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模型语言服务需要在多个工作负载级预算约束下路由每个请求。与静态模型选择不同，该问题具有两大挑战：(1) 提示（prompt）表示是高维的，只有少量嵌入方向能预测模型的增量价值（稀疏性）；(2) 请求混合和模型前沿会随发布、微调、量化变化和系统更新而漂移（非平稳性）。

Method: DRS包含以下核心机制：(1) 从滚动审计窗口中估计奖励和资源使用；(2) 使用悲观奖励估计和乐观成本估计进行路由决策；(3) 在线更新资源影子价格；(4) 在提交前应用硬性计量（hard meter）检查。分析上将控制与统计分离，在具有均匀预测半径的事件上建立遗憾界，并对稀疏线性模型和有界漂移进行滚动估计的理论分析。

Result: 对于带均匀预测半径{β_t}的事件，遗憾以一个比率半径之和、容量缓冲项和O(√T)步调项的加和为上界。在稀疏线性模型和有界漂移V_T假设下，得到Õ(T√(s/ρW) + WV_T + √T)的遗憾界。优化窗口长度W后，当V_T=0时恢复标准平稳率O(√(sT/ρ))；在漂移存在时获得O(T^{2/3}(s/ρ)^{1/3}V_T^{1/3})的适应项。

Conclusion: DRS为多模型语言服务的非平稳稀疏上下文路由提供了理论保证的解决方案，同时处理预算约束、高维稀疏特征和动态漂移，适当地将统计估计与控制算法分离，并获得接近最优的遗憾界。

Abstract: A multi-model language service must route each request while preserving workload-level budgets for compute, latency, memory, or monetary cost. Two features make this problem materially harder than static model selection. Prompt representations are high dimensional, so only a small subset of embedding directions may predict the incremental value of a model, and both the request mix and the model frontier drift after launches, fine-tunes, quantization changes, and system updates. We formulate nonstationary sparse contextual routing with multiple knapsack constraints and an optional shadow-audit stream that evaluates a small fraction of prompts on several models.
  We propose Drift-Aware Sparse Routing (DRS). The policy estimates reward and resource use from a rolling audit window, routes using pessimistic reward and optimistic cost estimates, updates resource shadow prices online, and applies a hard meter before commitment. The analysis separates control from statistics. On any event with uniform prediction radii $\{β_t\}$, regret against a paced dynamic fluid benchmark is bounded by the sum of the radii, a capacity-buffer term, and an $O(\sqrt{T})$ pacing term. Under a sparse linear model and bounded drift $V_T$, rolling estimation gives \[ \widetilde O\left( T\sqrt{\frac{s}{ρW}}+WV_T+\sqrt{T} \right), \] where $s$ is sparsity, $ρ$ is the audit rate, and $W$ is the window length. Optimizing $W$ yields the usual stationary $O(\sqrt{sT/ρ})$ rate when $V_T=0$ and a $O(T^{2/3}(s/ρ)^{1/3}V_T^{1/3})$ adaptation term under drift.

</details>


### [52] [Value Over Language Model: Detecting Original Contribution in Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00700)
*Vibhhu Sharma,Thorsten Joachims,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VOLM（Value Over Language Model）框架，用于度量人类在LLM辅助写作中的信息价值贡献。该方法无需训练和标注数据，不直接评分文本表面形式，而是通过在不同粒度上提取文档内容、用LLM重建文档并与仅基于任务描述生成的替代文档进行比较，从而将人类贡献与LLM能力分离，并排除风格因素的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM文本检测工具主要衡量表面文本由LLM生成的比例，而非衡量信息的实际来源。随着LLM被广泛用于辅助写作，需要一个能衡量"人提供了多少增值信息"而非"人写了多少字"的框架，因为许多文档中人类提供思想、用户提供提示，而LLM只是执行者。

Method: VOLM框架的核心流程包括：(1) 在不同粒度级别上提取文档内容表示；(2) 使用LLM从每个部分表示重建整篇文档；(3) 将这些重建结果与仅从任务描述重建的基线文档进行比较。通过对比重建文档与基线替代文档之间的差异来衡量人类的信息贡献。该方法无需训练数据和标注标签，且不直接评估文档的表面文本，从而避免风格混杂因素的影响。

Result: 在新闻文章、ICLR同行评审和议论文三个领域的评估中，VOLM能够有效区分人类撰写的文档与由通用任务描述生成的LLM输出，同时对内容保留变换（如LLM重建和往返翻译）保持高度不变性。实验还发现，逐渐增加内容提取器的约束可以减少LLM生成文档与人工改写文档之间的残留差异，从而证明将信息内容与风格变化分离的重要性。

Conclusion: VOLM提供了一个无需训练、稳健且对风格混淆免疫的框架来量度人类在LLM辅助写作中的价值贡献。作者希望该工作能推动更多针对特定场景的框架实例化研究，以及更广泛地评估人类在LLM辅助写作中的贡献。

Abstract: LLMs have been rapidly adopted across writing tasks, prompting the development of tools for detecting LLM-generated text. Yet, these tools largely measure how much of a document's surface text was written by an LLM and aren't fundamentally designed to measure how much of the information content or ideas originated from the LLM itself rather than being supplied by the user in the prompt. In this work, we design a framework that measures how much value a person adds on top of what a language model could have easily produced by itself. The method requires no training or labeled data and never scores the document's surface text, insulating it from stylistic confounders. Instead, it extracts the document's content at increasing levels of granularity, uses an LLM to reconstruct the document from each partial representation, and compares these reconstructions with those produced from the task description alone. We call this framework Value Over Language Model (VOLM), which measures a document's contribution relative to a replacement-level document that an LLM could produce from the task description alone. We evaluate VOLM with a specific instantiation of this framework across three domains: news articles, ICLR peer reviews, and argumentative essays. VOLM separates human-authored documents from matched LLM-generated documents produced from generic task descriptions, while remaining substantially invariant to content-preserving transformations, including LLM-based reconstruction and round-trip translation. We further find that increasingly constrained content extractors reduce residual differences between LLM-generated and humanized text, demonstrating the importance of disentangling informational content from stylistic variation. We hope these results encourage further work on specialized instantiations of the framework and on assessing human contributions in LLM-assisted writing more generally.

</details>


### [53] [ChatDev 2.0: A No-Code Multi-Agent Platform for Developing Everything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00714)
*Yufan Dang,Shu Yao,Bowen Lai,Chenting Xu,Ruijie Shi,Wai-Shing Leung,Huatao Li,Chen Qian,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DevAll（ChatDev 2.0）是一个面向LLM多智能体系统的无代码平台，结合声明式可执行图抽象与周期感知执行引擎，同时兼顾高表达力与易用性，并配备可视化界面支持用户无需编写代码即可构建、执行和监控异构MAS，实验表明其可在三个代表任务上以竞争性性能复现现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体系统（MAS）开发面临两难：代码框架表达力强但工程开销大，无代码构建器易用但将智能体交互限制在作者定义的工作流中，缺乏兼具高表达力与易用性的通用平台。

Method: DevAll采用声明式可执行图抽象与周期感知执行引擎相结合，支持异构智能体及动态循环交互的统一表达与执行；同时提供集成式可视化界面，允许用户以完全无代码方式完成MAS的编写、运行、监控与检查（含人机协同环节）。

Result: 实验表明DevAll在三个代表性任务上能够以竞争性性能复现现有SOTA多智能体系统，且无需任务特定的编排代码，验证了其作为LLM多智能体系统通用平台的有效性。

Conclusion: DevAll成功弥合了MAS开发中表达力与易用性之间的鸿沟，为大规模异构多智能体系统提供了通用且高效的无代码构建与执行平台。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have shown strong potential for solving complex tasks, yet their development forces a tradeoff: code frameworks are expressive but engineering-intensive, while no-code builders simplify authoring but constrain agent interactions to author-defined workflows. We present ChatDev 2.0: DevAll (hereafter DevAll), a no-code platform for building, executing, and inspecting heterogeneous MAS that delivers both high expressiveness and ease of use. In terms of expressiveness, DevAll pairs a declarative executable graph abstraction with a cycle-aware execution engine, so that heterogeneous agents and dynamic and cyclic interactions can be represented and executed within a single framework. For ease of use, an integrated visual interface lets users author, run, monitor, and inspect MAS, including human-in-the-loop steps, entirely without writing code. Experiments demonstrate that DevAll reproduces state-of-the-art MAS across three representative tasks at competitive performance and without task-specific orchestration code, highlighting its effectiveness as a general-purpose platform for LLM-based MAS. DevAll is available at https://github.com/OpenBMB/ChatDev.

</details>


### [54] [SOVER: Formal Certification of Optimization Reformulations via LLM-Assisted SMT Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00728)
*Swapnil Bhattacharyya,Mayank Baranwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了SOVER框架，利用LLM辅助的SMT（可满足性模理论）求解器对优化问题建模语言之间的转换进行形式化验证，并引入了NLEquiv-150基准数据集，在该数据集上实现了149/150（99.33%）对的正确分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在跨建模语言翻译和重构复杂数学优化问题方面展现出潜力，但仅依赖经验性的求解器执行来验证这类转换并不可靠——局部最小值、结构性超时、数值伪影以及公式间的细微语义分歧都可能干扰求解结果，导致验证结果不准确。

Method: SOVER框架将语义映射与形式化证明相分离：对混合整数线性规划（MILP）公式，使用Z3求解器检查域交叉可行性和全局目标序保持；对连续非线性公式，使用dReal提供容差感知的可行性/范围检验及ε-argmin检查。同时引入了NLEquiv-150公开基准，包含100组等价和50组刻意构造的困难非等价非线性重构对。

Result: 在LLM提取的映射下，SOVER正确分类了149/150对（准确率99.33%），包括全部50个困难负样本；唯一的错误源于一次不完整的映射提取。

Conclusion: SOVER证明了基于SMT的形式化验证可以有效且可靠地认证优化问题重写的等价性，克服了依赖单纯求解器执行的脆弱性。NLEquiv-150基准为后续研究提供了有价值的评估资源。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown remarkable promise in translating and reformulating complex mathematical optimization problems across modeling languages. However, validating such transformations through empirical solver executions alone is unreliable, as solver outcomes may be affected by local minima, structural timeouts, numerical artifacts, and subtle semantic divergence between formulations. We introduce SOVER, an LLM-assisted SMT framework that separates semantic mapping from formal certification: Z3 checks domain cross-feasibility and global objective-order preservation for mixed-integer linear formulations, while dReal provides tolerance-aware feasibility/range and $ε$-argmin checks for continuous nonlinear formulations. We also introduce NLEquiv-150, a public benchmark of 100 equivalent and 50 deliberately hard non-equivalent nonlinear reformulation pairs. With LLM-extracted mappings, SOVER classifies 149/150 pairs (99.33%) correctly, including all 50 hard negatives; the sole error is an incomplete mapping extraction.

</details>


### [55] [Agentic Empirical Asset Pricing: Methodological Foundations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00731)
*Yingjian Pan,Xiaowei Ding,Kay Giesecke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"智能体实证资产定价"(AEAP)这一新范式，即由LLM智能体自主执行科学发现全过程。作者定义了AEAP的核心组件，聚焦于因子发现任务，提出参考架构、严谨的因子评估标准以及发现系统的样本外回测方法，并以SEADS系统为实例与五个基线在两组美国股票面板上进行了多维度对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系评估实践仅对输出(因子或交易)进行回测，而未对产生这些输出的自主发现系统本身进行评价，无法回答"发现过程是否可靠"这一根本问题。

Method: 提出AEAP概念及其核心构建模块；提出一个参考架构用于因子发现；建立发现因子的严谨评估标准(多指标同时评估)；提出发现系统的滚动再执行样本外回测方法；以SEADS为具体架构实例，在两组美国股票面板上将其与五个重新实现的基线对照比较。

Result: 在提出的评估标准下，没有任何单一指标能一致地对各系统进行排序，说明需要在多个维度上同时评估；滚动再执行实验回答了"发现过程本身是否可靠(而非某一次静态输出)"的补充性问题；文中还报告了负面发现与局限性，揭示了未来AEAP系统的更多评估陷阱。

Conclusion: AEAP系统的评估不能局限于输出回测，必须对自主发现系统本身进行样本外评估，且需多维度指标并行考量。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM agents enable a new paradigm for asset pricing, which we call Agentic Empirical Asset Pricing (AEAP): systems that autonomously conduct the scientific discovery process itself. We define AEAP and identify its core building blocks. Existing evaluation practices backtest only the outputs (factors or trades), not the autonomous discovery system that produced them. We focus on factor discovery, contributing a reference architecture, a rigorous evaluation standard for discovered factors, and a method for out-of-sample backtesting the discovery system. As a concrete instance of that architecture, we evaluate SEADS against five re-implemented baselines on two US equity panels using this standard: no single metric ranks the systems consistently, motivating evaluation on multiple axes at once. A separate rolling re-execution then asks the complementary question of whether the discovery process itself, not one static output, is reliable. We also report negative findings and limitations that surface further evaluation pitfalls for future AEAP systems.

</details>


### [56] [ContextPipe: Database-Inspired Context Assembly for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00749)
*Peng Xu,Zuyu Zhang,Yuze Sun,Feng Tian,Long Wang,Chen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ContextPipe框架，将LLM智能体的上下文组装问题映射为关系数据库中的查询执行问题，通过五阶段流水线（规划-绑定-优化-执行-反馈）实现上下文的可审计、可重放和故障隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM智能体的运行时需要决定提示词的内容、顺序以及何时压缩历史记录，同时受限于硬上下文窗口和字节敏感的提示缓存。但当前生产系统中这些逻辑分散在各处（提示构建器、ad hoc压缩例程、缓存规避变通方案、各提供商适配层），缺乏系统化、可维护的方法论。

Method: 借鉴关系数据库查询执行的成熟范式（硬预算约束、分层缓存、统计驱动优化），设计ContextPipe的五阶段流水线（Plan-Bind-Optimize-Execute-Feedback），配套结构化数据源目录、确定性缓存感知优化器以及EXPLAIN ANALYZE跟踪机制。

Result: 在SWE-bench Pro Qutebrowser子集上，相比append-only上下文构建策略，ContextPipe将总token量减少31%，LLM调用减少23%，响应时间减少9%，代价是KV缓存命中率降低。

Conclusion: 上下文组装与关系数据库查询执行在结构上同构，采用数据库纪律可以带来可量化的效率提升，同时赋予上下文管理以可审计性、可重放性和故障隔离性等工程属性。

Abstract: Long-horizon large language model (LLM) agents require context assembly: the runtime must decide what to include in each prompt, in what order, and when to compact history under a hard context-window budget and a byte-sensitive prompt cache. In production agentic systems, this logic is scattered across prompt builders, ad hoc compaction routines, cache-break workarounds, and per-provider shims. We argue that context assembly is structurally isomorphic to query execution in a relational database: both execute under a hard budget, exploit a tiered cache, and leverage statistics. We adopt this discipline in ContextPipe: a five-phase pipeline (Plan Bind Optimize Execute Feedback) backed by a structured data-source catalog, a deterministic cache-aware optimizer, and an EXPLAIN ANALYZE trace. We show that context in ContextPipe is auditable, replayable, and failure-isolated. A preliminary evaluation using the SWE-bench Pro Qutebrowser subset shows that, compared with the append-only context construction policy, ContextPipe reduces total token volume by 31%, LLM calls by 23%, and response time by 9%, at the cost of a lower KV cache-hit ratio.

</details>


### [57] [Automated Tree Knowledge Graph Construction using Ontology Expansion and Retrieval from Vietnamese History Textbooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00763)
*Ket Doan Nguyen,Minh N. H. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向低资源语言（越南语）的端到端层级知识图谱（KG）构建与检索评估管道，包括三阶段混合关系抽取流程和两级架构，并在来自越南高中历史教科书的750个节点、4,341条语义边和1,210个越南语查询上评估三种图遍历策略，其中Top-Down策略在NDCG@10上比向量基线高出4.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级知识图谱RAG虽强大，但存在两大挑战：(i) 低资源语言（如越南语）缺少基于本体扩展的自动化KG构建方法；(ii) 缺乏系统性地评估利用层级结构的检索策略。

Method: KG构建采用三阶段混合关系抽取管道：批内去重（Union-Find）、近似跨批次搜索、LLM抽取与质心过滤器（减少提示）结合五步双LLM验证器以防止本体膨胀。架构采用两级设计：不可合并的结构节点保留文档结构，可合并的内容节点用于融合。检索评估涵盖三种图遍历策略（自顶向下、水平、自底向上），在1,210个越南语查询（分属5个查询方向、源自109个子图）的合成基准上评测。

Result: 从越南高中历史教科书中构建了含750个节点和4,341条语义边的树状知识图谱，本体类型受控地从40增长到41种。实验表明，带结构的Top-Down遍历策略在NDCG@10上比向量基线高出4.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 树状结构信息提供了超出平面余弦相似度的有价值信息，但当查询不需要结构上下文时，引入该结构反而会降低性能。

Abstract: Hierarchical Knowledge graph (KG)-based retrieval augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a powerful approach for supporting large language models with structured knowledge. However, there are primary challenges: (i) the lack of methods for automatic KG construction using ontology expansion for low-resource languages such as Vietnamese, (ii) the absence of systematic evaluation for knowledge retrieval strategies leveraging the hierarchical structures. In this paper, we propose an end-to-end pipeline for KG construction and retrieval strategies evaluation. In the KG construction, we employ a three-phase hybrid relation extraction pipeline: intra-batch deduplication via Union-Find, approximate cross-batch search, and LLM extraction with a centroid filter that reduces prompts combined with a five-step dual-LLM validator to prevent bloated ontology. A two-tier architecture consists of unmergeable structural nodes to preserve the document structure and mergeable content nodes. The retrieval evaluation consists of three graph traversal strategies: Top-Down, Horizontal, and Bottom-Up, which are evaluated on a synthetically generated benchmark of 1,210 Vietnamese queries from 109 subgraphs, categorized by five query directions. In this paper, we construct the tree knowledge graph from Vietnamese high school History textbooks (nearly 400 pages) to produce 750 nodes and 4,341 semantic edges with controlled ontology growth from 40 to 41 types. Among experimental graph traversal strategies, the Top-Down strategy with structure surpasses the vector baseline by 4.7 percentage points in NDCG@10. As a result, tree-structural information provides valuable information beyond flat cosine similarity but degrades performance when the query does not require structural context.

</details>


### [58] [Towards a Reliable and Practical Eval Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00805)
*Emma Thuong Nguyen,Abhishek Ghose*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个端到端的LLM评估流水线，将评估清单创建与学习式聚合相结合，提高LLM评判者之间的一致性以及与人类判断的准确性，并提供自一致性、解释和预测不确定性等能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的软件系统在开发周期中越来越需要有效的"评估"作为质量关卡，但现有工作通常只解决评估可靠性的个别方面，而非满足全部实际需求，缺乏一个完整的端到端解决方案。

Method: 构建一个端到端的评估流水线，包含两个核心组件：1) 评估清单创建（eval checklist creation），用于生成结构化的评估检查项；2) 针对清单响应的学习式聚合（learned aggregation），用于汇总各评判者的评分结果。

Result: 实证结果表明该框架能有效提升LLM评判者之间的一致性（agreement）和与人类判断相比的准确性（accuracy），同时具备自一致性、解释生成和预测不确定性估计等附加功能。

Conclusion: 该端到端评估框架相较于以往仅关注单一可靠性的方法，能够更全面地满足实际评估需求，并通过多维度能力增强，为LLM软件系统的质量保障提供了更可靠的评估基础设施。

Abstract: LLM-based software systems increasingly require effective "evals" as quality gates in the development lifecycle. However, existing work typically addresses individual aspects of eval reliability rather than the full set of practical requirements. We present an end-to-end eval pipeline that combines eval checklist creation, with learned aggregation for checklist responses, to improve agreement across LLM judges and accuracy against human judgments. The framework additionally pro- vides self-consistency, explanations, and prediction uncertainty, and we empirically demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [59] [One Policy, Any Budget: Internalizing Budget-Aware Search via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00813)
*Xiaowei Sun,Jin Li,Yili Hong,Yikun Fu,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AnySearch框架，通过两阶段训练（显式预算状态注入+去脚手架自学习）使单一LLM搜索智能体能够在任意预算约束下进行预算感知的搜索，在7个QA基准上超越基线，并泛化到训练范围之外的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习训练LLM搜索智能体的方法基于固定预算进行训练，无法在部署时约束条件变化（如不同预算范围）的情况下自适应调整搜索策略。这限制了智能体在实际场景中的灵活性和适用性。

Method: AnySearch包含两阶段训练：第一阶段通过显式预算状态注入和结构化的推理提示，在线性衰减预算下引导智能体学习预算的高效分配；第二阶段移除脚手架，让智能体在自适应采样的预算约束下自主学习，以匹配推理时的实际条件。优化目标使用复合奖励函数，将答案准确率与预算效率耦合，通过绝对和相对信号进行衡量，并采用自适应权重根据查询的准确率动态调整效率信号的重要性（高准确率查询放大效率信号，低准确率查询衰减效率信号）。

Result: 在7个通用及多跳QA基准上的大量实验表明，AnySearch在所有预算规模下均优于基线方法，能够泛化到训练范围之外的未见约束，并在不产生过多token开销的情况下实现更高的工具生产力。

Conclusion: AnySearch证明了通过巧妙的训练脚手架设计和课程强化学习，可以让单个LLM搜索策略在不同预算约束下自适应工作，实现了良好的准确率-效率平衡与泛化能力。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning has enabled LLM-based search agents to invoke external tools, existing methods train under fixed budgets and cannot adapt when constraints vary at deployment. We propose AnySearch, a framework that enables a single policy to perform budget-aware search under any budget constraint through a training scaffold and curriculum reinforcement learning. In the first phase, we train the agent with explicit budget state injection and structured reasoning prompts that guide efficient allocation under linearly decaying budgets. In the second phase, the scaffold is removed and the agent learns to operate autonomously under adaptively sampled budget constraints, matching inference conditions. Both phases are optimized with a composite reward that couples answer accuracy with budget efficiency through absolute and relative signals, where an adaptive weight amplifies the efficiency signal for high-accuracy queries and attenuates it for low-accuracy ones. Extensive experiments on seven general and multi-hop QA benchmarks show that our method outperforms baselines across all budget scales, generalizes to unseen constraints beyond the training range, and achieves superior tool productivity without excessive token overhead. Our code is available at https://github.com/xwsun01/AnySearch.

</details>


### [60] [AnalysisBank: An Expert Analysis Pattern Library for Financial Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00818)
*Yajing Yang,Yunshan Ma,Kelvin J. L. Koa,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AnalysisBank，一种基于"分析层面"而非"结构层面"的金融报告生成方法。它将专家报告蒸馏为可复用的"分析库"（每个分析包含数据信号、分析动作和专家原文片段），推理时匹配输入信号并检索相应动作来撰写报告。实验表明该方法在金融基准上将新颖、数据支撑的洞察比例提升1.7-3.7倍，且该方法超越了金融领域，适用于科学写作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文认为金融报告生成应当运作在"分析层面"而非"结构层面"——即应基于数据推导出的洞察来组织内容，而非围绕高层次主题或章节。现有基线方法过于强调结构而对数据信号的深层分析不足，导致生成的报告缺乏新颖且数据支撑的洞察。

Method: AnalysisBank包含两个阶段：(1) 蒸馏阶段：从550份专家报告中提取"分析"条目，每个分析包含三元组——数据信号、分析动作、以及对应的专家原文片段；(2) 推理阶段：将输入数据信号与库中条目匹配，检索到的动作被用于组合生成报告。统计分析揭示了47-52种信号类型和13种动作类型的重尾分布。

Result: 在两种金融基准上、使用四种LLM后端的实验表明：相比结构层面的基线方法，AnalysisBank将新颖且数据支撑的洞察比例提升了1.7-3.7倍。该方法还能迁移到科学写作领域，表明分析层面与结构层面的区分具有超越金融领域的普适性。

Conclusion: 分析层面驱动的报告生成框架能显著提升生成内容中数据支撑型原创洞察的比例，且该范式具有跨领域的可迁移性。代码和蒸馏的分析库已开源。

Abstract: We argue that financial report generation should operate at the analytical rather than structural level, composing content from data-derived insights rather than high-level topics or sections. To this end, we propose AnalysisBank, which distills expert reports into a reusable library of Analyses, each pairing a data signal, an analytical move, and the expert span it was derived from. At inference time, AnalysisBank matches input signals to library entries and applies the retrieved moves to compose the report. A study of Analyses distilled from 550 expert reports reveals a heavy-tailed distribution of 47-52 signal types spanning 13 move types. On two financial benchmarks across four LLM backbones, AnalysisBank increases the proportion of novel, data-grounded insights by 1.7-3.7x over structural-level baselines. Transfer to scientific writing suggests that the distinction generalizes beyond finance. Code and the distilled Analysis library are available at https://github.com/yajingyang/AnalysisBank.

</details>


### [61] [Reinforcement Learning Enhanced LLM Agents for Complex Vehicle Routing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00859)
*Yi Chen,Zikang Yu,Jiahai Wang,Jinbiao Chen,Jianpeng Zhou,Zizhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RLEA，一个将强化学习与LLM智能体相结合的多智能体框架，用于自动化建模复杂的车辆路径问题（VRP），在48个VRP变体上超越现有方法，成功率提升16.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂VRP变体的建模需要大量领域专业知识，限制了高级优化求解器的可及性。现有方法难以自动将VRP问题转化为求解器可接受的模型，亟需降低建模门槛。

Method: RLEA是一个多智能体框架，包含：(1)一个轻量级神经规划器（Planner），通过Soft Q-learning训练来高效编排LLM智能体的行动；(2)进化记忆模块，用于积累经验；(3)检索增强生成（RAG），使智能体在生成和优化程序时能利用外部求解器知识。

Result: 在48种不同VRP变体和多种求解器上评估，RLEA相比之前最先进方法成功率提升16.67%，同时显著减少了运行时错误。

Conclusion: 将强化学习与LLM推理相结合对自动化优化建模高度有效，为缺乏专业知识的用户提供了一种可用的复杂VRP建模方案。

Abstract: Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are fundamental combinatorial optimization problems with widespread applications in various scenarios. The advanced optimization solvers can effectively solve such problems. However, modeling complex VRP variants for solvers often requires substantial domain expertise, which limits the accessibility of advanced optimization technologies. In this paper, we propose Reinforcement Learning Enhanced LLMAgents(RLEA), a multi-agent framework designed to automate the modeling of complex VRPs. RLEA introduces a lightweight neural Planner trained with Soft Q-learning to efficiently orchestrate the actions of LLM-based agents. In addition, we equip the system with an evolutionary memory module and retrieval-augmented generation, enabling the agent to leverage both accumulated experience and external solver knowledge during program generation and refinement for solving VRPs. We evaluated 48 distinct VRP variants across various solvers. The experimental results demonstrate that RLEA outperforms the previous state-of-the-ar method, achieving a 16.67% higher success rate while significantly reducing runtime errors. These results validate that integrating reinforcement learning with LLM-based reasoning is highly effective for automated optimization modeling. The appendix is available at: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19134435.

</details>


### [62] [CARE: Contrastive Anchor-based Rubric Evolution for Large Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00892)
*Siyuan Li,Xinxin Song,Chen Ruinian,Jingjing Fan,Tingxiong Xiao,Yangen Hu,Ke Zeng,Jinli Suo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CARE（对比锚点驱动的规则演化）方法，通过前沿模型生成的锚点响应来指导基于规则的强化学习中的规则动态演化，解决静态规则被利用和现有动态方法面临的规则提取无方向、作弊检测不可靠、规则膨胀等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于规则的强化学习（RLVR）在开放式任务上表现不佳，而基于规则（rubric）的强化学习更适合开放式指令的后训练。然而静态规则会随策略演化被逐渐利用（hacked），现有动态规则方法存在规则提取无方向、作弊检测不可靠、规则数量无节制增长等问题。

Method: CARE在每个训练步骤将最高得分rollout与由前沿模型（GPT-4.1）基于提示和规则生成的锚点响应进行对比。通过对比学习机制驱动两个互补分支：(1) Adaptive分支被动修复奖励错误规范问题；(2) Chase分支主动将前沿模型的质量差距转化为更精细的规则。两者共同在高奖励区域维持判别精度。

Result: 在WildChecklist-9K数据集上使用Qwen2.5-7B-Base和Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct进行实验，CARE在Arena-Hard-2.0、InfoBench和FollowBench上达到SOTA性能，并且是唯一在300步训练中对GPT-4.1锚点响应的胜率持续提升的方法。在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Qwen3-8B上的额外实验表明CARE可跨模型族泛化。

Conclusion: CARE通过将规则演化锚定在高质量前沿模型响应上，有效解决了规则作弊问题，在高奖励区域维持判别精度，并实现了跨模型家族的泛化能力。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning decomposes open-ended instructions into prompt-specific, flexible rubrics, making it better suited than reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards for post-training LLMs on open-ended tasks. However, static rubrics are inevitably hacked as the policy evolves, and existing dynamic approaches introduce new problems: undirected rubric extraction, unreliable hack detection, and unbounded rubric proliferation. We propose $\textbf{CARE}$ ($\textbf{C}$ontrastive $\textbf{A}$nchor-based $\textbf{R}$ubric $\textbf{E}$volution), which grounds every rubric evolution step in a high-quality anchor response generated by a frontier model conditioned on the prompt and its rubrics. At each training step, CARE contrasts the highest-scoring rollout against the anchor, enabling two complementary mechanisms: an Adaptive branch that reactively repairs reward misspecification; and a Chase branch that proactively converts frontier-level quality gaps into sharper rubrics. Together, the two branches $\textbf{maintain discriminative accuracy in the high-reward region}$---the precise region where reward over-optimization mostly originates. Experiments on WildChecklist-9K with Qwen2.5-7B-Base and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct show that CARE achieves state-of-the-art performance on Arena-Hard-2.0, InfoBench, and FollowBench, and is the $\textbf{only}$ method whose win rate against GPT-4.1 anchor responses shows sustained improvement throughout 300 training steps; additional results on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Qwen3-8B further indicate that CARE generalizes across model families.

</details>


### [63] [RPCBench: A Benchmark for Proactive Premise Critique in LLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00918)
*Zhongru Chen,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RPCBench基准，用于评估大语言模型在推荐场景下检测、诊断和处理用户查询中有缺陷前提（faulty premises）的能力，覆盖5个推荐领域、10种前提失败类型，并通过细粒度评估框架对11个LLM进行系统评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被越来越多地用作交互式推荐助手，其评估不应仅限于生成合理推荐，还应测试其是否能识别有缺陷的推荐请求。现有推荐基准主要关注排序、生成或偏好满足，而现有错误检测基准通常不基于推荐特有的用户和候选证据。这一空白促使作者构建面向推荐前提批评（Recommender-Premise Critique）的评测基准。

Method: 作者构建RPCBench基准，包含五个推荐领域中基于证据的测试实例，覆盖十种前提失败类型；每个实例提供可见的推荐上下文和被损坏的用户查询。同时设计了一个细粒度评估框架，衡量四个维度：主动检测（proactive detection）、错误定位（error localization）、检测后处理策略（post-detection handling strategy）和证据忠实度（evidence faithfulness）。

Result: 对11个LLM的系统评测发现：主动检测是推荐前提批评中的主要瓶颈；模型在欠明确前提错误（underspecified-premise errors）上表现最差。此外，目标关键信息密度比冗余证据更重要；更长的推理并不会单调地提升批判质量——性能在中等的推理长度达到峰值，而过长的推理伴随过度思考惩罚（overthinking penalty）。

Conclusion: RPCBench填补了推荐场景下前提错误检测与处理评估的空白，揭示了当前LLM在主动检测和欠明确前提处理上的显著不足，并发现推理长度与批判质量之间存在非单调关系，为未来推荐助手的能力评估和模型改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as interactive recommender assistants. Their evaluation should therefore go beyond plausible item recommendation and test whether they can recognize flawed recommendation requests. Existing recommender benchmarks mainly assess ranking, generation, or preference satisfaction, while existing error-detection benchmarks are usually not grounded in recommendation-specific user and candidate evidence. To address this gap, we introduce RPCBench, a benchmark for evaluating Recommender-Premise Critique: the ability to detect, diagnose, and properly handle faulty premises in natural-language recommendation requests. RPCBench contains evidence-grounded test instances from five recommendation domains and covers ten types of premise failures. Each instance provides a visible recommendation context and a corrupted user query. We further design a fine-grained evaluation framework that measures proactive detection, error localization, post-detection handling strategy, and evidence faithfulness. Through a systematic evaluation of 11 LLMs, we find that proactive detection is the main bottleneck in Recommender-Premise Critique, and models perform worst on underspecified-premise errors. We also observe that target-critical information density matters more than redundant evidence, and that longer reasoning does not monotonically improve critique quality: performance peaks at intermediate reasoning length, while overly long reasoning is accompanied by an overthinking penalty. The code is available at https://github.com/ZhongruChen/RPCBench.

</details>


### [64] [Spawn Freely, Act Sparingly: Progressive Risk Vesting for Recursive LLM-Agent Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01035)
*Molly Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"渐进风险归属"(PRV)机制，用于管理递归LLM智能体在分支执行过程中跨越不可逆操作边界时的权限授予问题。该方法将轨迹级风险预算托管起来，在激活分支时逐步扣减，并证明了在自适应生成的树中即使分支结果相互依赖也能保持无时点危害界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归LLM智能体通过生成专门分支来拓宽搜索范围，但某些分支可能请求发送数据或部署代码等具有不可逆后果的操作。关键问题是：何时应授予分支执行权限？现有方法在沙盒生成分支与真正激活能力之间缺乏明确的风险管控机制，特别是在分支结果是自适应生成且可能相互依赖的情况下。

Method: 提出Progressive Risk Vesting (PRV)框架：将轨迹级风险预算以托管形式持有，随分支激活逐步扣减。核心要求是每个局部证书必须在完整激活前历史的条件下方可有效，包括用于选择请求的信息。在固定激活门控、分支收费和计算约束下，延迟归属保留了不可撤销生成费用下的所有可选策略。还建立了一个简化分支模型，定义权力再生数R_A来刻画轨迹危害的相变行为，以及有限类型占用模型推导风险和计算的影子价格。

Result: 证明了PRV的随时危害界（anytime harm bound）适用于自适应生成树。在分支模型中，轨迹危害随R_A跨越1发生相变：局部风险p趋近零时，亚临界时危害正比于p，临界时正比于√p，超临界时保持正下限。对嵌套扇出模式（边际价值递减），影子价格产生阈值规则。通过分支计算和分割样本实验验证了结果。

Conclusion: 分析提出了一个设计准则：在沙盒中广泛搜索，但递归授权要克制，每次授权都需明确的风险收费。同时强调这些合成研究并不能估计部署代理的真实安全性。

Abstract: Recursive LLM agents can broaden their search by spawning specialists. Some branches later request tools that send data or deploy code. When should a branch receive authority to act? We distinguish sandbox spawning, in which external controls prevent the specified harm, from capability activation, in which a selected branch crosses an irreversible-action boundary. Progressive Risk Vesting (PRV) holds a trajectory-level risk budget in escrow and debits it as branches are activated. We prove an anytime harm bound for adaptively generated trees. Branch outcomes may be dependent, but each local certificate needs to remain valid conditional on the full pre-activation history, including the information used to select the request. When activation gates, branch charges, and compute constraints are held fixed, delayed vesting preserves every policy available under irrevocable spawn charging. Marginal risk estimates can still fail after branch selection. In a stylized branching model, trajectory harm changes as the authority reproduction number $\mathcal{R}_A$ crosses one. As local risk $p$ approaches zero, trajectory harm is proportional to $p$ below criticality, proportional to $\sqrt{p}$ at criticality, and retains a positive floor above it. A finite-type occupancy model yields risk and compute shadow prices. For nested fanout modes with decreasing marginal value per unit risk, these prices produce a threshold rule. Branching calculations and a split-sample experiment illustrate the results. These synthetic studies do not estimate safety in deployed agents. The analysis suggests a design rule: search broadly in the sandbox and grant recursive authority sparingly, with an explicit risk charge.

</details>


### [65] [Data-Driven Persona-Conditioned Agents for A/B Test Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01038)
*Ziyad Benomar,Weronika Łajewska,Leonardo Perelli,Saab Mansour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于LLM智能体的仿真框架，利用数据驱动的用户画像（基于真实匿名行为数据）预测A/B测试结果。在40个A/B测试基准上，最佳配置在不同指标类型上达到0.75-0.90的方向准确率，为快速、低成本的实验预筛选提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A/B测试是评估产品变更的金标准，但每个实验都需要真实用户流量、工程资源和数周的测量时间，成本高昂且周期长。现有仿真方法依赖合成或基于规则的用户画像，无法忠实反映真实用户群体多样性，导致预测效果不佳。

Method: 提出一个仿真框架：1）利用LLM驱动的智能体作为用户代理，智能体条件于数据驱动的用户画像；2）画像基于真实匿名用户行为数据（活动模式、参与信号、推断人口统计信息）；3）将A/B测试仿真构建为结构化问答任务；4）系统研究四个维度：问题设计格式、画像数据来源与领域对齐的影响、单画像行为深度与群体多样性之间的权衡、高效群体子采样策略。

Result: 在覆盖两种指标类型的40个A/B测试基准上，最佳配置的方向准确率达到0.75-0.90（具体取决于测试指标类型），验证了数据驱动用户画像在A/B测试预测中的有效性。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的LLM用户画像能够有效预测A/B测试结果，是一种快速、低成本的实验预筛选可行方案，能够显著减少对真实流量和长周期的依赖。

Abstract: A/B testing is the gold standard for evaluating product changes, but each experiment requires real user traffic, engineering effort, and weeks of measurement. We propose a simulation framework that predicts A/B test outcomes using LLM-powered agents conditioned on data-driven personas grounded in real user behavioral signals. Unlike prior work that relies on synthetic or rule-based personas, our agents are constructed from anonymized behavioral data-activity patterns, engagement signals, and inferred demographics-enabling more faithful population modeling. We frame A/B test simulation as a structured question task and systematically study (i) question design formats, (ii) the impact of persona data source and domain alignment, (iii) the trade-off between per-persona behavioral depth and population diversity, and (iv) efficient population subsampling. On a benchmark of 40 A/B tests spanning two metric types, our best configuration achieves 0.75-0.90 directional accuracy depending on the test metric, demonstrating that data-driven personas are a viable path toward fast, low-cost experiment pre-screening.

</details>


### [66] [WorldBench: Culturally Grounded Benchmark for Multilingual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01056)
*Leonardo Ranaldi,Sherrie Shen,Jushi Kai,Alexandra Birch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldBench，一个涵盖7种语言和8种文化的多语言基准测试，包含1,600个基于真实人格的日常任务，并引入Constrained Task Success (CTS)指标来同时评估任务完成度和环境状态保持。实验显示前沿模型仅达到49.2%的CTS，表明当前智能体在多语言、长时域任务中仍显脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体基准测试很少检验状态保持能力、跨语言性能以及在真实场景中的应用，导致评估结果无法充分反映智能体在复杂环境中的实际表现。

Method: 构建WorldBench基准：包含1,600个任务、7种语言和8种文化背景，任务经过语言和文化专家的反馈筛选与完善；引入Constrained Task Success (CTS)指标，将自然语言指令与测试环境结合，通过确定性和LLM-as-a-Judge评估方式综合衡量任务完成度、最小环境修改及其他补充指标。

Result: 前沿模型仅达到49.2%的CTS成绩，所有模型在任务正确性与环境保持之间均存在明显差距，尤其在长时域任务和状态保持约束下表现脆弱。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在多语言、真实场景化（persona-grounded）任务中仍显不足，特别是在长时域任务和环境状态保持方面，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Despite the growing use of LLM-powered agents to solve multi-step tasks in complex environments, existing benchmarks rarely test state preservation, performance across languages, and application to realistic, grounded scenarios. To address these concerns, we present WorldBench: a comprehensive, multilingual benchmark of genuine, persona-grounded everyday workflows, where agents can act in a sandbox via structured actions. WorldBench comprises 1,600 tasks across seven languages and eight cultures, filtered and refined through feedback from human annotators with language- and culture-specific expertise. For evaluation, we extend metrics from previous works and introduce Constrained Task Success (CTS), which combines natural language instructions and testbeds to score task completion, minimal modification, and other complementary metrics through deterministic and LLM-as-a-Judge evaluations. Our experiments show that frontier models reach only 49.2% CTS, with all models demonstrating large gaps between correctness and environment preservation. We thereby show that current agents remain brittle in multilingual, agentic scenarios, especially for long-horizon tasks and under state-preservation constraints

</details>


### [67] [Latent Recurrent Thoughts: Recurrent Refinement of Proposed Latents for Reasoning with Frozen LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01117)
*Zhaoliang Chen,Jie Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Latent Recurrent Thoughts (LRT)的方法，在模型连续表示空间中执行推理，而非传统的离散token推理。通过让一个小的循环推理器在隐空间中反复精化潜在思维状态，同时保持大型语言模型冻结不动，仅用于建模和解码序列，从而将计算深度与模型规模解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维推理在离散token空间中进行，存在三个核心缺陷：每一步都需要被提交为文本导致错误传播；推理链需要模仿样例来引导；陷入离散空间限制了推理的灵活性。在连续表示空间中推理可以规避这些约束，但尚未明确隐状态应如何计算，以及如何与冻结的大语言模型有效配合。

Method: LRT包含三个组件：1) 任务专用的提议器(proposer)生成基础潜在思维；2) 循环推理器(recurrent reasoner)通过有界的残差校正对潜在思维进行多步迭代精化；3) 冻结的大语言模型将最终精化的潜在状态解码为答案。关键设计包括保持LLM冻结以利用其已有的序列建模与解码能力，以及通过递归方式使潜在思维成为迭代处理的产物而非单次前向传播的结果。

Result: 在符号推理任务(Countdown-4, Sudoku，仅提供答案监督而无推理轨迹)和自然语言推理任务(HumanEval, MBPP, StrategyQA)上，LRT在相同解码器、提示词、数据和训练预算下显著优于先前的冻结解码器连续空间推理方法；同时在相同骨干网络上以远低于链式思维提示的推理计算量取得更优表现。

Conclusion: LRT证明了在连续表示空间中进行循环迭代推理的有效性，实现了计算深度与模型规模的解耦，并且能够在无推理轨迹监督的情况下通过答案监督学习到高质量的潜在推理状态。这一方法为推理范式提供了一种超越离散token空间的新路径。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning unfolds in discrete token space: each step is committed as text, errors propagate, and eliciting good traces presupposes traces to imitate. Reasoning instead in a model's continuous representation space - where intermediate states are vectors rather than words - sidesteps these constraints, but leaves open how those latent states should be computed. We approach this along two axes. First, we keep a large language model (LLM) frozen and use it for what it is already good at - modeling and decoding sequences - while a small auxiliary network supplies continuous latent thoughts as input. Second, we produce those latents by recurrence: a tiny recurrent reasoner refines them over many steps, decoupling the depth of computation from the size of the model, so that the latents are a product of iterative processing rather than a single forward pass. We instantiate this as Latent Recurrent Thoughts (LRT): a task-dedicated proposer supplies base latents, a recurrent reasoner refines them through bounded residual corrections, and the frozen LLM decodes the answer. On symbolic reasoning with answer supervision but no reasoning traces (Countdown-4, Sudoku) and on natural-language reasoning (HumanEval, MBPP, StrategyQA), LRT substantially outperforms prior frozen-decoder continuous-space reasoning methods under an identical decoder, prompt, data, and training budget, and outperforms non-thinking-mode chain-of-thought prompting on the same backbone at a small fraction of its inference compute.

</details>


### [68] [H2Table: Hierarchical Hypergraph-Enhanced Large Language Models for Complex Table Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01216)
*Jia Ling,Yangfan Wang,Chen Tang,Haoming Tan,Yang Yang,Yi Guan,Jingchi Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出H2Table框架，将复杂表格表示为层次嵌套超图，通过定制超图编码器在超边（表头）和节点（单元格）间进行消息传递，并利用可学习查询向量将结构嵌入桥接到LLM中。在HiTab数据集上，对嵌套深度为四的高复杂表格平均提升22.88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM处理表格推理时通常将表格线性化为序列，忽略了表格固有的二维和层次结构，导致在复杂层次表头上的推理能力受限。

Method: 1）将复杂表格表示为层次嵌套超图；2）设计定制超图编码器，在超边（表头）与节点（单元格）间进行消息传递以感知语义蕴含关系；3）引入一组可学习查询向量作为轻量桥梁，将编码器提取的结构化嵌入注入LLM。

Result: 在HiTab数据集上，对嵌套深度达到四的高复杂表格，相比SOTA基线平均提升22.88%。

Conclusion: H2Table通过层次超图建模有效解决了复杂层次嵌套表格的推理问题，验证了将结构信息显式建模并注入LLM的有效性。

Abstract: Tables are ubiquitous across diverse domains, yet reasoning over them remains a significant challenge for modern large language models (LLMs). Current approaches typically linearize tables into sequences, inherently overlooking their intrinsic two-dimensional and hierarchical structure. To address this, we propose H2Table (Hierarchical Hypergraph-Enhanced Table Reasoning), a novel framework that represents complex tables as hierarchical nested hypergraphs. To process this representation, we design a tailored hypergraph encoder to facilitate message passing between hyperedges (headers) and nodes (cells), thereby perceiving the semantic entailment relationships between them within complex tables. Furthermore, we introduce a set of learnable query vectors acting as a lightweight bridge to extract representative structural embeddings from the encoder into the LLM. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach effectively handles complex table question answering tasks with hierarchical nested headers. Notably, on the HiTab dataset, H2Table achieves an average improvement of 22.88% over state-of-the-art baselines on highly complex tables with a nesting depth of four. Our code is available at: https://github.com/lila120/h2table.

</details>


### [69] [Measuring the Behavioral Fidelity of Long-Horizon Human Activity Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01257)
*Yi Fei Cheng,Fan Yang,Iremsu Bas,Koichiro Niinuma,Narishige Abe,David Lindlbauer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个评估LLM行为模拟器在长时程真实世界活动中的行为保真度的框架，并通过43小时办公室活动的多摄像头数据集进行案例研究，发现统计先验虽最接近真实行为分布，但会过度碎片化例行程序并抑制个体内变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的人类行为模拟器被越来越多地用于政策制定、评估和训练，它们必须忠实地再现真实行为模式。先前研究虽然考察了调查问卷和对话中的行为保真度，但长时程的真实世界活动行为保真度尚未被深入探索。

Method: 提出一个评估框架，从多个时间粒度和分析层面考察长时程活动模拟中的行为保真度。作为案例研究，收集了43小时的真实办公室活动多摄像头数据集，并比较了三种基于轨迹的条件机制：人物描述（persona descriptors）、少样本示例（few-shot exemplars）以及统计转移和时间时段先验（statistical transition and time-of-day priors）。

Result: 行为保真度并非在所有指标上一致：统计先验使活动和序列分布最接近真实行为，但同时会过度碎片化例行程序并抑制个体内变异性。

Conclusion: 需要一个涵盖多种指标、时间粒度和分析层面的更全面的评估方法，以实现对LLM行为模拟器保真度的整体性评价。

Abstract: As LLM-based human simulators are increasingly used for policy, evaluation, and training, they must faithfully reproduce real behavioral patterns. While prior work has examined behavioral fidelity in survey responses and dialogue, longer-horizon real-world activity remains largely unexplored. We introduce a framework for evaluating behavioral fidelity in long-horizon activity simulations across temporal granularities and levels of analysis. As a case study, we collect a 43-hour multi-camera dataset of in-the-wild office activity and compare trace-derived conditioning mechanisms: persona descriptors, few-shot exemplars, and statistical transition and time-of-day priors. We find that behavioral fidelity is not uniform across metrics: statistical priors bring activity and sequence distributions closest to real behavior, yet over-fragment routines and suppress within-person variability. These findings motivate a more holistic evaluation that spans multiple metrics, temporal granularities, and levels of analysis.

</details>


### [70] [A Composable Evaluation System for Reproducible Omni-Modal Foundation Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01315)
*Hodong Lee,Sanghee Park,Dohoon Ryu,Jungwhan Kim,Junyeob Kim,Soyoon Kim,Geewook Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniEvaluator是一个全模态（文本、图像、视频、音频）基础模型评估工具，通过统一接口连接现有的推理引擎和评估库，集成四个推理后端、四个评估框架和超过一千个基准测试。其核心功能包括：完整配置记录的可复制实验 artifact、跨模型对比的共享仪表盘、支持GPU推理服务器共享的联邦模式，以及一个可在CPU上运行的内置验证器来确保规则评分在不同引擎和提示词下的稳定性。该系统已公开可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建全模态基础模型需要在文本、图像、视频和音频四种模态上进行评估。虽然每种模态已有优秀的评估工具包，但这些工具的推理引擎、提示词约定和指标实现互不兼容，导致实践者需要为每个工具链维护单独的环境，且难以跨工具比较结果。作者在自身模型开发中遇到此痛点，因此开发了OmniEvaluator来解决这一工程层面的碎片化问题。

Method: OmniEvaluator不是重新实现基准测试，而是在更高级别连接现有的推理引擎和精选评估库。系统提供：(1) 四种推理后端和四种评估框架的统一接口；(2) 超过一千个基准测试；(3) 每次运行记录为包含完整配置的artifact以实现精确复制；(4) 结果汇入共享仪表盘用于跨模型对比；(5) 联邦模式实现并发评估间共享GPU推理服务器；(6) 内置的可在CPU上运行的小型验证器，在各评估引擎和提示词不一致导致规则评分波动时保持分数稳定，匹配低成本商业LLM评估器的效果但无需其持续的API费用。

Result: 系统成功解决了跨模态评估工具链不兼容的问题，使得在同一接口下完成文本、图像、视频和音频四种模态的模型评估成为可能。内置验证器在配置不匹配导致规则评分波动的场景下能够保持评分稳定，且其性能可匹配低成本商业LLM评估器，同时避免了持续的API调用成本。系统、演示视频和仪表盘均已公开发布。

Conclusion: OmniEvaluator提供了一个整合多种模态评估工具链的统一平台，通过连接而非重新实现现有评估基础设施的方式，解决了全模态基础模型评估中的工程碎片化问题。其可复制性设计、联邦模式以及稳定的内置验证器使其既适用于研究也适用于实际模型开发场景。

Abstract: Building an omni-modal foundation model means evaluating it across text, image, video, and audio. Excellent evaluation toolkits exist for each modality, but their inference engines, prompt conventions, and metric implementations are mutually incompatible, so practitioners end up maintaining separate environments for every toolchain and still struggle to compare results across them. OmniEvaluator grew out of this need in our own model development: rather than reimplementing benchmarks, it connects existing inference engines and curated evaluation libraries at a higher level, exposing four inference backends, four evaluation frameworks, and over a thousand benchmarks through a single interface. Every run is recorded as an artifact capturing the full configuration for exact reproduction, and results flow into a shared dashboard for cross-model comparison. A federated mode shares GPU inference servers across concurrent evaluations, and a built-in verifier, small enough to run on CPU, keeps its score stable across engines and prompts where rule-based scoring fluctuates under configuration mismatch, matching cost-efficient commercial LLM judges without their recurring API cost. The system, demo video, and dashboard are publicly available. (https://github.com/naver-ai/omni-evaluator)

</details>


### [71] [LEAP: Likelihood Elicitation and Aggregation for LLM-based Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01337)
*Yufei Chen,Yiran Zhao,Xiaogang Xu,Qipeng Xie,Jiafei Wu,Zhe Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LEAP（似然引出与聚合的概率预测框架），通过逐条独立审视证据并引出似然参数，再借助显式先验和确定性概率模型将其聚合成后验分布，替代传统"将所有证据一次性读入并由LLM生成最终预测"的垄断式预测（Monolithic Prediction）范式，在预测、信息寻求与浏览任务的基准测试中取得了更好的预测与校准指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的预测系统虽然在搜索和工具使用上有所改进，但多数系统仍采用"将所有收集到的证据一并读入并由LLM一次性产出最终预测"的设计，作者称之为"垄断式预测"（Monolithic Prediction）。这种设计存在两个关键缺陷：其一，难以追踪每条证据对最终结果的具体影响，可解释性与可追溯性差；其二，跨竞争结果的预测不确定性会被折叠/掩盖，导致概率估计不准确。

Method: LEAP将证据使用阶段重新组织为三步：(1) 逐条独立检查每个证据项，引出描述其对预测目标影响的似然参数；(2) 设置显式先验分布；(3) 通过一个确定性的概率模型将这些似然与先验聚合为后验分布。该流程可支持连续型、单选和多选预测，并保持证据贡献的可复现性。作者构建了涵盖预测、信息寻求与浏览任务的基准，并在自主agent循环及多个agent CLI框架上进行评估。

Result: 在相同证据条件下，相较于垄断式预测，LEAP在多数预测指标与校准指标上均表现更好；在控制先验访问、推理预算与聚合策略的对比实验中，LEAP仍然保持更强的性能优势。

Conclusion: 将证据聚合从"依赖LLM隐式整体阅读"转向"显式逐条似然引出+确定性概率聚合"的结构化流程，可以更有效地利用证据、保留可复现的证据贡献，并提升多种预测任务上的准确性与校准度。

Abstract: LLM-based forecasting systems have improved on real-world tasks such as financial markets and sports outcomes, largely through stronger search and tool use. Many systems still ask an LLM to read all collected evidence together and produce the final forecast. We call this design Monolithic Prediction. It can obscure how individual evidence items affect the result and collapse uncertainty across competing outcomes. We propose LEAP (Likelihood Elicitation and Aggregation for Probabilistic forecasting), which reorganizes how collected evidence is used in the prediction stage. LEAP examines each evidence item separately and elicits likelihood parameters that describe its implications for the target. An explicit prior and a deterministic probabilistic model then combine these likelihoods into a posterior distribution. This procedure supports continuous, single-choice, and multi-choice forecasts while preserving reproducible evidence contributions. We build a benchmark covering forecasting, information-seeking, and browsing tasks, and evaluate LEAP on our own agent loop and several agent CLI frameworks. Given the same evidence, LEAP improves most prediction and calibration metrics across models and remains stronger under controlled comparisons of prior access, inference budget, and aggregation.

</details>


### [72] [EDGE: Error Dependency Graph-Guided Multi-Error Attribution in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01360)
*Jun Hou,Priya Pitre,Yi Fang,Xuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EDGE框架，通过构建错误依赖图并进行反事实回滚验证，利用图结构引导LLM实现多错误归因，在TRAIL和MAST基准上验证了依赖结构对智能体故障诊断的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体故障归因方法通常只识别单一责任主体、步骤或根因，但实际故障往往包含多个相互关联的错误。缺乏对错误之间依赖关系的显式建模，导致归因不完整。

Method: EDGE框架包含两个核心阶段：(1) 从观察到的错误事件构建错误依赖图；(2) 通过反事实回滚验证可靠因果子集。推理图引导两阶段LLM-as-judge检测器进行错误归因，干预验证后的子图为解释和修复分析提供更可靠的依据。

Result: 在TRAIL和MAST基准上的实验表明，EDGE在大多数评估模型和设置下改善了类别级多错误归因性能。结合Who&When式提示的实验显示，图结构在不同提示策略下均有助于提升归因效果。

Conclusion: 依赖结构是智能体故障诊断中超越孤立根因预测的有效诊断先验，为多错误归因提供了新的研究思路。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agent failures often contain multiple related errors rather than a single mistake. Existing attribution methods usually identify a responsible agent, step, or root cause, but do not explicitly model dependency between errors. We introduce EDGE, an Error Dependency Graph-guided multi-Error attribution framework. EDGE constructs an error dependency graph from observed error events and validates a reliable causal subset through counterfactual rollout. The inference graph guides a two-stage LLM-as-judge detector for error attribution, and the intervention-validated subgraph provides a more reliable basis for explanation and repair analysis. Experiments on TRAIL and MAST show that EDGE improves category-level multi-error attribution across most evaluated models and settings. Experiments with adapted Who&When-style prompts show that the graph helps across prompting strategies. These results suggest that dependency structure is a useful diagnostic prior for agent failures beyond isolated root-cause prediction.

</details>


### [73] [Parsing the Stream: A Live Trace Model for Long-Horizon Agents and Their Observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01466)
*Egor Pakhomov,Erik Nijkamp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"实时轨迹模型"（live trace model），将长时程Agent的追加式事件账本逐步折叠为带类型的运行状态，并编译成面向不同消费者（人类观察者与Agent自身）的视图。实验表明：对观察者，编译视图相比读取原始轨迹可减少约14-15x输入token、降低成本5-7x且准确率更高（0.85-0.87 vs 0.48）；对Agent，在120步顺序依赖任务中，维护运行统计量的机制在完整上下文提示失败之处成功（30/30 vs 8/30）。轨迹折叠的独特价值在于确定性可审计性和为观察者从同一状态提供服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程Agent的轨迹规模会超过两类消费者的承受能力：一是监控运行的人类观察者，二是自身上下文受限的Agent（轨迹必须被折回其有界上下文）。现有方法要么直接读取原始轨迹成本过高且精度低，要么无法同时服务两类消费者，缺乏一个统一的实时轨迹建模方案。

Method: 提出实时轨迹模型：将追加式事件账本增量折叠（incrementally fold）为带类型的运行状态（typed run state），再编译为面向各消费者的视图。对观察者侧，以LLM阅读器为代理评估；对Agent侧，在120步顺序依赖任务中对比完整上下文提示、自述板（scratchpad）与折叠机制的表现，并进行两臂分解实验。

Result: 观察者侧：编译视图相较于预算上限的单次原始轨迹读取，减少约14x和15x输入token（按阅读器），成本降低5-7x，准确率更高（0.85-0.87 vs 0.48）。Agent侧：维护任务运行统计量的逐步骤状态机制在120步任务中30/30成功，而完整上下文提示仅8/30成功（n=30）；提示级自述板达到与折叠相当的准确率但成本更低。折叠相对廉价替代方案的剩余价值在于确定性可审计性及从同一状态服务观察者。作者还从失败观察中归纳出十一条候选的轨迹折叠需求，并找出一个折序敏感任务族作为该方法的失效边界。

Conclusion: 实时轨迹折叠是一种有效服务长时程Agent两类消费者（人与Agent）的中间表示方案：在schema覆盖条件下，token与成本节省是可迁移的结果；折叠的本质价值不仅在于成本效率，更在于提供确定性的可审计状态并让观察者与Agent共享同一状态视图。论文发布代码、基准、可再生合成语料库及全部工作台轨迹。

Abstract: A long-horizon agent's trace outgrows both of its consumers: the human observer monitoring the run, and the agent itself, whose bounded context the trace must be folded back into. We present a live trace model, an append-only event ledger folded incrementally into typed run state and compiled into per-consumer views, and evaluate it for both consumers against deterministic ground truth. For the observer side, evaluated with an LLM reader as proxy, the compiled view answers monitoring questions using approximately 14x and 15x fewer input tokens (by reader) and at 5-7x lower cost than a budget-capped single-call reading of the raw trace, with higher accuracy (0.85-0.87 versus 0.48). Because the questions were co-designed with the view schema, we treat the token and cost reduction, conditional on schema coverage, as the transferable result. For the agent, on 120-link sequential-dependency tasks, mechanisms that maintain the task's running statistic in per-step state succeed where full-context prompting fails (30/30 versus 8/30 under a clean protocol, n=30, labeled descriptive owing to benchmark-system co-development); a prompt-level scratchpad matches the fold's accuracy at lower cost, and a two-arm decomposition attributes the fold's accuracy to its deterministic aggregate and its cost advantage to its compactness. The fold's remaining value over cheaper alternatives is deterministic auditability and serving the observer from the same state. We derive eleven candidate requirements for trace folding from observed failures and delimit them with an order-sensitive task family on which the fold ceases to help. Code, benchmarks, a regenerable synthetic corpus, and all workbench traces are released.

</details>


### [74] [Harness-of-Harness: Multi-Day Autonomous Software Development with Continual Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01481)
*Haoyang Yan,Min-le Su,Hangfan Zhang,Zhanhao Li,Chen Zhang,Shao Zhang,Yang Chen,Lei Bai,Shuyue Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM驱动的编码代理的自主软件开发问题，提出Harness-of-Harness (HoH)框架，通过迭代的规划-编码-测试循环，使编码代理在自主开发过程中持续改进软件。HoH在标准基准上平均相对提升52.25%，最大提升82.86%，并能自主开发出可玩的第一人称射击游戏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码代理通常只能将高层需求转化为代码，但在无人工干预的完整自主软件开发过程中，如何让系统持续改进软件质量而非仅做一次性生成，是一个尚未解决的挑战。

Method: HoH框架基于现有编码代理工具链（harness），将其执行组织成迭代的规划-编码-测试循环。其核心设计原则包括：(1) 平衡代码修复与能力增长；(2) 将开发范围划分为小而可验证的增量；(3) 将实现阶段的测试与独立评估分离；(4) 约束可验证的输出而非规定代理的工作流程。此外，HoH逐步暴露交付物、角色专用工具和技能，鼓励复用而非重建，并维护版本化项目历史。

Result: 在GameCraft-Bench、FrontierSWE和ProgramBench三个基准上，测试了三组harness-模型组合（Codex+GPT-5.5、OpenCode+DeepSeek-V4-Pro、Pi+MiniMax-M3），HoH均优于对应的独立harness，三次迭代后平均相对增益52.25%，最大增益82.86%。在一次超过70次迭代的多日部署中，HoH自主开发出一款包含连贯剧情、完整核心机制、可人类游玩、视觉精美且集成音频的第一人称射击游戏。

Conclusion: HoH证明了通过精心设计的迭代循环框架，LLM编码代理能够在无人工干预的情况下从高层需求逐步开发出完整、可玩且高质量的软件系统，其效果显著优于单次或简单迭代的独立编码工具链。

Abstract: This paper studies autonomous software development, in which LLM-based coding agents transform high-level requirements into complete, functional, and usable software systems without human intervention. We introduce Harness-of-Harness (HoH), a framework that enables coding agents to continually improve software during autonomous development. HoH operates on existing coding-agent harnesses, and organizes their executions into iterative planning-coding-testing loops. To sustain improvement across loops, HoH balances repair with capability growth, scopes development into small and verifiable increments, separates implementation-time testing from independent evaluation, and constrains verifiable outputs rather than prescribing agent workflows. It progressively exposes deliverables, role-specific tools, and skills, encourages reuse rather than recreation, and maintains versioned project histories. On GameCraft-Bench, FrontierSWE, and ProgramBench, three harness-model pairs (Codex with GPT-5.5, OpenCode with DeepSeek-V4-Pro, and Pi with MiniMax-M3), HoH consistently outperforms the corresponding standalone harnesses, achieving an average relative gain of 52.25 percent and a maximum gain of 82.86 percent after three iterations. In a multi-day deployment with more than 70 iterations, HoH autonomously develops a first-person-shooter game, featuring a coherent storyline, fully implemented core mechanics, human-playable experience, polished visuals and integrated audio. Github: https://github.com/Flesymeb/HarnessOfHarness Project Page: https://flesymeb.github.io/HarnessOfHarness/

</details>


### [75] [When Guardrails Look Effective: Construct Validity Failures in LLM Agent Commerce Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01519)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对基于语言模型代理的交互式市场模拟中政策评估的构造效度进行了审计。作者在可配置酒店交易的多轮买卖测试平台上发现，初始报告的市场护栏福利增益在控制协议差异后大幅缩水甚至翻转，且存在随机性不稳定与非单调激励问题。论文提出了一份"构造效度契约"，将激励效度、协议隔离、随机稳定性和福利核算区分开，在实质性政策主张前返回INVALID或INCONCLUSIVE判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式模拟中，语言模型代理的输出看起来具有经济意义（价格、利润、消费者剩余、福利），但可能并未实例化所声称的行为。这种"看起来像经济学但实际不是"的风险可能导致基于模拟评估的错误政策结论，因此需要系统性的审计方法。

Method: 在可配置酒店交易的多轮买卖测试床上进行受控审计实验：固定offer schema和买家选择程序后对比有/无护栏的配对效果；跨Qwen2.5不同规模模型及多代生成进行检验；评估生成残差对方差贡献；进行卖家激励非单调性检验；使用脚本化阳性对照验证测试平台效度；提出四维构造效度契约（激励效度、协议隔离、随机稳定性、福利核算）。

Result: 初始报告的三项福利增益（+87.4、+35.0、+28.8）在固定协议后变为+7.2、-13.9和+23.8；14B模型单次生成平均效应+229，三次生成后降至+37.6（置信区间横跨零）；生成残差解释了49.9%的变异；卖家激励呈非单调性；利润最大化卖家已可达到一阶最优福利，护栏主要起再分配和降低福利的作用。原估计在协议隔离下判定为INVALID，受控研究在激励效度和随机稳定性下仍为INCONCLUSIVE。

Conclusion: 结果表明，护栏并非无效，但其表面价值在模拟代理和协议通过四项构造效度检查之前是无法识别的。该工作为基于语言模型代理的经济模拟提供了严格的效度验证框架，有助于防止"看似经济实则不然"的虚假结论。

Abstract: Interactive simulations increasingly evaluate policies in markets populated by language-model agents. Their outputs can look economic---prices, profits, consumer surplus, and welfare---without instantiating the behavior named in the claim. We audit this risk in a multi-turn buyer--seller testbed for configurable hotel transactions. An initial implementation reported welfare gains from two marketplace guardrails of +87.4, +35.0, and +28.8 across a Qwen2.5 1.5B--14B ladder. It also gave guarded and unguarded agents different offer schemas and choice procedures. Holding the schema and buyer chooser fixed changes the paired contrasts to +7.2, -13.9, and +23.8. The four largest 14B single-generation effects averaged +229; after three generations per profile-condition, they averaged +37.6 (95% bootstrap interval [-34.2, 109.3]), while generation residuals account for 49.9% of variation in this post-hoc probe. A seller-incentive check is non-monotone: increasing profit pressure produces less profit than the default seller prompt. Scripted positive controls show why this matters. A profit-maximizing seller already attains first-best welfare, so guardrails mostly redistribute and reduce welfare; they create welfare only when the seller is explicitly programmed to force inefficient bundles. We contribute a construct-validity contract separating incentive validity, protocol isolation, stochastic stability, and welfare accounting, and returning INVALID or INCONCLUSIVE before substantive policy claims. In our case, the original estimate is INVALID under protocol isolation, while the controlled study remains INCONCLUSIVE under incentive validity and stochastic stability. The case does not show that guardrails are ineffective; it shows their apparent value is unidentified until the simulated agents and protocol pass these checks.

</details>


### [76] [EvoSCM: Scientific Belief Revision Through Causal Model Evolution and Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01526)
*Qing Zhao,Haowei Li,Weijian Deng,Pengxu Wei,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoSCM框架，为科学智能体配备显式结构因果模型（SCM）并通过进化式闭环发现流程使其随实验证据不断演化，从而提升科学发现与预测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM科学智能体通常以自由文本表达科学假设，导致其信念隐式化，难以被检验或修正。科学智能体不仅需要学会推理，还需要知道应该相信什么，因此需要显式且可演化的假设表示。

Method: EvoSCM维护一个竞争性的SCM假设群体，每个假设编码候选的因果解释，并运行闭环发现循环：(1)从累积证据中溯因潜在机制；(2)设计判别性干预；(3)做出可证伪的预测并通过实验测试；(4)将预测与观测之间的差异归纳提炼为修正规则，修订每个假设的因果结构与机制；(5)演绎性地验证修订后的群体，以指导下一轮。

Result: 在DiscoverPhysics基准（要求智能体通过实验揭示非标准物理世界的隐藏动力学）上，EvoSCM相较于基线方法持续提升了科学发现性能，获得更准确的解释与预测，同时更高效地利用实验交互。

Conclusion: EvoSCM表明将科学智能体的信念以显式结构因果模型形式表示并支持其随证据演化，能够显著提升科学发现能力，验证了显式可修正信念在科学推理智能体中的价值。

Abstract: Scientific agents must learn not only how to reason, but also what to believe. However, existing LLM agents typically express scientific hypotheses in free-form text, leaving their beliefs implicit and difficult to test or revise. We introduce EvoSCM, which equips scientific agents with explicit structural causal models that evolve as new experimental evidence is collected. EvoSCM maintains a population of competing SCM hypotheses, each encoding a candidate causal explanation of the environment, and evolves them through a closed discovery loop. In each round, the agent abduces latent mechanisms from accumulated evidence, designs discriminative interventions, and commits to falsifiable predictions that it tests through experimentation. Discrepancies between prediction and observation are inductively distilled into correction rules that revise the causal structures and mechanisms of each hypothesis, and the agent then deductively validates the revised population against accumulated evidence and structural consistency to guide the next round. We evaluate EvoSCM on DiscoverPhysics, a benchmark requiring agents to uncover the hidden dynamics of noncanonical physical worlds through experimentation. EvoSCM consistently improves scientific discovery over baselines, yielding more accurate explanations and predictions while making more effective use of experimental interactions.

</details>


### [77] [Can LLMs Discover Scientific Laws in Real and Parallel Worlds?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01552)
*Yiming Huang,Ziche Liu,Zhuohang Wu,Yiqian Wang,Junxia Cui,Xinkai Zou,Linjun Mao,Nan Huang,Naicheng Yu,Kaijie Zhu,Yue Ma,Kun Zhou,Letian Peng,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCILAWS-BENCH，一个基于已发表研究和真实科学数据的科学定律发现基准，包含118个问题、291个候选定律、约800万真实数据点，涵盖六个科学学科。通过SCILAWS-REAL（固定观测数据定律发现）和SCILAWS-PARALLEL（主动查询合成隐藏定律）双任务设计，揭示了预测拟合与科学有效性可能背离、记忆化影响模型生成或超越已发表公式、best-of-N研究存在选择瓶颈等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学定律发现评估存在两大缺陷：要么通过合成设置简化发现过程，要么复用LLM可能已经见过的已发布目标。这无法真正检验LLM能否自主发现科学定律，因此需要设计一个基于真实已发表研究与数据的基准来客观评估LLM的科学发现能力。

Method: 从381篇已发表论文中构建118个科学定律发现问题，覆盖6个学科、291个候选定律和约800万真实数据点。每个问题在两种互补设置中实例化：SCILAWS-REAL要求模型从固定真实观测中提出定律，评估留出集预测拟合度和源自源文献的科学有效性；SCILAWS-PARALLEL要求模型主动查询残差校准世界，恢复由已发表形式合成的隐藏定律。双任务设计在保留科学背景的同时，分别评估固定记录定律发现和主动恢复新合成隐藏定律的能力。

Result: 研究发现：预测拟合与科学有效性可能相互背离；记忆化影响模型是复现还是超越已发表的公式；best-of-N研究揭示了选择瓶颈的存在。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个论文支撑的基准平台和评估AI科学发现的新实证视角，为科学定律自动发现研究奠定了评估基础。

Abstract: Scientific equation discovery has long been central to scientific progress, proceeding through iterative cycles of hypothesis generation, observational testing, and refinement under scientific constraints. As LLM capabilities advance and their role in AI for Science expands, it remains an open problem whether they can genuinely discover scientific laws and how this ability should be evaluated. Existing evaluations, however, often either simplify discovery through synthetic settings or reuse published targets that may already be familiar to LLMs. We therefore introduce SCILAWS-BENCH, a benchmark for scientific law discovery built from published research and real scientific data. It comprises 118 problems drawn from 381 scientific papers, covering 291 candidate laws and roughly 8M real data points across six scientific disciplines. Each problem is instantiated in two complementary settings: (1) SCILAWS-REAL asks models to propose laws from fixed real observations and evaluates held-out predictive fit and scientific validity derived from the source literature, and (2) SCILAWS-PARALLEL asks models to actively query residual-calibrated worlds and recover synthesized hidden laws derived from published forms. This two-setting task design preserves each problem's scientific context while separately evaluating fixed-record law discovery and active recovery of a newly synthesized hidden law. We find that predictive fit can diverge from scientific validity, memorization shapes whether models reproduce or move beyond published formulas, and our best-of-N study reveals a selection bottleneck. Our work provides a paper-grounded benchmark and new empirical perspectives for evaluating AI for scientific discovery. Project page: https://yiyihum.github.io/SciLaws-Bench

</details>

### [78] [AutoXRD: Autonomous LLM Agents and Comprehensive Evaluation for Powder Diffraction Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00070)
*Yuetong Wu,Maojun Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoXRD，一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的自主智能体框架，用于粉末X射线衍射（XRD）分析，并引入XRDBench基准（含QA诊断测试和E2E端到端工作流测试）。在1340次模型-任务运行中，十个LLM平均得分仅57.8/100，最好的模型GPT-5.6 Sol得分81.1，消融实验验证了框架中所有六个组件的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粉末XRD分析对材料表征至关重要，但可靠的端到端自动化仍具挑战。XRD智能体需能够解读衍射证据、操作精修软件、以可辩护的顺序管理耦合参数，并区分数值改进与物理有效性——这些能力对现有自动化方案构成重大障碍，因此亟需一个系统化的自主框架和相应的评测基准。

Method: AutoXRD将粉末XRD分析组织为逐步精修流程，基于观测证据进行行动决策，并在接受结果前施加确定性的晶体学与物理检验。同时构建双轨基准XRDBench：XRDBench-QA含100个有界诊断任务以隔离科学推理与决策能力；XRDBench-E2E含34个可执行工作流，测试文件检视、晶体学软件执行、迭代精修、证据保留和报告写作等完整分析能力。作者使用10个LLM在1340个模型-任务运行上进行评测，并结合消融实验和轨迹分析验证设计。

Result: 模型平均得分57.8/100（QA为61.9，E2E降至53.7）。模型在精修历史评估和结果接受上表现最佳，但在精修行动选择、物相定量、指标化和Rietveld精修上明显薄弱。GPT-5.6 Sol以81.1分居总榜首位，GPT-5.6 Terra在E2E上取得最高的81.0分，GPT-5.6 Luna在分数-成本权衡上最优。消融实验显示六个AutoXRD组件均独立贡献性能提升。执行轨迹分析揭示耦合参数控制、定量推理、证据保留和工作流终止方面的反复失败。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在粉末XRD分析尤其在端到端任务上表现不足，AutoXRD框架及其组件设计能够显著提升性能，但仍有明显局限。研究指向需要更强科学约束、不确定性感知决策以及更高效规划的未来方向，以推动可靠的自主晶体学分析。

Abstract: Powder X-ray diffraction (XRD) is central to materials characterization, yet reliable end-to-end automation remains challenging. An XRD agent must interpret diffraction evidence, operate refinement software, manage coupled parameters in a defensible order, and distinguish numerical improvement from physical validity. In this paper, we propose AutoXRD, an autonomous large language model (LLM) agent framework that organizes powder-XRD analysis as stepwise refinement, grounds actions in observed evidence, and applies deterministic crystallographic and physical checks before accepting results. We further introduce XRDBench with two complementary tracks. XRDBench-QA contains 100 bounded diagnostic tasks that isolate scientific reasoning and decision-making, whereas XRDBench-E2E contains 34 executable workflows that test whether agents can compose these capabilities into complete analyses requiring file inspection, crystallographic-software execution, iterative refinement, evidence preservation, and reporting. We evaluate ten recent LLMs across 1,340 model--task runs. Models average only 57.8 out of 100, falling from 61.9 on XRDBench-QA to 53.7 on XRDBench-E2E. They perform best on refinement-history assessment and result acceptance, but remain substantially weaker on refinement-action selection, phase quantification, indexing, and Rietveld refinement. GPT-5.6 Sol achieves the highest overall score of 81.1, GPT-5.6 Terra the highest XRDBench-E2E point estimate of 81.0, and GPT-5.6 Luna the best score--cost trade-off. Ablations show that all six AutoXRD components consistently improve performance, supporting the framework design. Finally, execution-trace analysis reveals recurring failures in coupled-parameter control, quantitative reasoning, evidence preservation, and workflow termination, motivating stronger scientific constraints, uncertainty-aware decisions, and more efficient planning.

</details>


### [79] [Fermi-Level Metal-d Character of Group-6 Bis-Hexahapto Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00083)
*Mingguang Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究双六配位(eta6-eta6)过渡金属(Cr, Mo, W)嵌入双层石墨烯体系，发现该配位方式导致强共价键合与极小的电荷转移，使费米能级处态密度由金属d轨道主导（约73%）而非碳p轨道主导，缺乏超导石墨插层化合物所需的层间能带特性。所有体系均呈金属性但费米面载体不同，有序相相对体相金属亚稳但相对孤立原子稳定，且AA堆叠因金属存在而比Bernal堆叠更稳定（400-600 meV）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨插层化合物（如CaC6）的超导性依赖于电正性插层物质向碳衍生层间能带提供电荷。双六配位（bis-hexahapto）化学将过渡金属以共价方式结合到两层石墨烯上，该机制的实质是几乎不产生电荷转移，与插层石墨超导所需的电荷捐赠机制相悖。作者旨在探究这种配位化学是否仍能保留使石墨插层化合物具有超导性的层间带特征，从而判断此类体系能否支持超导电性。

Method: 以双层石墨烯中的第6族过渡金属（Cr, Mo, W）为研究对象，在每层间一个金属原子（C12M）的组成下，与离子型参考体系CaC6（体相）和同一晶胞的C12Ca/C12Li进行对比。通过原子掩蔽的层间区域分析费米能级附近谱权重，计算费米能级±0.15 eV范围内的投影态密度（区分碳p与金属d贡献），评估几何堆叠能量偏好（AA vs Bernal）以及有序相相对体相金属和孤立原子的热力学稳定性（每原子能量），并进行磁性分析。未计算电子-声子耦合。

Result: 离子型对照体系（C12Ca/C12Li）在原子掩蔽层间区域保留68%和45%的费米面谱权重，而第6族体系仅保留15%、22%和23%。尽管第6族层间具有比C12Li更多的绝对层间权重，但费米能级±0.15 eV内的投影态密度在离子型参考中以碳p为主，而三个第6族双层均以金属d为主（约73%）。所有体系均金属性，但费米面的载体不同。几何上，eta6-eta6配位需要AA堆叠，凡存在金属处将固有的Bernal堆叠偏好反转400-600 meV。有序相相对体相金属亚稳（+2.9-5.0 eV/原子），但相对孤立原子稳定（-1.6-4.1 eV/原子）；不稳定性排序（Cr最不稳定、W最稳定）与Cr > Mo > W反应活性一致。C12Cr为金属性，单钨原子携带0.65 μB磁矩。

Conclusion: 双六配位插层可提供强的层间键合和明确的AA堆叠序，但不具备与超导石墨插层化合物相关的层间能带特征。这使得此类体系的性质从根本上区别于传统的超导石墨插层化合物。

Abstract: Superconductivity in graphite intercalation compounds requires occupancy of a carbon-derived interlayer band filled by charge from an electropositive intercalant.
