We ask, for group-6 Cr, Mo and W in bilayer graphene, whether Fermi-level states retain the interstitial, carbon-p character of ionic references, with bulk CaC6 and same-cell C12Ca/C12Li controls. Electron-phonon coupling is not computed. At one metal per gallery (C12M), the ionic controls retain 68 and 45 per cent, respectively, of gallery spectral weight in atom-masked interstitial regions at EF; the group-6 systems retain 15, 22 and 23 per cent. The contrast is compositional: group-6 galleries carry more absolute interstitial weight than C12Li, but projected density of states within +/-0.15 eV of EF is carbon p dominated in the ionic references and metal d dominated (~73 per cent) in all three group-6 bilayers. Every system studied is metallic; they differ in what carries the Fermi surface. Geometrically, eta6-eta6 coordination requires AA stacking and reverses the intrinsic Bernal preference by 400-600 meV wherever metal is present. Ordered phases are metastable against bulk metal (+2.9-5.0 eV per atom) but bound against isolated atoms (-1.6-4.1 eV); instability ordering (Cr least, W most) matches Cr > Mo > W reactivity. C12Cr is metallic; tungsten alone carries 0.65 muB. Bis-hexahapto intercalation delivers strong interlayer bonding and a definite AA registry, but not the interlayer-band character associated with superconducting graphite intercalation compounds.

*Matt Grau,Colin Kirk,Greg Blume,John Hill,Sushil Poudel,Alimohammed Kachwala,Marcy Stutzman,Joseph Michael Grames,Sylvain Marsillac,Matt Poelker*

Abstract: We report electron spin polarization of 95.0 +/- 0.8 (stat) +/- 2.4 (sys)% from a strained GaAs/GaAsP superlattice photocathode grown by metalorganic chemical vapor deposition (MOCVD) and cooled to dry-ice temperature (195 K). We achieved this polarization with 97.8% circularly polarized excitation light and a quantum efficiency of 0.7% at the peak polarization wavelength. This measurement exceeds the values of previously reported GaAs-based photocathode polarizations, which have clustered near 92% for two decades. We vary the temperature of the cathode and measure the polarization and quantum-efficiency spectra at 295 K, 273 K, 195 K, and 77 K. The polarization rises from 91.2(1)% at 295 K to its maximum at 195 K, while the spectral peak shifts from 775 nm to 739 nm (78 meV) over the full temperature range, tracking the widening band gap. Abstract: Materials exhibiting strongly anisotropic lattice thermal conductivity are desirable for thermal-management applications, yet such behavior is commonly associated with layered or quasi-one-dimensional van der Waals crystals and highly anisotropic elastic properties. Here, we investigate lattice thermal transport in the hexagonal perovskites Cs$BX_3$ ($B=$ Mg, Cd; $X=$ Cl, Br, I) using first-principles calculations. At 300~K, the calculated in-plane and out-of-plane lattice thermal conductivities range from 0.13--0.83 and 0.34--6.26~Wm$^{-1}$K$^{-1}$, respectively, corresponding to anisotropy ratios of 2.6--7.5. This pronounced anisotropy is remarkable given the relatively modest elastic anisotropy, characterized by $C_{33}/C_{11}$ = 0.994--1.842. Our analysis reveals that medium-frequency optical phonons provide an efficient out-of-plane heat-transport channel, contrary to the conventional picture in which heat transport is dominated by acoustic phonons. These findings identify face-sharing octahedral frameworks as a promising platform for engineering strong thermal-conductivity anisotropy in mechanically near-isotropic, non--van der Waals crystals.

Abstract: Continuous, non-invasive monitoring of the arterial pulse is a clinical priority for cardiovascular disease, the leading cause of global mortality. Flexible piezoelectric electronic skins can transduce the 1-10 kPa pressure wave into a self-powered voltage, but the best-performing piezoceramics are lead-based, and their toxicity is incompatible with skin contact and with tightening RoHS/REACH regulation. Among lead-free alternatives, the BaTiO$3$-based solid solution BZT-BCT reaches $d{33} \approx 620$ pC/N near its tricritical morphotropic phase boundary, rivalling soft PZT while remaining biocompatible. Exploiting this in a wearable confronts a sensitivity-flexibility paradox and three computational walls: the combinatorial explosion of atomic configurations in a disordered solid solution, the band-gap error of affordable density-functional approximations, which corrupts leakage and insulation estimates, and the 0 K nature of standard calculations against a 310 K operating temperature. We review lead-free piezoelectrics, morphotropic-boundary physics and fabricated flexible devices, then argue that equivariant machine-learning interatomic potentials --- coupled to a tiered functional hierarchy and finite-temperature lattice dynamics --- can survey the full configurational ensemble at body temperature and close the gap to a clinically viable lead-free pulse sensor.

Conclusion: BCC NiW合金被确立为一种可扩展、热稳健的自旋流材料平台，适用于高能效SOT-MRAM器件的后段(BEOL)集成。

Abstract: The development of high-performance spin-orbit torque (SOT) magnetic memories is fundamentally constrained by a persistent trade-off between spin Hall efficiency, thermal structural stability, and perpendicular magnetic anisotropy in conventional heavy metals. Here, we overcome this limitation by engineering body-centered-cubic (BCC) Ni-doped W alloys as highly efficient and thermally robust spin-current sources. Ni$_{30}$W$_{70}$/CoFeB heterostructures achieve deterministic out-of-plane magnetization switching at an ultra-low critical current density of 1.78 MA/cm$^2$, nearly threefold lower than that of $β$-W, while maintaining a high anisotropy field of 8,500 Oe and a thermal stability factor of 57.9. The BCC Ni$_{30}$W$_{70}$ alloy preserves its structural integrity and the perpendicular magnetic anisotropy of the adjacent CoFeB layer after annealing at 450 $^\circ$C, demonstrating robustness under the stringent thermal processing conditions relevant to back-end-of-line integration. Harmonic Hall and ferromagnetic resonance measurements reveal a large spin Hall angle of -0.39 and a high interfacial spin transparency of 0.75, demonstrating efficient spin-current generation and interfacial transmission. First-principles calculations further reveal enhanced intrinsic spin Hall conductivity in W-rich BCC NiW alloys, associated with the Fermi level lying within a spin-orbit-coupling-induced band gap. These findings establish BCC NiW alloys as a scalable and thermally resilient material platform for energy-efficient SOT-MRAM.

### [84] [Phase Switchable Photocatalytic Water Splitting via a Paraelectric-Ferroelectric Transition in Zr2Ge2S6 Monolayer: A Comprehensive Theoretical Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00724)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光催化水分解（PWS）是解决全球能源危机和制取可再生清洁氢燃料的前沿技术。虽然大量二维材料已被提出作为潜在光催化剂，但由于性能调控挑战，有效调控光催化反应和提高能量转换效率的策略仍然有限。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，研究Zr2Ge2S6单层在顺电相与铁电相中的结构稳定性、力学性能、带隙、带边位置、光吸收性能，以及相变对氧化还原电位和光生载流子驱动力（OER/HER）的影响。

Result: Zr2Ge2S6单层具有优异的结构稳定性、良好的力学性能、合适的带隙、最优的带边位置和宽光谱光吸收。铁电相中氧化电位更高，促进OER；顺电相中还原电位更高，促进HER。太阳能到氢能转换效率从顺电相的7.71%提升至铁电相的15.31%。

Conclusion: 该理论工作不仅凸显了铁电极化在光催化水分解中的关键作用，还为通过铁电开关调控二维铁电材料的光催化性能提供了一种有效策略。

Abstract: Photocatalytic water splitting (PWS) is a promising technology for addressing the global energy crisis and producing renewable and clean hydrogen fuel. Although numerous 2D materials have recently been proposed as potential photocatalysts, effective strategies for regulating photocatalytic reactions and improving energy conversion efficiency remain limited due to performance regulation challenges. Here, using first-principles calculations, we demonstrate that the photocatalytic activity and energy conversion efficiency of a Zr2Ge2S6 monolayer can be effectively tuned through a paraelectric-ferroelectric phase transition. The Zr2Ge2S6 monolayer exhibits excellent structural stability, favorable mechanical properties, a suitable band gap, optimal band edge positions, and broad-spectrum light absorption. Moreover, the Zr2Ge2S6 monolayer exhibits a higher oxidation potential and a stronger driving force for photogenerated holes to promote oxygen evolution reaction (OER) in the ferroelectric phase. In contrast, the paraelectric phase provides photogenerated electrons with a greater reduction potential and driving force for hydrogen evolution reaction (HER). The solar-to-hydrogen conversion efficiency is also strongly influenced by the phase transition, increasing from 7.71% in the paraelectric phase to 15.31% in the ferroelectric phase because of the improved carrier utilization. Our theoretical investigation not only highlights the crucial role of ferroelectric polarization in photocatalytic water splitting but also provides an effective strategy for tuning the photocatalytic properties of 2D ferroelectric materials through ferroelectric switching.

</details>


### [85] [Agentic programs: an emerging form of scientific software in computational materials science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00795)

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新兴的"智能体程序"(agentic programs)概念，将确定性算法与基于大语言模型(LLM)的有界判断相结合，实现科学软件中从人工判断到完全自动化的转变。作者以DeMARS为例，展示了该概念在从实验测量的无序晶体结构构建原子模型方面的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算材料科学传统上将算法任务交给计算机，而将科学判断留给人类。作者认为，基于LLM的智能体(agent)工具的出现，使得一种新的科学软件形式成为可能——即能够在生产中完全委托(complete delegation)且结合算法与LLM判断的智能体程序，从而突破传统人机分工的局限。

Method: 作者提出"智能体程序"(agentic programs)的概念框架，其核心要素包括：确定性算法与有界LLM判断的结合(bounded LLM-based judgment)、任务特定的验证机制(task-specific verification)、情景式成熟过程(episodic maturation)以及生产环境中的完全委托。并以DeMARS为实例系统，展示了该框架在无序晶体结构原子建模任务中的具体实现。

Result: 介绍了DeMARS系统，能够从实验测量的无序晶体结构出发自动构建原子模型，验证了智能体程序这一概念材料科学中的可行性和实用性。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的智能体程序代表了计算材料科学软件范式的新方向，能够将有限的人类判断经验编码进自动化系统，实现从"人类判断+计算机算法"到"算法+LLM判断"的范式转移，大幅提升建模流程的自动化程度。

Abstract: Computational materials science has traditionally delegated algorithmic tasks to computers while leaving scientific judgments to humans. We argue that recent LLM-based agent harnesses enable an emerging form of scientific software, agentic programs, that combine deterministic algorithms with bounded LLM-based judgment, task-specific verification, episodic maturation, and complete delegation in production. We illustrate this concept with DeMARS, an agentic program for constructing atomistic models from experimentally measured disordered crystal structures.

</details>

*M. S. Fadeev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文采用DFT+G$_0$W$_0$+BSE方法并结合量子分子动力学（QMD）的离子温度效应，系统研究了LiF在环境压力及冲击压缩条件下的折射率色散与光学响应。结果表明G$_0$W$_0$准粒子带隙（14.25 eV）与BSE激子吸收峰（12.5 eV）均与实验高度吻合；沿主Hugoniot至140 GPa，计算折射率与冲击实验数据符合良好，优于此前第一性原理计算；110-120 GPa以上QMD显示离子动力学可能导致偏离Gladstone-Dale线性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiF既是重要的光学窗口材料，其冲击压缩下的折射率行为对惯性约束聚变等应用至关重要。然而此前第一性原理计算在描述其光学色散时精度不足，尤其是在激子效应、准粒子修正及离子热效应方面缺乏系统全面的理论处理。作者旨在通过高阶GW-BSE方法结合QMD，显著提升折射率预测精度，并厘清高压下偏离线性n-ρ关系的物理机制。

Method: 采用DFT+G$_0$W$_0$+BSE方法计算光学折射率，其中离子温度效应通过QMD引入晶格动力学。在环境压力下计算光子能量色散n(ω)和k(ω)；在冲击压缩（532 nm和1550 nm波长）下沿主Hugoniot计算n(ρ,T)，其中密度ρ与温度T随压缩耦合变化。通过准粒子带结构、轨道投影态密度连接光学响应与电子结构，并评估QMD采样、有限k网格和过渡峰展宽的不确定性。同时以HSE杂化泛函作为对照。

Result: 环境压力下G$_0$W$_0$准粒子带隙为14.25 eV（实验14.2 eV），BSE激子主导峰位于12.5 eV（实验12.6 eV）；HSE无法重现光学谱。沿主Hugoniot至140 GPa，1550 nm处n(ρ)与冲击数据高度一致，532 nm处吻合可接受，整体优于此前第一性原理结果。高于约110-120 GPa时，QMD预测n(ρ)相比冷曲线出现下降趋势，暗示离子动力学偏离线性Gladstone-Dale关系。压力下带隙增大，约50 GPa处转变为Γ→L间接带隙；~1400 GPa的探索性计算显示带隙约为24 eV，排除压力诱导金属化。

Conclusion: 该工作证明GW-BSE方法结合QMD是准确描述LiF光学色散及其压力-温度依赖性的高效理论框架，揭示了离子动力学对高压下折射率偏离线性规律的重要贡献，并提供了结构及弹性基准，为冲击实验和未来极端条件光学材料设计提供了可靠的理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate the refractive index $n$ of LiF using DFT+G$_0$W$_0$+BSE, with ionic-temperature effects included through QMD. We calculate photon-energy dispersions $n(ω)$ and $k(ω)$ at ambient pressure and $n(ρ,T)$ at 532 and 1550 nm under shock compression, where $ρ$ and $T$ vary together. Quasiparticle band structures at ambient and compressed conditions and the ambient-pressure orbital-projected density of states connect the optical response to the electronic structure. At ambient pressure, G$_0$W$_0$ yields a quasiparticle gap of 14.25 eV, close to the experimental 14.2 eV, while BSE reproduces the main excitonic feature at 12.5 eV, close to the observed 12.6 eV. Quasiparticle and excitonic effects are thus essential for accurate optical dispersion; the hybrid HSE functional does not reproduce the optical spectra. Along the principal Hugoniot up to 140 GPa, $n(ρ)$ agrees closely with shock data at 1550 nm and acceptably at 532 nm, and agrees better overall than earlier first-principles calculations. Above approximately 110-120 GPa, QMD produces a downturn relative to cold $n(ρ)$ curves, suggesting that ionic dynamics may contribute to deviations from the linear $n$-$ρ$ Gladstone-Dale relation. QMD-sampling and finite-k-grid uncertainties are estimated, and transition-peak broadening is assessed using a recently proposed criterion. The gap increases under pressure, with a transition to a $Γ\to\mathrm L$ indirect gap near 50 GPa. An exploratory calculation at approximately 1400 GPa finds a gap of about 24 eV, disfavoring gap-closure metallization. Structural and elastic benchmarks of the underlying models are also provided.

</details>


### [87] [Magnetic quantum defects in a uniaxial antiferromagnetic insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00801)
*Shangfei Wu,Laur Peedu,Zhihao Wang,Xuecong Wang,Xianghan Xu,Kai Du,Sang-Wook Cheong,Aleksei Boldin,Joosep Link,Ivo Heinmaa,Raivo Stern,Sai Mu,Urmas Nagel,Toomas Rõõm,Girsh Blumberg*

TL;DR: 本文通过偏振分辨拉曼光谱和太赫兹吸收光谱，在单轴反铁磁绝缘体Cr₂O₃中发现了三种由铬空位诱导的激发态，其中最低能量模式（64 cm⁻¹）在易轴方向外部磁场下（尤其在6T附近的spin-flop区域）会从64软化至27 cm⁻¹，经第一性原理计算确认其为铬空位态中的局域磁激发，表明Cr₂O₃中的磁性缺陷态具有量子应用的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点缺陷已被成功用于量子计算、量子通信和量子计量等量子技术中（分别作为量子比特、单光子发射器和纳米级传感器），但其进一步发展面临关键挑战——需要发现和探索合适的缺陷-宿主系统，使其满足量子应用的必要条件。本文旨在寻找新的缺陷-宿主系统，拓展点缺陷在量子技术中的应用范围。

Method: 实验上采用偏振分辨拉曼光谱（polarization-resolved Raman spectroscopy）和太赫兹吸收光谱（terahertz absorption spectroscopy）对单轴反铁磁绝缘体Cr₂O₃进行表征；理论计算上采用基于缺陷超胞的第一性原理计算（defect supercell first-principles calculations）来解析模式的物理起源。

Result: 在Cr₂O₃中发现三种由铬空位诱导的激发态，能量尺度为几十毫电子伏特且具有二重简并。最低能量模式位于64 cm⁻¹，尖锐且对外部磁场（沿易轴方向）敏感，尤其在约6T的spin-flop区域附近，该模式从64软化至27 cm⁻¹。第一性原理计算确认64 cm⁻¹模式为铬空位态中局域磁矩的局域磁激发。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了Cr₂O₃中的磁性缺陷态具有用于量子应用的潜力，为在反铁磁绝缘体中探索和利用磁性缺陷提供了新的平台和方向。

Abstract: Point defects have been successfully utilized in various quantum technologies, serving as quantum qubits for quantum computation, single-photon emitters for quantum communication, and nanoscale sensors for quantum metrology. However, their further development faces key challenges, particularly in discovering and exploring suitable defect-host systems that meet the necessary criteria for quantum applications. Here, using polarization-resolved Raman spectroscopy and terahertz absorption spectroscopy, we discover three distinct chromium-vacancy-induced excitations in the uniaxial antiferromagnetic insulator, Cr$_2$O$_3$. These vacancy-induced excitations have an energy scale of a few tens of millielectronvolts and are twofold degenerate, and the lowest one at 64 $cm^{-1}$ is sharp and sensitive to the external magnetic field along the easy-axis direction, particularly close to the spin-flop regime around 6T, where the mode softens from 64 to 27$cm^{-1}$. Based on the defect supercell first-principles calculations, we interpret the mode at 64 $cm^{-1}$ as a local magnetic excitation of the local moment within the chromium vacancy state. Our results establish that the magnetic defect states in Cr$_2$O$_3$ have potential for quantum applications.

</details>


### [88] [Fourier Neural Operators for Composition-Driven Crystal Structure Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00900)
*Zhijie Yu,Jingyu Li,Yang Huang,Jingrun Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于傅里叶神经算子(FNO)的晶体场求解器，结合条件变分自编码器和生成-求解耦合框架，可从指定化学式与晶格参数生成周期性的数密度和电子密度场，并通过重建与多级筛选实现新型晶体结构发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体材料发现对能源、电子和催化领域至关重要，但庞大的化学与结构空间使得穷举筛选不可行。现有的基于体素的方法受限于三维卷积神经网络的局部感受野和高维变分自编码器的后验坍缩问题，难以捕捉晶体场中的长程相关性和生成多样化结构。

Method: 方法包含三部分：(1) 基于FNO的晶体场求解器，在全局傅里叶模式上操作以捕获周期性晶体场的长程相关性；(2) 耦合的生成-求解框架，用条件VAE在低维基系数空间生成候选晶格参数，随后进行密度场预测和原子重建（峰检测、位置优化、权重优化）；(3) 多级筛选流程，包括体素级过滤、机器学习原子间势弛豫和第一性原理计算。

Result: 该框架在104种化学式上生成了新型结构，重建精度具有竞争力，展示了高生成多样性和结构有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将傅里叶神经算子扩展到周期性晶体场并与成分条件的晶格生成相耦合，该方法提供了一条从给定化学组成出发进行晶体结构发现的可扩展路径。

Abstract: Crystalline materials discovery is essential for energy, electronics, and catalysis, but the vast chemical and structural space makes exhaustive screening infeasible. Existing voxel-based methods are limited by the local receptive fields of three-dimensional convolutional neural networks and the posterior collapse of high-dimensional variational autoencoders. Here, we develop a Fourier Neural Operator (FNO)-based crystal-field solver that maps a prescribed chemical formula and lattice parameters to periodic number-density and electron-density fields. By operating on global Fourier modes, the solver captures long-range correlations in periodic crystal fields beyond conventional local convolutions. Building on this solver, we construct a coupled generation-solving framework in which a conditional variational autoencoder generates diverse candidate lattice parameters in a low-dimensional basis-coefficient space, followed by density-field prediction and atomic reconstruction through peak detection, position optimization, and weight optimization. The reconstructed structures are further screened using voxel-level filtering, machine-learning interatomic-potential relaxation, and first-principle calculations. The framework generates novel structures across 104 chemical formulas with competitive reconstruction accuracy, demonstrating high generative diversity and structural validity. By extending Fourier neural operators to periodic crystal fields and coupling them with composition-conditioned lattice generation, our approach provides a scalable route to crystal structure discovery from prescribed chemical compositions.

</details>


### [89] [Weisfeiler-Lehman subtree encoding for Bayesian optimization of atomic configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00953)
*Akira Kusaba,Tatoshi Yonemori,Tetsuji Kuboyama,Yoshihiro Kangawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出将Weisfeiler-Lehman（WL）子树核引入贝叶斯优化的原子构型搜索中。由于该核可表示为显式特征（局部拓扑模式的L2归一化直方图）的内积，其引入等价于引入相应特征编码，从而以普通描述符形式无缝嵌入现有BO框架。在立方BC₂N基态构型搜索基准测试中，WL编码在5轮独立实验中均几乎立即找到基态（平均108±5次评估），而one-hot基线需要280±122次评估。WL驱动的采样器先穷尽简并基态组，再按能量升序发现亚稳简并组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化在原子构型搜索中的效率高度依赖于原子构型的编码方式。传统编码（如one-hot）未能充分捕捉局部结构相似性，导致搜索效率低下。作者希望找到一种能有效衡量构型之间结构相似度、从而提升BO采样效率的编码方案。

Method: 将Weisfeiler-Lehman子树核引入贝叶斯优化驱动的构型搜索。该方法将原子构型视为元素标注图，通过统计共享局部结构模式（局部拓扑子结构）的数量来度量构型间相似度。关键在于该核可被分解为L2归一化的局部拓扑模式直方图的内积，因此只需引入相应显式特征编码，即可作为普通描述符无缝嵌入现有BO框架。

Result: 在立方BC₂N基态构型搜索基准中（基于通用机器学习原子间势评估），共享100个随机初始样本后，WL编码在5轮独立实验中均几乎立即收敛到基态（平均108±5次评估），而one-hot基线需要280±122次评估。WL驱动的采样器搜索过程表现出清晰的物理规律：先穷尽简并基态组，再按能量升序逐一发现亚稳简并组。

Conclusion: WL子树核通过捕捉局部拓扑结构模式，为贝叶斯优化提供了一种高效的原子构型编码方式，显著降低了找到基态所需的评估次数，并能以能量升序系统性探索简并态空间，为基于BO的材料构型搜索提供了一种即插即用的高效描述符。

Abstract: The efficiency of Bayesian optimization (BO) of atomic configurations depends strongly on how configurations are encoded. We introduce the Weisfeiler-Lehman (WL) subtree kernel, which views configurations as element-labeled graphs and measures their similarity by how many local structural patterns they share, into Bayesian-optimization-based configuration search. Because this kernel is reproduced as the plain inner product of explicit features (L$^2$-normalized histograms of local topological patterns), introducing it reduces to introducing the corresponding features: the encoding enters existing BO frameworks as an ordinary descriptor. In a benchmark ground-state configuration search of cubic BC$_2$N evaluated with a universal machine-learning interatomic potential, the WL encoding reached the ground state almost immediately after a shared random initialization of 100 samples in every one of five independent rounds (108$\pm$5 evaluations on average), whereas the one-hot baseline required 280$\pm$122 evaluations; the WL-driven sampler first exhausted the degenerate ground-state group and then discovered the metastable degenerate groups from the bottom up, in order of increasing energy.

</details>


### [90] [Numerical study of the grain-growth-induced non-parabolic kinetics of a solid-state reaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01002)
*Ya. A. Nikiforov,S. A. Chizhik,N. I. Baklanova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个移动边界扩散模型，解释了固态扩散偶中反应层非抛物线生长的机理，揭示了由晶粒生长历史导致的三个不同动力学阶段，并解释了为何瞬时生长指数并非唯一动力学常数而有限时间拟合却能产生清晰的表现指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态扩散偶中反应层常表现出非抛物线生长，一般认为与晶粒生长导致的晶界扩散贡献减少有关，但微观组织演化与动力学之间的定量关系尚未被充分理解。作者旨在通过建模阐明这一耦合机制。

Method: 建立了一个移动边界扩散模型，将连续形成产物的局部晶粒生长历史、体扩散和晶界扩散均纳入考虑，并通过数值模拟研究反应层生长动力学。

Result: 数值模拟显示反应经历三个不同的动力学阶段：由晶界扩散主导的近似抛物线初始阶段、由有效扩散系数强空间变化引起的瞬态亚抛物线阶段、以及由体扩散主导的后续近似抛物线阶段。亚抛物线阶段的幅度和持续时间取决于晶界扩散与体扩散的相对大小以及晶粒生长动力学。瞬时生长指数连续演化而非唯一常数，但在有限时间区间内拟合层厚数据可获得明确的表现指数。

Conclusion: 瞬态扩散过程可以看似遵循单一幂律，这解释了实验中常见表观唯一次方生长指数的来源；研究强调了将组织演化与扩散动力学耦合建模的重要性。

Abstract: Non-parabolic growth of reaction layers is frequently observed in solid-state diffusion couples, but the relationship between microstructural evolution and the resulting kinetics remains incompletely understood. This effect is commonly attributed to grain growth, which can progressively reduce the contribution of fast grain-boundary diffusion, causing the effective diffusivity of a polycrystalline product layer to evolve during reaction. This work develops a moving-boundary diffusion model to describe product-layer growth while accounting for the local grain-growth history of the continuously formed product, as well as both bulk and grain-boundary diffusion. Numerical simulations show that the reaction can pass through three distinct kinetic regimes: an initial approximately parabolic regime dominated by grain-boundary diffusion, a transient sub-parabolic regime associated with strong spatial variation of the effective diffusivity, and a subsequent approximately parabolic regime dominated by bulk diffusion. The magnitude and duration of the sub-parabolic regime depend on the relative grain-boundary and bulk diffusivities and on the kinetics of grain growth. The instantaneous growth exponent therefore evolves continuously and does not represent a unique kinetic constant. Nevertheless, fitting simulated layer-thickness data over finite experimental time intervals produces well-defined apparent exponents, demonstrating how a transient process can appear to obey a single power law.

</details>


### [91] [First-Principles Electronic Structure Calculation of Crystals in Laboratory Magnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01053)
*Sichao Wang,Chengye Lü,Xingao Gong,Yingwei Chen,Hongjun Xiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文首次构建了基于规范不变高斯型原子轨道的磁Bloch基组，用于在实验室尺度磁场下对晶体进行第一性原理计算。该方法通过线性组合包含磁场相位因子的GIAO轨道来满足磁平移对称性，所需基函数远少于实空间或平面波表象，同时保留原子中心基组的稀疏性，大幅降低计算成本。通过在石墨烯中从头复现朗道能级谱验证了框架的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部磁场能从根本上重塑晶体的电子结构，支撑量子霍尔物理、朗道能级谱及场致拓扑相。然而，在实验室尺度的磁场下进行第一性原理处理受到磁通量子化的阻碍——磁单胞面积与外加场成反比，包含大量化学单胞，使得实空间和平面波计算代价过高。因此需要一种在有限磁场上高效可行的第一性原理方法。

Method: 构建由规范不变高斯型原子轨道（GIAO）线性组合组成的磁Bloch基组。该基组将磁平移对称性要求的磁场相位因子纳入基函数中，从而不需要显式构造巨大的磁超胞。由于磁Bloch框架需要远少于实空间或平面波磁超胞表示的基函数，且保留了原子中心基组的稀疏性，大幅降低了计算成本。

Result: 通过从第一性原理复现石墨烯的朗道能级谱，验证了该框架的正确性与有效性。结果表明该方法能够在实验可及的磁场条件下对晶体材料进行模拟。

Conclusion: 该磁Bloch基组方法为在实验可及磁场下模拟晶体材料提供了实用途径，克服了传统方法中磁通量子化造成的巨大计算瓶颈。

Abstract: External magnetic fields can qualitatively reshape the electronic structure of crystals, underpinning quantum Hall physics, Landau-level spectra and field-induced topological phases. Their first-principles treatment at laboratory-scale fields is, however, hindered by magnetic-flux quantization, which requires magnetic unit cells with areas inversely proportional to the applied field. Such cells contain a large number of chemical unit cells, rendering real-space and plane-wave calculations prohibitively expensive. Here we, for the first time, construct a magnetic Bloch basis built from linear combinations of gauge-including Gaussian-type atomic orbitals, which incorporate the magnetic-field phase factors required by magnetic translation symmetry. The framework requires far fewer basis functions than real-space or plane-wave representations of the same magnetic supercell and retains the sparsity of an atom-centred basis, together substantially reducing computational cost. We validate the framework by reproducing Landau-level spectrum of graphene from first principles. This approach provides a practical route to simulations of crystalline materials under experimentally accessible magnetic fields.

</details>


### [92] [Exchange striction determines how fast antiferromagnetic insulators demagnetize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01069)
*Aleksandr Buzdakov,Ravi Kaushik,Nikolai Khokhlov,Sergey Artyukhin,Alexey Kimel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过实验和理论计算揭示了反铁磁绝缘体中超快退磁速率由交换伸缩（exchange striction）决定，表明交换耦合对离子位移的导数可作为预测材料退磁速度的可计算参数，为筛选热辅助反铁磁存储器候选材料提供了实用途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁材料在太赫兹自旋动力学和对杂散场的免疫方面具有独特优势，但其超快退磁时间在不同化合物间从皮秒跨越到纳秒，且缺乏可解释或预测这一差异的材料参数，限制了材料筛选和器件设计。

Method: 采用时间分辨二次谐波产生（TRSHG）实验测量Cr2O3和FeBO3的超快退磁动力学；结合第一性原理计算分析交换伸缩机制，量化交换耦合对原子位移的敏感性；并通过自旋-晶格模拟以从头算参数再现实验测得的退磁速率比。

Result: Cr2O3在晶格被驱动至尼尔温度以上后在2 ps内退磁，比结构相似的FeBO3快两个数量级。第一性原理计算表明，Cr2O3中较短的Cr-Cr接触使交换耦合对原子位移的敏感性提高约十倍，并拓宽了声子衰变为磁振子对的相空间。自旋-晶格模拟成功再现了实验测得的速率比的数量级。

Conclusion: 交换耦合对离子位移的导数可作为预测绝缘反铁磁体退磁速度的可计算参数，为理解反铁磁体的超快调控提供了新见解，并为在合成前筛选热辅助反铁磁存储器候选材料提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Antiferromagnets combine terahertz spin dynamics with insensitivity to stray fields, and how quickly their order can be manipulated sets the speed limit on device operation. Femtosecond optical pulses demagnetize antiferromagnetic insulators on timescales that span picoseconds to nanoseconds across compounds, and no material parameter is known that accounts for the spread or predicts where a new compound will fall. In a compensated antiferromagnet, no angular momentum needs to leave the spin system, so the rate is set by energy flow from the lattice into the spins. Time-resolved second-harmonic generation experiments show that Cr2O3 demagnetizes within 2 ps once the lattice is driven above the Neel temperature, two orders of magnitude faster than the structurally similar FeBO3. First-principles calculations trace the disparity to exchange striction: short Cr-Cr contacts make the exchange coupling tenfold more sensitive to atomic displacements and widen the phase space for phonon decay into magnon pairs. Spin-lattice simulations with ab initio parameters reproduce the order of magnitude of the measured ratio. The derivative of the exchange coupling with respect to the ionic displacement thus emerges as a computable parameter that predicts how fast an insulating antiferromagnet can be demagnetized. The results advance our understanding of ultrafast control in insulating antiferromagnets, and suggest a practical pathway to screen candidate materials for thermally assisted antiferromagnetic memory before synthesis.

</details>

Method: 提出TFMat，一个文本条件化的流匹配（flow-matching）框架，使用结构化材料语言作为CrystalFlow生成器的语义先验，将可读的材料设计意图通过文本条件注入到生成流程中。

Conclusion: 结构化文本可作为可检查的控制层，将人类可读的材料设计意图转化为候选晶体，供下游模拟和验证使用。

Abstract: Crystal generators can now propose periodic structures, but their control interfaces remain poorly matched to the mixed descriptors used in materials design. Text provides a compact way to combine composition, symmetry, prototype and property cues, yet it has not been clear whether such information can steer flow-based crystal generation. Here we introduce TFMat, a text-conditioned flow-matching framework that uses structured materials language as a semantic prior for a CrystalFlow generator. Across Perov-5, Carbon-24 and MP-20 crystal structure prediction benchmarks, TFMat improves one-candidate match rates over CrystalFlow and reaches a 92.04% MP-20 match rate with 20 candidates; in de novo generation, it improves element-count and density distribution alignment while retaining coarse property consistency in composition-selected outputs. These results position structured text as an inspectable control layer for translating human-readable materials intent into candidate crystals for downstream simulation and validation.

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用压力感知、电荷分辨的机器学习分子动力学模拟（经DFT验证），系统研究了堆叠压力大小与加载几何对Li||Li6PS5Cl界面相间层形成的影响。发现压力效应呈非单调性：1 kbar压缩加速PS4分解和Li2S类有序化，而10-100 kbar压缩则限制结构重排和长程结晶；单轴加载比等静压加载更强烈地促进界面反应；压力还以缺陷位置依赖的方式调控空洞闭合与死锂扩展。研究将压力确立为耦合相间化学、离子输运和缺陷演化的机械化学过程变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点仅将堆叠压力视为维持固-固物理接触的手段，忽略了压力对反应性固-固界面化学的调控作用。在全固态锂金属电池中，界面的化学演化（如相间层形成）对性能至关重要，而压力大小与加载几何如何影响界面化学机制尚不清楚。

Method: 采用压力感知、电荷分辨的机器学习分子动力学（machine-learning molecular dynamics, MLMD）模拟，并以密度泛函理论（DFT）验证其准确性。通过系统调控压力大小（1 kbar至100 kbar）和加载几何（单轴vs等静压），模拟Li||Li6PS5Cl界面演化，并结合电荷分辨动力学追踪早期相间环境中硫中心、富锂配位结构与后续Li2S类有序化的关联。

Result: 压力对界面反应的影响呈非单调性：低压缩（1 kbar）促进PS4分解及Li2S类有序结构形成；高压（10-100 kbar）抑制结构重排和长程结晶。电荷分辨动力学识别出与Li2S类有序化相关的硫中心、富锂早期相间环境。单轴加载在相同标称压力下比等静压加载加速界面反应。压力对空洞闭合和死锂扩展的影响具有缺陷位置依赖性。

Conclusion: 施加的压力不仅仅是维持接触的手段，而是可作为机械化学过程变量，同时调节相间界面化学、离子输运与缺陷演化。该工作为从机理层面理解和调控硫化物固态电池中的压力效应提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Stack pressure is commonly treated as a means of maintaining physical contact in all-solid-state lithium-metal batteries, but it can also alter the chemistry of reactive solid-solid interfaces. Here, using pressure-aware, charge-resolved machine-learning molecular dynamics validated against DFT, we determine how pressure magnitude and loading geometry regulate interphase formation at Li||Li6PS5Cl interfaces. The response is nonmonotonic: compression at 1 kbar accelerates PS4 decomposition and Li2S-like ordering, whereas 10-100 kbar compression restricts structural rearrangement and long-range crystallization. Charge-resolved dynamics further identify sulfur-centered, lithium-rich early-interphase environments associated with subsequent Li2S-like ordering. Uniaxial loading accelerates interfacial reaction relative to isostatic loading at the same nominal pressure. Pressure also changes void closure and dead-lithium spreading in a defect-location-dependent manner. These results establish applied pressure as a mechanochemical process variable coupling interphase chemistry, ion transport and defect evolution, providing a mechanistic framework for interpreting pressure effects in sulfide solid-state batteries.

</details>


### [95] [Autonomous discovery of new structure-plausibility laws for explainable and rapid crystal diagnosis and screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01209)
*Zhilong Song,Lixue Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于主动证伪机制发现的无机结构合理性规则（PRIS），包含五条物理化学定律，可有效筛选DFT计算前的候选晶体结构，相比传统距离截断和Pauling规则具有显著更高的检测准确率，并能解释合成难易度与结构损坏检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体生成器和工具智能体能比DFT能量/声子计算或实验更快地提出候选结构，因此决定哪些候选值得昂贵的评估成为瓶颈。然而大多数筛选仅检测原子重叠，未能提供化学层面上的失败原因，亟需一套可解释的、物理化学合理的筛选机制。

Method: 自主智能体生成、测试并主动证伪了两百万个候选定律（agents），最终筛选出八条"无机结构合理性规则"（PRIS）。这些规则编码五种机制：短程斥力、离子接触与堆积、静电平衡、键价守恒和晶体学位点复杂度。研究者将PRIS与Pauling规则、距离截断方法进行对比，并检验其在损坏结构检测、合成难易度筛选和逆设计中的表现。

Result: 实验结构满足PRIS定律集的比率为82-99%，而仅6.5%满足Pauling规则2-5；PRIS能检测87.9%的损坏晶体结构（距离截断仅1.6-3.2%）；PRIS派生合成分数（PSS）可解释地筛除83.7%的难合成结构同时保留80.7%实验结构；在逆设计中可减少67.3%的DFT验证队列，同时保留99.2%达到设计目标的候选。

Conclusion: PRIS将筛选从简单通过/失败判定提升到提供化学层面失败原因的高度，并证明了自主智能体可以通过主动证伪发现指导计算和实验的物理化学规律。PRIS还解释了GNoME中低对称稀有结构的富集现象，能揭示伪造晶体报告中错误元素分配隐藏在合理坐标背后的情况。

Abstract: Crystal generators and tool-using agents propose structures faster than density functional theory (DFT) energy and phonon calculations or experiments can assess them. Deciding which candidates merit expensive assessment is therefore the bottleneck, yet most screens test little beyond atomic overlap and give no chemical reason for failure. Here, our agents generate, test and actively refute two million candidate laws, leaving eight Plausibility Rules for Inorganic Structures (PRIS). These laws encode five mechanisms: short-range repulsion, ionic contact and packing, electrostatic balance, bond-valence conservation and crystallographic site complexity. Experimental structures satisfy our law sets at 82--99%, but satisfy Pauling's rules 2--5 together at only 6.5%. The strictest set detects 87.9% of damaged crystal structures, whereas distance cutoffs detect only 1.6--3.2%. PRIS plausibility is linearly correlated with synthesizability, so the PRIS-derived synthesis score (PSS) explainably screens 83.7% of hard-to-synthesize structures while retaining 80.7% of experimental structures. In a property-conditioned inverse-design run, PRIS and PSS can reduce the DFT validation queue by up to 67.3% and keep 99.2% of the candidates whose DFT-validated bulk moduli reach the design target. Beyond screening, PRIS explains why GNoME remains enriched in rare low-symmetry structures and reveals how wrong-element assignments in falsified crystal reports hide behind plausible coordinates. PRIS moves screening from a pass-or-fail verdict to a chemical reason for failure, showing that autonomous agents can discover, by active refutation, physicochemical laws that guide calculations and experiments.

</details>


### [96] [Accelerating dynamic simulations of photoexcited materials and their evolution by electron-informed machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01492)
*Yunzhe Jia,Fankai Xie,Yunfei Bai,Miao Liu,Cui Zhang,Sheng Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非绝热激发态机器学习分子动力学（EMLMD）框架，通过从高精度实时时间依赖密度泛函理论（rt-TDDFT）基准模拟中严格校准光激发后的非平衡电子信息（如电子温度），实现激发态势能面的精确重建。该框架天然包含激发态电子-声子耦合与光致声子非谐性，弥补了标准机器学习分子动力学的不足，在保持高精度的同时实现大规模模拟，成功解析了铋光致相变中相干声子与热声子的竞争以及硒光致非晶化中的微观动力学与宏观结构重排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光激发下非绝热耦合的电子-核动力学是理解光致功能现象微观机制和理性调控的基础，但直接的第一性原理模拟计算成本过高，而现有标准机器学习分子动力学方法无法准确描述激发态属性（缺失激发态电子-声子耦合与光致声子非谐性），因此需要一种兼顾精度与效率的新型计算框架。

Method: 以rt-TDDFT高精度基准模拟结果为依据，严格校准光激发后的非平衡电子信息（如电子温度），以此为基础构建激发态势能面；将激发态电子-声子耦合天然纳入机器学习分子动力学框架中，通过机器学习方法实现大规模非绝热激发态分子动力学模拟。

Result: 大规模EMLMD模拟成功解析了光激发材料中时间分辨与动量分辨的声子动力学，直接揭示了铋在光致相变中光生相干声子与热声子的竞争关系，同时解析了硒光致非晶化过程中难以捉摸的原子级微观动力学与整体结构重排过程。

Conclusion: EMLMD在高精度与高效率之间取得了良好平衡，为研究复杂激发态分子动力学中的关键挑战提供了通用且有效的计算范式，有望广泛应用于光致功能材料的研究。

Abstract: Nonadiabatic coupled electron-nuclear dynamics upon electronic excitation underpin the microscopic mechanism and rational modulation of diverse photoinduced functional phenomena in materials, yet their direct first-principles simulations remain computationally demanding. Here we develop a framework for nonadiabatic excited-state machine-learning molecular dynamics (EMLMD) simulations, where the nonequilibrium electronic information upon photoexcitation such as electron temperature is rigorously calibrated from high-precision real-time time-dependent density functional theory (rt-TDDFT) benchmark simulations, enabling accurate reconstruction of excited-state potential energy surfaces (PES). This framework natively incorporates the excited-state electron-phonon couplings and intrinsically captures photoinduced phonon anharmonicity, both of which are missing in standard machine learning molecular dynamics, thus delivering first-principles-level accuracy for excited-state atomic evolutions. Large-scale EMLMD simulations resolve time- and momentum-resolved phonon dynamics in photoexcited materials, directly uncovering the competition between photogenerated coherent phonons and thermal phonons during photoinduced phase transition of bismuth. It also simultaneously resolves elusive atomic-scale microscopic dynamics and global structural rearrangement for selenium photoamorphization. Balancing high accuracy and efficiency, EMLMD offers a versatile paradigm to tackle key challenges in the study of complex excited-state molecular dynamics.

</details>


### [97] [No source-free exchange-correlation magnetic fields in non-collinear spin DFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01533)
*Ester Livshits,Roi Baer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文证明非共线自旋密度泛函理论中的麦克斯韦方程∇·B_xc=0（即xc磁场无源条件）并非精确条件，它违反了全局自旋旋转对称性。作者通过构造源自由（SoF）泛函将该条件以最优方式强加于任意母泛函，并在Mn₂分子上对比SoF与局部共线（LoC）LSDA的结果。尽管SoF成功得到了实验观测的¹Σ⁺_g反铁磁态和正确符号的磁交换耦合，但破坏自旋旋转对称性带来了严重后果：刚体自旋旋转会使E_xc改变约1 eV，且弱均匀场感应的磁化垂直于场而非反平行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非共线自旋密度泛函理论中常用的麦克斯韦方程∇·B_xc=0被视为一个精确条件，但其有效性从未被严格检验。作者希望验证该条件的真伪，并评估在实践中强行施加该条件会带来多大误差。

Method: 作者首先从理论上证明∇·B_xc=0违反全局自旋旋转对称性，因而并非非共线自旋DFT的精确条件。随后构造了一种源自由（source-free, SoF）构造方法：对任意母xc泛函进行修改，使得Kohn-Sham过程产生的B_xc无散度、是可导泛函导数、对均匀电子气保持精确，并能施加局部共线泛函无法提供的局部力矩。最后在Mn₂分子上以LSDA为母泛函进行数值对比测试。

Result: 在Mn₂上，SoF-LSDA和LoC-LSDA都给出过短的键长和过大的键能，但在磁交换耦合和基态上存在本质分歧：SoF得到了实验观测的¹Σ⁺_g反铁磁态，耦合符号和大小大致正确；而LoC预测了高自旋¹¹Π_u铁磁态和错误的耦合符号。然而，破坏对称性的代价严重：刚体旋转磁化方向会使E_xc改变约1 eV（理论上不应有任何变化），且弱均匀磁场感应的磁化方向垂直于场方向（正确结果应为反平行）。

Conclusion: ∇·B_xc=0并非非共线自旋DFT的精确条件。虽然在Mn₂案例中SoF构造意外地给出了正确的磁态预测，但该条件系统性地破坏全局自旋旋转对称性，导致无法接受的物理后果（旋转不变性破坏和磁化方向错误）。因此该无源条件不应用于非共线自旋DFT框架之中。

Abstract: We show that the Maxwellian equation $\nabla\cdot\mathbf{B}_{\mathrm{xc}}=0$, where $\mathbf{B}_{\mathrm{xc}}$ is the exchange-correlation (xc) magnetic field, is not an exact condition of non-collinear spin density functional theory: it violates global spin-rotation symmetry, the operation which turns the electron spins. How much does this matter in practice? To find out we impose the condition anyway, in the best way we can: we modify any parent xc functional so that the Kohn-Sham procedure yields a divergence-free $\mathbf{B}_{\mathrm{xc}}$ that is a functional derivative, stays exact for the homogeneous gas, and exerts the local torques a locally collinear functional cannot. Any non-physical result from the calculation must be due to the spurious condition, not the implementation. On Mn$_2$ with LSDA as parent, the source-free (SoF) construction and locally collinear (LoC) LSDA both give too short a bond and too large a bond energy, but differ on the magnetic exchange coupling and on the state itself: SoF finds the experimentally observed $^1Σ^+_g$ antiferromagnet, with a coupling of the right sign and roughly the right size, while LoC finds a high-spin $^{11}Π_u$ ferromagnet and the wrong sign. Despite this seeming success, breaking the symmetry has serious consequences: a rigid spin rotation of the magnetization changes $E_{\mathrm{xc}}$ by about 1 eV (it should not change at all), and the magnetization induced by a weak uniform field points perpendicular to the field (it should be antiparallel to it).

</details>
